					kaviñar muruku cuntaram

13 jūṉ, 2024 aṉṟu vikkimūlattil iruntu pativiṟakkappaṭṭatu

patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēviyilli.

﻿muṉṉuri



putukkavitipaṟṟik kūṟappaṭum kuṟṟaccāṭṭukaḷ iraṇṭu:

(1) putukkaviti purivatilli.

(2) putukkavitiyil purintu koḷḷa eṉṉa irukkiṟatu?



口

paṇṭik kālattil kaviñaṉukku ellām terintirukka vēṇṭum; avaṉ cakalakalā vallavaṉ.

iḷaṅkō kaviñar maṭṭumallar; ici mēti; nāṭakāciriyar; vāṉanūl vallunar; navamaṇikaḷiṉ kuṇa vēṟupāṭukaḷi uṇarntavar.

mikkēl ēñcalō ōviyar; ciṟpi; kaviñar.

liyaṉārṭō ṭāviṉci ōviyar; ciṟpi ; kaṭṭiṭa mēti; poṟiñar; iyaṟpiyal aṟiñar; kaviñar.

aṟiviyal mētiyāṉa kalīliyō kaviñarum kūṭa.

ketē mākaviñar; icimēti; nāṭakāciriyar; araciyal aṟiñar. tamatu amiccar poṟuppiliruntu viṭuppu eṭuttuk koṇṭu pāricu nakaram ceṉṟu ōviyam payiṉṟār; tāvaraviyal (Botany) paṟṟiyum oru nūl eḻutiyirukkiṟār.

ṣēkspiyar ulakam aṟinta tellām aṟintavar. nam nāṭṭilum ‘kalviyil periyavaṉ kampaṉ’;

19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil aṟiviyal poruḷiyal, araciyal, uḷaviyal, tattuvaviyal ākiyavaṟṟil ēṟpaṭṭa vaḷarcciyiṉ kāraṇamāka innili māṟat toṭaṅkiyatu.

﻿ovvoru kaliyilum ciṟapput tuṟikaḷ (Specialization) tōṉṟikkiḷittaṉa. ōr iyaṟpiyalvātiyiṉ moḻi maṟṟōr iyaṟpiyalvātiyālum, oru tuṟiyic cārnta ciṟpi, ōviyar allatu ici mētiyiṉ nuṭpam, avvat tuṟiyic cārnta kaliñarkaḷālum maṭṭumē purintu koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ippātippu kavitiyilum taṉit taṉmi vāynta, ciṟappup pirivukaḷit tōṟṟuvittatu.

urinaṭiyiliruntu taṉṉi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvataṟkākak, kaviti ivvaḷavu kālamākattāṉ aṇintirunta ceḻumi, iruṇmi mayakkum mantiram ākiya aṇikalaṉkaḷi iḻakka vēṇṭi nēriṭṭatu.

camakāla makkaḷōṭu pēcuvataṟkākavum, araciyal uṇarvukaḷi veḷippaṭuttuvataṟkākavum, ṭiriṭaṉ, pōp, piraṉ, ṭeṉṉicaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷ kaviti moḻiyiṉ cila paṇpukaḷit tiyākam ceytaṉar. ṭaṉ , kāleriṭj, piḷēk, hāpkiṉs pōṉṟa kaviñarkaḷ taṅkaḷukkeṉṟu oru kuṟukalāṉa vaḻiyit tērnteṭuttuk koṇṭu, taṅkaḷ vācakarkaḷiṉ aḷavik kuṟukkik koṇṭaṉar.

āṉāl, kavitit tuṟiyil uṇmiyāṉa puraṭciyi uṇṭākkik kavitipputumi (Modernism in Poetry) yit tōṟṟuvittavaṉ pireñcu nāṭṭic cērnta patiṉāṟu vayatu iḷiñaṉ rempō. avaṉ kaviti eḻutiyatu mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷ maṭṭumē. am mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷil avaṉ eḻutiya kavitikaḷ airōppiyak kavitiyulakiyē tali kīḻākap puraṭṭiviṭṭaṉa. eṉṟālum, rempō eṉṉum utaya cūriyaṉukku viṭiveḷḷiyāka muṉṉāl niṉṟavar pireñcu makākavi pōtalēr eṉpatiyum kuṟippiṭṭāka vēṇṭum.

pōtalēriṉ ‘mānakarak koḷki’yi appaṭiyē ēṟṟuk koṇṭatōṭu, viṭalip paruvak kaṉavukaḷiyum uṇarvukaḷiyum mikattulliyamākat taṉ paṭippukaḷil mutaṉ mutalāka veḷippaṭuttukiṟāṉ rempō.

inta kōpakkāra iḷiñaṉ (angry young man) paḻamiyōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa camayam, araciyal, potu oḻukkam ākiyavaṟṟi maṟutalittatōṭu, kaviti moḻiyiyum etirttup puraṭci ceytāṉ. kavitic coṟkaḷiṉ iyalpāṉa poruḷiyē māṟṟiṉāṉ. uri naṭiyilēyē uyarnta kavitikaḷi eḻutiṉāṉ.

kaṭavuḷ matam ākiyavaṟṟil nampikki vikkāta rempō, “oru kaviñaṉ taṉṉiyē ñāṉiyākkik koṇṭu, taṉ āṉmāvit tēṭi cōtittuṇarntu atip paṇpaṭuttik koḷvataṉ mūlam ‘pāva vimōcaṉam’ peṟalām” eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṟāṉ.

﻿rempōviṉ tākkam pireñcuk kaviti mītum, maṟṟa airōppiyak kavitikaḷ mītum aḷaviṭamuṭiyāta aḷavu iruntatu. ēṟkeṉavē pireñcu nāṭṭil kuṟiyīṭṭiyakkam kāl koṇṭiruntatu, mallārmē, verlēṉ pōṉṟa kuṟiyīṭṭukkaviñarkaḷ paḻañ ciṟappiyac cimmācaṉattiliruntu kavitiyik kīḻē iṟakki, ataṉ vaṭivattil putiya cōtaṉi muyaṟcikaḷ mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar. uḷaviyal aṟiñarāṉa ḵprāyṭiṉ āyvukaḷ kaliyilakkiyat tuṟiyil puraṭcikaramāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvittaṉa ‘puṟa meymmiyiya’ (Surrealism) iyakkam tōṉṟiyatu.

paṇṭiya marapukaḷip puṟakkaṇittal, uḷḷatticcutantiramāka vittukkoḷḷutal, kaṉavukaḷil mūḻkiyiruttal, mikikkaṟpaṉikaḷil īṭupaṭutal, akakkāṭcikaḷiṉ tuṇiyāl puṟavulakip purintu koṇṭu vāḻviṉ aṭippaṭi uṇmikaḷi aṟitalyāvum, puṟameymmi iyakkattiṉ paṇpukaḷ ākum. ivviyakkam 1924-il āntar pireṭaṉ eṉpavarāl muṟiyākat tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭālum, itaṉ aṭippaṭik kūṟukaḷ ellām rempōviṉ paṭippukaḷil muḻumiyākap poruntiyirukkak kāṇalām. eṉavē puṟa meymmi iyakka muṉṉōṭi rempōtāṉ eṉpatil entavita aiyappāṭum illi.

inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa āṉmīkak kaviñaṉ eṉṟum, mikac ciṟanta taṉṉuṇarccik kaviñarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ eṉṟum kuṟippiṭappaṭupavaṉ jermaṉ nāṭṭic cērnta reyṉarmēriyā rilk. ivaṉ paḻamiyil nampikkiyaṟṟavaṉ. ivaṉuṭiya cintaṉi vaḷarcciyi, kali nuṇukkac cemmiyil īṭupaṭṭirunta kuṟiyīṭṭāḷarkaḷ, veṟittaṉamākac cūṉyattip piṉ toṭarntu ceṉṟa rempō ākiyōriṉ vaḷarccikku aṭutta vaḷarcciyākak koḷḷalām. taṉṉiyē aḻittukkoḷḷum ulakap perumpōrukku etirāṉa makkaḷuṇarcci peṟṟeṭutta kuḻantiyāka ivaṉik kuṟippiṭalām. ivaṉuṭiya kavitikaḷil iruṇmik kūṟu mika atikam.

“eṉ pāṭalkaḷ iṟukkamum curukkamum mikkavi. ivaṟṟi ‘aṟintu koḷḷutal’ eṉpativiṭa ‘uḷḷuṇarvāl uṇarntu koḷḷutalē’ paṭippōrāl iyalum” eṉṟu rilkkē ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. avaṉuṭiya uṇmiyāṉa ulakam kaṇṇukkut teriyāta meypporuḷ ulakamē (World of Spirit). avvulakiṉ uṇmiyi uṇarntu tuykka, poyyāṉa ipparup poruḷ ulakil, aṉpum maṉitat taṉmiyumē vaḻi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. im muyaṟciyil tēvatikaḷiyum tuṇikkaḻikkiṟāṉ.

itē ‘āṉmīkat tēṭal’ , pēkay, ṭi. es. eliyaṭ, ṭi. ec. lāraṉs, ṭilāṉ tāmas, rāparṭ lōval ākiyōriṉ paṭippukkaḷil vevvēṟu vaṭivam koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku nērmāṟākac camatarmak kaviñarkaḷ oru pūlōka ﻿corkkattit tam kaṟpaṉiyil paṭikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. mārkciyak kaviñarkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavar jermaṉ nāṭṭic cērnta perṭōlṭ prekṭ. rilktaṉimaṉitaṉiṉ āṉma taricaṉattip pāṭiyavar. prekṭ makkaṭcamutāyattiṉ ēkkaṅkaḷip piratipalitta cīrtiruttavāti, rilk cīmāṭṭikaḷiṉ maṭiyilamarntu āṉmāvip paṟṟic cintittavar; prekṭ teruvōrttilamarntu ēḻmiyip paṟṟic cintittavar. rilkkiṉ kaviti aṟivi mayakkum putirkkaviti (mystic poetry). prekṭiṉ kaviti aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyi āyvu ceyyum naṭappiyak kaviti (Functional poetry) itip putiya meymmiyiyam (New Realism) eṉṟum collalām.

taṟperumi, tattuvārttam, pirapañca rakaciyam, aṭimaṉak kaṟpaṉi eṉpavaṟṟip pāṭu poruḷākak koṇṭuvarum kavitikaṭku etirāṉatu naṭappiyakkaviti. nērppaṭum uṇmikaḷi uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭiyākat taramāṉa kavitikaḷil aṟivārnta cintaṉiyōṭu veḷippaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṟu naṭappiyaṟ kaviñarkaḷ virumpiṉar.

prekṭ kavitiyiṉ ellāc caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷiyum uṭitteṟintu viṭṭu eḻutiṉār; tamatu karuttukkaḷi makkaḷukku vaṉmiyākak kūṟum kaṇṭippāṉa āciriyarāka viḷaṅkiṉār. muṉṉōkkāḷar iyakkam (Futurist movement) ruciyāvilum, ittāliyilum irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil paraviyatu, pḷāk, ehrṉ, pōrk, māyakōsski ākiyōr muṉṉōkkiyatti ātaritta muṉṉōṭikkaviñarkaḷ. ittāli nāṭṭic cērnta ṭi’aṉṉaṉsiyō, māriṉeṭṭi ākiya iru kaviñarkaḷum ḵpāciliyattiṉ tattuvat tantikaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. muṉṉōkkiyamum, ḵpācisamum taṉimaṉita cutantiratti (individualism) muṟṟāka veṟuppavi.

viḷāṭimir māyakōvski ruciyappotuvuṭimik koḷkiyāl kavarappaṭṭu, ataṉ puraṭcik kuralāka olittavar. avar eḻutiya kaṭṭaṟṟa kaviti, vālṭ viṭmaṉ, appōliṉar, māriṉeṭṭi ākiyōrip piṉapaṟṟip puraṭciyiṉ tiṭīr vēkattōṭum, kuttūcik kiṇṭalōṭum eḻutappaṭṭavi: ālit toḻilāḷarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷil aṭikkaṭi pāṭappaṭṭu, ruciya makkaḷiṭiyil atika viḷamparam peṟṟavi.

inta nūṟṟāṇṭuk kaviñarkaḷuḷ, atikamāka ulaka ilakkiya vātikaḷiṉ kavaṉattik kavarntavarkaḷ iruvar; avarkaḷ ṭi. es. eliyaṭṭum, esrā pavuṇṭum.

ṭi.es. eliyaṭ kiṟittava camayattiṉpāl atika īṭupāṭu koṇṭa āṉmīkak kaviñar. vāḻum kālattilēyē peruṅ kaviñarākavum ilakkiya mētiyākavum matikkappaṭṭu elllārālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷap paṭṭavar, ivaratu paṭippiluḷḷa iruṇmiyiṉ kāraṇamāka aruvak kaviñar (invisible poet ﻿eṉṟu ilakkiya vātikaḷāl aḻikkappaṭupavar. āḻntakaṉṟa perumpulamiyum, marapilakkiyap payiṟciyum mikkavar. ivar paṭippāṉa ‘pāḻ nilam’ (Waste Land) oppaṟṟa kuṟiyīṭṭuk kāppiyamākavum, aṅkatamākavum karutappaṭṭu, ulakiṉ uyarnta paricāṉa nōpel pariciyum peṟṟatu, mutal ulakap pōrukkup piṉ airōppāvil ēṟpaṭṭa naṉmīka vaṟaṭciyiyum, ataṉ viḷivāka makkaṭ camutāyattil ēṟpaṭṭa cīrkuliviyum ‘pāḻnilam’ vivarikkiṟatu.

ṭi. es. eliyaṭṭiṉ camakālattavarum naṇparumāṉa esrā lūmis pavuṇṭ ciṟanta kaviñar; paṉmoḻi vallunar; palkali aṟiñar; cīrtiruttavāti. jappāṉiya hikkū kavitikaḷiṉ ciṟappi ulakaṟiyacceytavar. peruṅkaviñar yēṭs, eliyaṭ pōṉṟōriṉ kavitikaḷil tamatu kattarik kōli nīṭṭic cemmippaṭuttiyavar. yārum neruṅkuvataṟku añciya, piṭivātamāṉa muraṭṭu ilakkiyamēti. kavitittuṟiyil ‘paṭima iyakkattit’ tōṟṟuvittavar. ḵpācisatti ātarittup piracāram ceytavar; mucōliṉiyiṉ naṇpar. kaṇṭapaṭi maṉampōṉa pōkkil eḻutappaṭṭa putukkaviti ivarāl ceṟivum, iṟukkamum, cemmiyum nuṭpamum, vēlippāṭum mikkatāka māṟiyatu.

spāṉiṣ moḻikkaviñarkaḷāṉa lārkāvum, pāplō neruṭāvum kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷuṭiya paṭippukkaḷ pireñcu, āṅkilak kaviñarkaḷiṉ paṭippiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavi. mutal ulakappōr muṭintapiṟaku airōppiya nāṭukaḷiliruntu kiḷampiya muṉṉōkkiyam, ṭāṭāyiyam eṉṟa puraṭci alikaḷ resiṭeṉciyāvil irunta spāṉiya ilakkiya vātikaḷiṭattilum pātippukaḷi uṇṭākkiṉa. āṉāl lārkā enta ilakkiyak kaṭciyilum īṭupāṭu kāṭṭavilli. marapilum, putumiyilum kāṇappaṭṭa nalla amcaṅkaḷ yāvum, avaṉiyum aṟiyāmal lārkāviṉ paṭippukkaḷil iṭampeṟṟaṉa. lārkā putukkaviñaṉākavum, atēcamayattil marapōṭu kūṭiya kirāmiyappāṭal pāṭum kaviñaṉākavum (Folk Poet) viḷaṅkiyatu viyappiṟkuriyatu.

pāplō neruṭā cilināṭṭuk kaviñaṉ. prekṭippōl araciyalvāti; māyakōvskiyippōl potuvuṭimik kaṭciyiṉ tīvira uṟuppiṉaṉāka iruntatōṭu, avaṉip pōla pāṭṭāḷikaḷiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷil paṭikkum pēccuk kavitikaḷi (spoken poetry) eḻutiyavaṉ; kiḻakku nāṭukaḷilum, airōppāvilum cilināṭṭiṉ araciyal tūtarāka nīṇṭa kālam paṇiyāṟṟiyavaṉ; cilināṭṭu araciyalil mukkiya paṅku vakittavaṉ. araciyal veḷippāṭṭiyamum, naṭappiyamum ivaṉ kavitik koḷkikaḷ. tāntēyiyum milṭaṉiyum eppaṭit teyvīkak kōṭpāṭṭiṉiṉṟum pirittup pārkkamuṭiyātō, hyūkōviyum, viṭmaṉiyum eppaṭi makkaḷāṭcik kōṭpāṭṭiṉiṉṟum pirittup pārkka muṭiyātō, ﻿atupōl pāplō neruṭāvi araciyal, ēḻmi, nīti, camattuvam ākiya kōṭpāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum pirittup pārkka muṭiyātu, ataṉāltāṉ neruṭāvi nintupōṉa ulaka apalikaḷiṉ oṭṭu mottamāṉa āttirak kuralākavum, otukkappaṭṭa teṉṉamerikka ēḻi nāṭukaḷiṉ puraṭcic caṅkanātamākavum kēṭka muṭikiṟatu. potuvuṭimik kaṭciyic cārnta kaviñarkaḷuḷ mutaṉ mutalāka nōpel paricu peṟṟavaṉ neruṭā.

irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērnta āṅkilak kaviñarkaḷuḷ, eliyaṭṭukku aṭuttāṟpōlak kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ, ṭapiḷyū. ec. āṭaṉum, ṭilāṉtāmacum āvar. āṭaṉ tīvira mārkciyavātiyāka iruntu, iruppiyal tattuvattil nuḻintu iṟutiyil āṉmīkavātiyāka māṟiyavar. naṭappu ulakiliruntu vilakiyirunta mantirat taṉṉiyakkattiyum (magical automatism) kuṟiyīṭṭāḷarkaḷiṉ moḻip puraṭciyiyum, (linguístic revolution) uṅkiyalōṭu iṇikkum kaviti muyaṟciyil ṭilāṉ tāmas īṭupaṭṭaṉar.

“kaviñaṉ eṭuttiyampiyākavum āṟutal kūṟuvōṉākavum, tīrkkatariciyākavum iruppativiṭat taṉ conta aṉupavaṅkaḷiyē kavitiyāka veḷippaṭutta vēṇṭum; ataṟku varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattukku avaṉ tirumpa vēṇṭum. immuyaṟciyiṉ mūlam maṉitaṉukkuḷ kuṭiyirukkum mirukattiyum teyvattiyum aṟintu koḷḷuvatōṭu, varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa aṭimaṉattiṉ toṭarcciyiyum aṟintu kaliyip putuppikka muṭiyum”

eṉṟu tāmas kuṟippiṭukiṟār. itu mīṇṭum iyaṟkikkut tirumputal (return back to nature) eṉṉum kucōviṉ koḷkiyic cārntatu.

irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iyantiranākarikattāl cōrvuṟṟa rāparṭ krēvs eṉṟa kaviñar veṇ tēvati (White Goddess) eṉṟa nūlukku eḻutiya muṉṉuriyil kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu uḷḷam nintu eḻutukiṟār:

"kavitiyiṉ ceyalpāṭu eṉpatu kalit tēvatiyiṉ karuṇivēṭṭal, nam vēṇṭutalukkēṟpak kalit tēvati veḷippaṭuttum uṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷ nammip paravacappaṭuttiyum, naṭuṅkac ceytum aṉupavaṅkaḷāka māṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum kavitiyiṉ ceyalpāṭum payaṉum appaṭiyē uḷḷaṉa eṉṟālum, aṇuku muṟi māṟiyirukkiṟatu. paṇṭināḷil iyaṟkiyiṉ eccarikkiyi uṇarntu maṉitaṉ, tāṉ vāḻum uyiriṉattōṭu icintum, kuṭumpattil peṇṇiṉ viruppaṅkaḷi matittuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭum vāḻntāṉ. ippōtu anta iyaṟkiyiṉ eccarikkiyi matikkāmal tattuvam, aṟiviyal, toḻil ākiya tāṟumāṟāṉa

﻿āyvukaḷiṉāl vīṭṭit talikīḻākap puraṭṭik kuṭumpa vāḻkkiyic cīrkulittuk koṇṭāṉ.

“iṉṟiya nākarikattil kavitiyiṉ taliyāya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ matippiḻantu pōyiṉa. pāmpum ciṅkamum vallūṟum vaṭṭak kāṭcik koṭṭakikkuc ceṉṟuviṭṭaṉa. erutukaḷum, karaṭikaḷum, mīṉkaḷum kūṇṭukkuḷ aṭikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. vēkakkutirikaḷum vēṭṭi nāykaḷum pantaya araṅkiṟkukkoṇṭucellappaṭṭaṉa. tūya kāṭukaḷellām aḻikkappaṭṭu aṟuppu ālikku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. kātal nilavu, erintu taṇinta kōḷākiviṭṭatu. peṇkaḷ eṭupiṭikaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭaṉar. paṇam, uṇmiyit tavira ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum, uṇmik kaviñaṉit tavira ellāriyum vilikku vāṅkum āṟṟal peṟṟirukkiṟatu”

mēlē ituvari nāṉ kūṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷiliruntu, kālap pōkkil kavitikkali peṟṟa vaḷarcciyum, māṟutalum, avaṟṟiṉ viḷivākappeṟṟa taṉittaṉmikaḷum viḷaṅkum. ittaṉittaṉmikaḷiyum avaṟṟic cārntu tōṉṟiyuḷḷa kavitik koḷkikaḷiyum kaviti iyakkaṅkaḷiyum purintu koṇṭāltāṉ putukkavitiyip purintu koṇṭu cuvikka muṭiyum.

kaviti vaḷarntu koṇṭē irukkiṟatu. ikkaṇip poṟiyukattil kaviñaṉiṉ cintaṉikaḷ ceyaṟkik kōḷil ēṟi aṇṭattil pavaṉi varukiṉṟaṉa. putaṉil toṭaṅkavirukkum putu vāḻvip paṟṟik kaviñaṉ cintikkat toṭaṅki viṭṭāṉ. kaviti oru toṭar viḷivu.

aṭuttup ‘putuk kavitiyil purintu koḷḷa eṉṉa irukkiṟatu?’ eṉṟu kūṟuvōrukkum, cila viḷakkaṅkaḷik kūṟa virumpukiṟēṉ. marapuk kavitikaḷil ‘veḷḷippāṭṭu’ iruppatu pōl putuk kavitikaḷilum veḷḷippāṭṭu uṇṭu. iṉṟu tamiḻil veḷiyākum putuk kavitikaḷil nūṟṟukkut toṇṇūṟu, kavitikaḷē alla. avi alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa urinaṭiyum (decorated prose), tuṇukkukaḷumtāṉ. putuk kavitikkuriya paṇpukaḷōṭu veḷivarum kavitikaḷip paṭikkum pōtu, avaṟṟiliruntu nām purintu koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu ērāḷamāka irukkum.

nilavu-

vēlikkuḷ aṭikkappaṭṭatu;

eṉ kātalum maṭintatu.





ikkātal varikaḷiṉ avalaccuvi, nam uḷḷattil muḷḷākat tikkiṟatu. itu oru spāṉiya nāṭṭup pāṭal. itippaṭitta lārkā paravacappaṭṭu “nāṭṭup puṟa makkaḷ virumpippāṭum inta varikaḷil putintu kiṭakkum putir mika ﻿eḷimiyāṉatu; cuviyāṉatu; tūymiyāṉatu. ipputirc cuvi, pukaḻpeṟṟa mēṭṭarliṅk nāṭakattilum kiṭiyātu” eṉṟu viyantu pārāṭṭukiṟāṉ.

ici

ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mūccu

ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ pēccu

itayattiṉ veḷivaḷarcci





rilkkiṉ inta varikaḷippaṟṟi muṭivillāmal cintittuk koṇṭē irukkalām.

ruciyakkaviñar sveṭṭēvā oru kaviti nūli eḻuti muṭittavuṭaṉ kaviñar pāsṭar nākkiṟkuk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu oru kaṭitam eḻutukiṟāḷ:

“nāṉ mikavum kaḷittuviṭṭēṉ; āciris tuṇṭu tuṇṭāka veṭṭi noṟukki eṟiyappaṭṭatu pōl nāṉum uṇarkiṟēṉ ...camayattūtar tāmasiṉ viralkaḷ, puṇkaḷit toṭṭup pārttatu pōl nāṉum oru kavitikkum maṟṟoru kavitikkum iṭiyil tīṇṭippārkkiṟēṉ.”</poem>

itu urinaṭiyil eḻutappaṭṭa kaṭitam. itil kūṟappaṭṭa ceytikaḷip purintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumāṉāl, ekiptiyat toṉmamum, kiṟittuva viviliyamum paṭittirukka vēṇṭum.

esrā pavuṇṭiṉ kavitippūṭṭit tiṟavukōliṉ (Key book) tuṇiyillāmal tiṟakkamuṭiyātu. avar kavitikaḷip purintu koḷḷap palamoḻiyaṟivum, paṉmukappaṭṭa ilakkiya varalāṟṟup pulamiyum, kirēkka itikācat teḷivum peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum; cīṉa jappāṉiyak kavitip pōkkum, kaṉḵpūciyat tattuvamum, ḵpācicamum purintirukka vēṇṭum. pērakarāti (Encyclopedia) maṟṟum paṉmoḻic ciṟṟakarātikaḷiṉ tuṇiyum vēṇṭum. pavuṇṭiṉ camakāla naṇparkaḷ avarkaḷiṭam avar koṇṭirunta naṭpu, antaraṅkam yāvum terintirukka vēṇṭum. ivvaḷavum pōtātu; iciyaṟivum, ciṟpa ōviyak kaliyaṟivum vēṇṭum.”

putuk kavitiyil purintu koḷḷa

evvaḷavō irukkiṟatu.





kavitiyiṉ tōṟṟam paṟṟip paṇṭi nāḷ toṭṭup palvēṟu karuttukkaḷ palvēṟu kaviñarkaḷālum aṟiñarkaḷālum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kirēkka ñāṉi cākraṭṭīs.

“kaviñarkaḷ cāmiyāṭiyip pōṉṟavarkaḷ. cāmiyāṭi cila camayaṅkaḷil maruḷ vantu aruḷ vākkuk kūṟuvatu pōla, ivarkaḷum cila camayaṅkaḷil āvēcappaṭṭu kavitikaḷi veḷippaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ.”

eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār. ﻿kaviti āvēcam peṟuvataṟkākac cila kaviñarkaḷ matuviyum maṟṟa pōtip poruḷkaḷiyum nāṭuvatuṇṭu. pireñcuk kaviñar pōtalēr,

“kañcāp pōtiyiṉ tuvakkattil vaḻakkattiṟku māṟāṉa vaṇṇattiṉ ātikkam namakkup pulappaṭum, kaṇṇip paṟikkum oḷiyuṭampōṭu kāṭci tarum mōkiṉip peṇkaḷ, nīlavāṉiviṭat teḷivāṉa āḻamāṉa viḻikaḷāl nammi uṟṟup pārppārkaḷ. appōtu ēṟpaṭum. iṉampuriyāta kṣaṉa nēra maṉa nilikaḷil nam vāḻkkiyiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷum, teḷivu cuḻivukaḷum, aṟputaṅkaḷum nam kaṇ muṉ paḷicciṭum. nam kaṇṇukku mutaṉmutalil entap poruḷ teṉpaṭukiṟatō, atuvē nam aṟivi naṭattic cellum kuṟiyīṭāka amiyum. teḷinta nīr nilikaḷum, nilik kaṇṇāṭikaḷum, kaṉavulakatti namakkut tiṟantu viṭum; cōkamayamāṉa oru pēriṉ pattil nam uḷḷam āḻntu, elliyaṟṟa kāṭcikaḷil tiḷikkum”

eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

uḷḷam tīviramāka uṇarccivacappaṭum pōtu, kaviti tāṉāka ūṟṟeṭuttuk karipuraṇṭu varuvatāka āṅkilak kaviñar vōrṭs vort kuṟippiṭukiṟār. ikkarutti ottuk koṇṭatu pōl, pireñcuk kaviñar rempōvum kavitit tōṟṟam paṟṟik kīḻkkaṇṭa karutti veḷiyiṭukiṟār:

“kaviñaṉukkuk kaṭṭuppāṭu kūṭātu. kaviti kaviñaṉuḷḷattil tāṉākak karukkoṇṭu, kaṟpaṉi vaṭivam peṟṟup pakkuvamaṭintu poṅki varavēṇṭum. kaviñaṉ taṉatu uḷḷattil karuttukkaḷ malarntu varutalik kaṇṭu cuvittu ati appaṭiyē aṉumatikka vēṇṭum.”

potuvāka, rempōvukkuppiṉ tōṉṟiya puṟameymmiyiyalār ellārum kaviti paṟṟiya ik koḷkiyi ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. putitāka aṭimaṉak kōṭpāṭu ivarkaḷ karuttukkaḷōṭu cērkiṉṟatu.

potuvuṭimik koḷkiyāḷaṉum, muṉṉōkkiya vātiyumāṉa māyakōvski, kavitit tōṟṟam paṟṟic cuviyāṉa karuttoṉṟik kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu veḷiyiṭukiṟār.

“nāṉ eṉ kikaḷi vīci vārttikaḷ ētumiṉṟi muṇumuṇutta vaṇṇam naṭakkiṟēṉ; eṉ muṇu muṇuppukku iṭiyūṟu varāta vaṇṇam naṭakkiṟēṉ. eṉ naṭiyil vēkam kūṭumpōtu, nāṉ vēkamāka muṇumuṇukkiṟēṉ.



﻿ “ippaṭiyē taṉṉittāṉē cetukkik koṇṭu, kavitikku aṭippaṭiyāṉa cantam pīṟiṭṭuk kiḷampukiṟatu. avvāṟu kiḷampum anta ōciyiliruntu coṟkaḷ vaṭivam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

“pīṟiṭṭuk kiḷampum antac canta ōci eṅkiruntu varukiṟatu eṉṟu yārukkut teriyum? atu eṉakkuḷ tirumpat tirumpat tōṉṟum ōr oli; oru tālāṭṭu; eṉṉuḷ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum tōṉṟum ētō oṉṟukku nāṉ koṭukkum oli vaṭivam. anta olikaḷi muyaṉṟu iyakkutalum, oṉṟic cuṟṟi anta olikaḷi oḻuṅkupaṭutti amittalum, avaṟṟiṉ iyalpiyum paṇpiyum kaṇṭaṟitalumtāṉ kavitiyiṉ toṭarnta uḻippākum.”

ruciyak kaviñar sveṭṭēvāvum kavitit tōṟṟampaṟṟi, itē pōṉṟa karuttiyē veḷiyiṭukiṟāḷ. “eṉ paṭippukkaḷ ellāmē eṉ kēṭkum ceyal” eṉpatu avaḷ karuttu.

jermāṉiyak kaviñarrilkkiṉ aṉupavam ivarkaḷiṉiṉṟum caṟṟu māṟupaṭṭatu. ṭiyūṉō kōṭṭiyil oru nāḷ taṉimiyil amarntiruntapōtu, taṉ uḷḷattil nikaḻnta kaviti āvēcatti rilk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu paṭarkkiyil pativu ceykiṟār:

"iṉampuriyāta oṉṟu avaṉiṭam aṉṟu nikaḻntatu. vaḻakkampōl oru puttakattik kiyilēntiya vaṇṇam aṅkumiṅkumāka naṭantu koṇṭirunta avaṉ, kaviyākap pirintirunta marakkiḷiyoṉṟil vacatiyāka amarntu koṇṭāṉ. mikavum ōyvāka amarnta niliyil kiyilirunta puttakatti mūṭi vittu viṭṭuc cuṟṟiyirunta amitiyāṉa, aḻakiya iyaṟkic cūḻniliyil taṉṉi maṟantu mūḻkiṉāṉ.

"itaṟkumuṉ aṉupavittirāta ōr uṇarcci veḷḷam avaṉuḷ taliyākap paravuvati uṇarntāṉ. avaṉ amarntirunta marattiṉiṭiyiliruntu avvuṇarcci melliya atirvukaḷāka avaṉuḷ iṟaṅkiyatu. itu pōṉṟa iṉpa atircciyi avaṉ eṉṟum aṉupavittatilli, avaṉ uṭampē āṉmāvākamāṟi iyalpāṉa taṉ ceyalkaḷi maṟantu ētō oru pērāṟṟali taṉṉuḷ ēṟṟuk koṇṭatu pōliruntatu; atuvumaṉṟi eḷitil pulappaṭātiyaṟkiyiṉ maṟupuṟattik kaṇṭatu pōlavum iruntatu."

māyakōvski acāttiyat tuṇiccalkāraṉ. vāṉattil ēṟivarum pakalavaṉit tēnīr aruntak kūppiṭukiṟāṉ.

﻿ eṉṉa...?

kaṭavuḷē!

nāṉ vantirukkiṟēṉ.

kātil viḻukiṟatā?

uṉ toppiyittukki

vaṇakkam cey!





eṉṟu vaḻippōkkaṉiṭam pēcuvatu pōl āṇṭavaṉiṭam tuṭukkākap pēcukiṟāṉ. karuttil maṭṭumaṉṟu; vaṭivattilum cila tuṇiccalāṉa muyaṟcikaḷi avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭāṉ.

kaviti ceṟivākavum, iṟukkamākavum irukka vēṇṭum eṉpati ellāk kaviñarkaḷum ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟārkaḷ. jappāṉil hikkūvum, tamiḻil kuṟaḷum vaṭivattāl ciṟappup peṟṟavi. māyakōvski miṉ veṭṭukkaḷip pōl paḷicciṭum tantik kaviti vaṭivam oṉṟit tōṟṟuvittirukkiṟāṉ.

tākam-taṇikka

paci-tīrkka

uṭampu-itunēram

pōrkakaḷam-pōriṭa



kuṇṭukaḷ paṟakkaṭṭum ,

kōḻikaḷi nōkki

tuppākki muḻaṅkaṭṭum

etiri ōṭa





kavitiyi oru ‘varamākavum’ kalitvētiyiṉ kaṭik kaṇ nōkkākavum ellārum pōṟṟuvatu vaḻakkam. āṉāl oru ruciya iḷaṅkaviñaṉ,

kavitiyē!

eṉ turatirṣṭamē!

eṉ celvamē!

eṉ puṉitak kaliyē!





eṉṟu pāṭukiṟāṉ. kavitiyit ‘turatirṣṭam’ eṉṟu avaṉ kuṟippiṭak kāraṇam uṇṭu. kaviti ellārukkum pukaḻiyum celvattiyum vārik koṭuttu viṭuvatilli. nalla kaviti eḻutavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākac cilar taṅkaḷ vāḻkki vacatiyiyum, varuvāyiyum, makiḻcciyiyum, tiyākam ceyvati iṉṟum kāṇkiṟōm. nalla kavitikku avarkaḷ koṭukkum vili ivi. eṉavē kaviti, kaviñaṉukku oruvakit turatirṣṭam tāṉē?

‘intiyam’ cila airōppiyak kaviñarkaḷāl matittup pōṟṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. eliyaṭ tamatu ciṟanta kāppiyamāṉa pāḻnilatti.

tattā, tayātavam, tamyatā

cānti cānti cānti





﻿eṉṟu upaniṭata varikaḷāl niṟivu ceykiṟār. intiyac camayamāṉa peḷattattiyum naṉku purintu koṇṭirukkiṟār. āṉāl teṉṉamerikkak kaviñaṉāṉa pāplō neruṭā,

aḻakiya nirvāṇap puttarkaḷ

matuviruntip pārttavaṇṇam

tiṟanta veḷiyil

veṟumiyākac

cirittukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.





eṉṟu intiya nāṭṭiyum, intiyac camayattiyum kēli ceykiṟāṉ. innūlil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa pattukkaviñarkaḷum mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ varalāṟē putukkaviti varalāṟu. taramāṉa kaviñarkaḷiyum, taramāṉa kavitikaḷiyum purintu koṇṭu cuvikka, innūl, niccayamāka utavum.

mēvār mīrāviṉ kuṟikkōḷ piruntāvaṉamum nantakumāraṉum; civakaṅki mīrāviṉ kuṟikkōḷ nalla naṇparkaḷum nalla nūlkaḷum nāṉ avarukku nalla naṇpaṉ; nalla nūlum eḻutik koṭuttirukkiṟēṉ.

cēlam-16





aṉpuṭaṉ





1–1–93





muruku





﻿kalic coṟkaḷ



aḻakiyam — Aestheticism

āṉmarakaciyam — Mysticism

iruṇmi — Obscurity

kaṭṭaṟṟa kaviti — Vers libre

taṟkātaliyam — Narcissism

tuṇṭuk kaviti — Hack Verse

toṉmam — Myth

naṭappiyam — Functionalism

patippiyam — Impressionism

cevviyam — Classicism

puṟa meymmiyiyam — Surrealism

puṟanili oppīṭu — Objective co-relative

puṉiviyam — Romanticism

poruḷ mutal vātam — Materialism

maṟuppiyam — Nihilism

mikip puṉiviyam — Romantic excess

meymmiyiyam — Realism

paci, iṉpam, aṉpukkiṉiya tāy

ākiya ellāvaṟṟukkum mēlāka

nāṉ nēcippatu ilakkiyamē.





cārl pōtalēr

(1821-1867)



eṭkār ālaṉpō eṉṟa amerikka eḻuttāḷariṉ paṭippiṉ mītu pōtalērukku aḷavu kaṭanta īṭupāṭu uṇṭu. ilakkiyat tuṟiyil avarit tam kuruvākavum vaḻikāṭṭiyākavum ēṟṟuk koṇṭār. atōṭu niṉṟiruntāl paravāyilli; avaruṭiya paḻakkaṅkaḷiyum ēṟṟuk koṇṭār.

ālaṉpō oru apiṉ piriyar: pōtalēr kañcāp piriyar. apiṉum kañcāvum payaṉpaṭuttupavarit tūkkattil āḻttuvatilli. avarkaḷ uḷḷattil oruvitak kiḷarcciyi ēṟpaṭutti, virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa kaṉavu nilikku āṭpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttiyavuṭaṉ avarkaḷ uḷḷam entap pōkkil payaṇm ceykiṉṟatō, antap pōkkiṟku avarkaḷi entavitak kaṭṭuppāṭum illāmal celuttukiṉṟaṉa.

kañcā mayakkam ēṟpaṭuttum kuḻappamāṉa aṟputam paṟṟip pōtalēr piṉkaṇṭavāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār: "kañcāp pōtiyiṉ tuvakkattil, vaḻakkattiṟku māṟāṉa vaṇṇattiṉ ātikkam namakkup pulappaṭum. kaṇṇip paṟṟikkum oḷiyuṭampōṭu kāṭci tarum mōkiṉip peṇkaḷ, nīla vāṉi viṭat teḷivāṉa āḻamāṉa viḻikaḷāl nammi uṟṟup pārppārkaḷ. appōtu ēṟpaṭum iṉam puriyāta kṣaṇanēra maṉanilikaḷil nam vāḻkkiyiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷum neḷivu cuḻivukaḷum, aṟputaṅkaḷum nam kaṇmuṉ paḷicciṭum. nam kaṇṇukku mutaṉ mutalil entap poruḷ teṉpaṭukiṟatō, atuvē nam aṟivi naṭattic cellum kuṟiyīṭāka amiyum. teḷinta nīrnilikaḷum, nilik kaṇṇāṭikaḷum kaṉavulakatti namakkut tiṟantuviṭum; cōkamayamāṉa oru pēriṉpattil nam uḷḷam āḻntu, elliyaṟṟa kāṭcikaḷil tiḷikkum."

ittakiya aṉupavattil tiḷittu mikavum nūtaṉamāṉa uṇarcci veḷippāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya ariya kavitikaḷip paṭitta kaviñar pōtalēr maṭṭum allar; vēṟupalarum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. makākavi pāratiyārukkum kañcāp paḻakkam uṇṭu; kuyilpāṭṭum inta aṭippaṭiyil, avar virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa kaṉavu nilik kaṟpaṉi tāṉ. kōlariṭjiṉ 'kupḷāykāṉ' eṉṟa kavitiyiyum, inta variciyil cērttuk koḷḷalām.

piyar cārl pōtalēr (Baudelire Piere Charles) 1821 ām āṇṭil ōyvu peṟṟa caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉariṉ makaṉākap pāricil piṟantār. ivar ciṟuvaṉāka irukkum pōtē tanti iṟantār tāy ōr irāṇuva atikāriyi maṟumaṇam ceytukoṇṭār. pōtalēr paḷḷik kalviyi muṭittukkoṇṭu caṭṭakkallūriyil nuḻintār.

vīṭṭuk kaṭaṅkāta piḷḷiyāka irunta ivarik kappalēṟṟik kalkattāvukku aṉuppiṉar peṟṟōr. āṉāl naṭuvaḻiyil morīṣiyas tīvil iṟaṅkik koṇṭār. veppamaṇṭalattil irunta morīṣiyas tīvil cilakālam taṅkiyiruntu vetuvetuppāṉa aṉupavaṅkaḷōṭu pārīs tirumpiṉār pōtalēr.

cutantaramāṉa paṇavacatiyōṭu irunta pōtalēr, pārīciṉ navanākarikak kēntiramāṉa 'lattīṉ kuvārṭṭars' eṉṟa pakutiyil kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa ullāca vāḻkkiyil īṭupaṭṭār. 'ciṟappuk keṭṭavar' (decadent) eṉṟa peyari virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭār. nīkrōk kalappu iṉak kāriki, karuppu rati ḻāṉ tuyvāl (Black Venus Jeanne Duval) eṉpavaḷ ivarukku nirantarak kātaliyākak kiṭittāḷ. cīmāṭṭi caparttiyar eṉṟa naṭikiyiṭamum avarukkut toṭarpiruntatu. pōtalēriṉ corkka narakak kaṟpaṉikaḷ, ivviruvar mītum avar koṇṭa kātaṟ cuḻiyilē cuḻaṉṟaṭikkiṉṟaṉa.

iḷamiyil avar virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa koḷki 'ṭāṇṭiyicam'. taṉ viruppappaṭi vāḻtal, taṉmuṉippōṭu mutaṉmiyāka iruttal, naṭpu, nāṭu, kuṭumpam eṉṟa paṟṟukkaḷi aṟuttuc cutantā maṉitaṉāka vāḻtal, pirapukkaḷi veṟittal eṉpaṉa ṭāṇṭiyicattiṉ kuṟik kōḷkaḷ

“paci, iṉpam, aṉpukkiṉiya tāy ākiya ellāvaṟṟukkum mēlāka nāṉ nēcippatu ilakkiyamē” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭum pōtalēr 'kali kalikkākavē' eṉṟu koḷkiyuṭiyavar. romāṇṭik kaviñarkaḷ virumpip pōṟṟum, 'poṅkavarum maṉaveḻuccik koḷkiyi' ivar ēṟṟuk koḷvatilli. kalikku oru teyvīka mariyātiyum ivariṭattil kiṭiyātu.

"aḻaku, aṟam, kali yāvum ceyaṟkiyāṉavi; avi kaliñaṉ allatu kaviñaṉiṉ tiṭṭamiṭṭa cintaṉiyiliruntu tōṉṟupavi. kavitiyi entavitap piracārattukkum payaṉpaṭuttak kūṭātu. aḻakiṉ mītu aṭaṅkākkātal koḷḷutalum, uḷḷattiṉ āḻattiliruntu puṟappaṭum aḻippukaḷukku aṭipaṇitalum tavirkka muṭiyātavi eṉṟa kāraṇattāl, kaliñaṉum kaviñaṉum tām mēṟkoṇṭa paṭippup paṇikku nirantara aṭimi" eṉṟa cintaṉip pōkkuṭiyavar pōtalēr.

kavitiyiṉ karupporuḷ uyarntatākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttil pōtalērukku uṭaṉpāṭu kiṭiyātu. aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil taṭṭuppaṭum cātāraṇap poruḷkaḷum, iḻinta poruḷkaḷumē aḻakāṉavi eṉṟum, apporuḷkaḷi aḻakiya collōviyattāl alaṅkarippatē mēlāṉa kaviti utti (Grand Style of Poetry) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭār. ēḻi, kuṭikāraṉ, terup piccikkāraṉ, vipacāri, apali ākiyōrum paḻi, pāvam tīviṉi, pollāṅku ākiyaviyum, avar kavitikku virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa karupporuḷkaḷ. ikkoḷkikku avar pāṭiyuḷḷa 'cūriyaṉ' eṉṟa kaviti ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭu, mēṟku vāyilil paṭiyum mālic cūriyaṉiṉ varṇa jālattāl ulakiṉ cātāraṇap poruḷkaḷum eppaṭip pēraḻaku pūṇukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati appāṭṭil ciṟappittup pāṭukiṟār. ittakiya racavātatti ēṟpaṭuttak kaviñaṉāṉavaṉ mappumantāramaṟṟa mēl vāṉattukkut taṉ oḷipaṭitta kaṇkaḷi uyartta vēṇṭum; appōtu poruḷaṟṟa aṟpap poruḷkaḷum nuṭpamāṉa poruḷ viḷakkam peṟum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār pōtalēr. pōtalēriṉ ik koḷkiyi mānakark koḷki (The religious intoxication of great cities) eṉṟu tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.

‘the spantaneous cverflcw of powerful feelings’



mēlināṭṭuk kali ilakkiyap pōkki aṭiyōṭu māṟṟiyamitta āṟṟalmikka iyakkaṅkaḷāṉa aṭimaṉaviyam, kuṟiyīṭṭiyam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ūṟṟuk kaṇṇē pōtalērtāṉ eṉpatu ellōrum ēṟṟuk koṇṭa uṇmi. avaruṭiya kaviti nūlāṉa 'naccup pūkkaḷ' (Les Fleurs du Mal) 1857 ām āṇṭu mutaṉmutalāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. atil teyva nintaṉi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟic camayavātikaḷ kaṇṭaṉak kuāl eḻuppiṉar. paṭippavar uḷḷattil kīḻttaramāṉa uṇarccikaḷi avar kavitikaḷ tūṇṭuvatākak kuṟṟam cāṭṭi pireñcu aracāṅkam 300 pirāṅk avarukku aparātam vitittatu.

avarukkup piṉ vanta cantatiyiṉariṭiyē naccup pūkkaḷip pōl aparimitamāṉa celvākkuppeṟṟa vēṟu kaviti nūl mēli nāṭṭil etuvumilli eṉṟu collalām. mēli nāṭṭiṉ kavitip putumiyē (Modernism in Poetry) in nūlil toṭaṅkukiṟatu.

putuk kaviñarkaḷiṉ muṉmātiriyē (Archtype) pōtalērtāṉ. putuk kaviñarkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷāṉa variyaṟukkappaṭṭa taṉimi, etirppuṇarcci, maṉac cañcalam, taṉṉiyē aḻittuk koḷḷum āttira uṇarcci yāvum pōtalēriṭamiruntu peṟṟaviyē. “piṟar tūṟṟumpaṭi puraṭcikaramāka vāḻnta pōtalēruṭiya vāḻkkiyiṉ aṭikkuṟippup paṟṟiya varalāṟē, putuk kavitiyiṉ tōṟṟam, vaḷarcci paṟṟiya varalāṟākum” eṉṟu mēli nāṭṭut tiṟaṉāyvāḷar kuṟippiṭuvatu poruttamē.

“kaṟpaṉi eṉpatu aṟiviyal aṭippaṭiyil aminta pērāṟṟal. pēraṇṭattiṉ oḻuṅkiyum iyakkattiyum kaṟpaṉi āṟṟalāl tāṉuṇara muṭiyum” eṉṟu pōtalēr kuṟippiṭṭār. svīṭaṉparkkum ālaṉpōvum itē koḷkiyuṭiyavarkaḷ.

pōtalēr ōr aiyuṟavāḷar (Sceptic). kaṟpaṉi oru pērāṟṟal eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭālum atu ki koṭuttu utavāta cila nēraṅkaḷil ataṉ mītu avarukku aiyaṟavu ēṟpaṭṭatu. anta nēraṅkaḷil kaṟpaṉikku ūkkam koṭuppataṟkāka matuviyum, kañcāviyum nāṭiṉār. avaṟṟiṉ tūṇṭutalāl oru ceyaṟkic corkkatti avarē paṭittukkoṇṭu atil tiḷittār.

pōtalēr kaṭavuṭpaṟṟō matappaṟṟō illātavar. eṉṟālum avar uḷḷattil paṭintuviṭṭa camayakkaruttukkaḷ cilavaṟṟi avarāl utaṟa muṭiyavilli. kiṟittuva camayak koḷkippaṭi, maṉitaṉ pāviyākavē irukkiṟāṉ eṉṟa uṇarvum, ātāmum ēvāḷum pāvam ceyvataṟkumuṉ vāḻnta 'ētēṉ' eṉṟ corkkatti maṉitaṉ mīṇṭum aṭiyamuṭiyātu eṉṟa uṇarvum, ivvulakiṉ tuṉpamākiya kōrappiṭiyiliruntu yārum viṭupaṭamuṭiyātu eṉṟa uṇarvum pōtalēri āṭṭippaṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ittuṉpa ulakiṉ piṭiyiliruntu viṭupaṭṭuttaṉ āṉmāvip paravacappaṭuttuvataṟkāka, avarē paṭittuk koṇṭatutāṉ ceyaṟakic corkkam!'

pōtalēriṉ naccuppūkkaḷmūṉṟukottāka malarntirukkiṉṟaṉa. mutalkottu avar uḷaccōrviyum, iraṇṭāvatu kottu pāris nakarak kāṭcikaḷiyum, mūṉṟāvatu kottu ceyaṟkic corkkatti nōkkiya avar payaṇattiyum, vivarikkiṉṟaṉa. iḷamiyil kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟi vāḻnta tam iḻitta vāḻkkiyip palapāṭalkaḷil avar tirumpip pārttu uḷaccōrvu koḷkiṟār. tammi vāṭṭum uḷac cōrvil aḻuntippōvatā allatu ceyaṟkic corkkattil paṟappatā eṉṟa ūcalāṭṭam pala pāṭalkaḷil kaṟpaṉi nayattōṭu eṭutturikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. pala pāṭalkaḷil ceyaṟkic corkkattil avar aṭiyum paravacanili pēcappaṭukiṉṟatu. peṇṇāl peṟum aimpulaṉ iṉpattik kaṟpaṉi ceyyāmal oli, oḷi, maṇam ivaṟṟāl peṟum iṉpaṅkaḷiyē kaṟpaṉiceykiṟār pōtalēr. avaruṭiya corkkattil varum aṭimikaḷum ēvalarkaḷum nirvāṇamākak kāṭcitarukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷuṭiya nirvāṇam, ivvulaka nākarikattiṉ tīkkaraṅkaḷāl tīṇṭappaṭāta tūymiyiṉ uruvakam.

ceyaṟkic corkkattil pōtalēr aṭikkaṭi tiḷittup paravacappaṭṭālum, cāvip paṟṟiya eṇṇam avar uḷḷatti viṭṭu nīṅkavilli. naccuppūkkaḷiṉ iṟutiyil uḷḷa 'payaṇam' eṉṟa pāṭṭil tām kaṭaliṉ ali vayiṟṟil āḻntuviṭa viruppam terivikkiṟār. tām ceṉṟaṭiyavirumpum kuṟikkōḷ corkkamākiruntālum cari, narakamāka iruntālum cari, iṉan teriyāta oṉṟaṉ āḻattil tām mūḻkiviṭa virumpukiṟār. 'ēḻiyiṉ cāvu' eṉaṟa pāṭalil cāvi makiḻcci tarum iṉpa uṇarvōṭu varavēṟṟup pāṭukiṟār.

cāvē!

teyvīkap perumpukaḻē!

tēṭalukkup pulappaṭāta

kaṟpaṉik kaḷañciyamē!

ēḻi virumpum pōtu

eṭuttuc celviṭum

paramparic cottē!

purintu koḷḷa muṭiyāta

vāṉulakiṉ vāyiṟ katavē!





eṉṟu cāvit taṉ caṅkīta varikaḷāl alaṅkarikki ceyaṟkic corkkattup paravaca niliyikkūṭa maraṇat tuṉpattik kalantutāṉ aṉupavittār. [1]'citiriyāp payaṇam' eṉṟa kavitiyil, kātal tīvē avarukkuk kaṟi paṭintu kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.

vāṉam—

teḷinta aḻakōṭu

kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.

kaṭalum—

āḻnta amitiyuṭaṉ

paṭuttirukkiṟatu.

āṉāl—

eṅkum iruḷ:

tiṭṭut tiṭṭāṉa

rattak kaṟikaḷ

eṉ itayam—

oru muraṭṭuk kākitattil

curuṭṭap paṭṭup

putintu kiṭappatāka

eṉakkuḷ ōr uruvakam.



ō viṉasē!

uṉ kātal tīvil

oru kaṟpaṉit

tūkku marattil

eṉa uruvam

toṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu.

āṇṭavaṉē!

eṉ uṭampiyum

itayattiyum

veṟuppillāmal pārkka

eṉakku āṟṟalik koṭu!





eṉṟu vēṇṭukiṟār pōtalēr.

avar eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭaṟpuṟavi (Albatross) eṉṟa pāṭal avaruṭiya avastiyiṉ kuṟiyīṭākavum, naccup pūkkaḷiṉ miyak karuttākavum amintuḷḷatu. ālpaṭrās eṉṟa kaṭaṟpaṟavi nīṇṭa iṟakkikaḷi uṭiyatu, ati vāṉamaṇṭalattaracaṉ (The prince of the clouds) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. vāṉatti aṉāyācamāka aḷakka utavum apperiya ciṟakukaḷē. pūmiyi aṭintatum ataṟkupperiya cumiyāka māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟit tūkkikkoṇṭu appaṟaviyāl aṭiyeṭuttu naṭakkamuṭivatilli.

pōtalēr tamatu āṟṟal mikka kaṟpaṉiyiṉ utaviyōṭu corkkamaṇṭalattil paṟantālum, ivvulakap paṟṟum, ciṟṟiṉpa icciyum tammip piṇittu muṭakki viṭuvatāka eṇṇi varuntukiṟār; tām āḻntirukkum tuṉpak kaṭaliliruntu mīṭciyē kiṭiyātu eṉṟa varuttam aṭikkaṭi avariṭam mēlōṅki niṟpatuṇṭu.

pāris nakaram

māṟik koṇṭirukkiṟatu

eṉṉi aḻuttum

tuyarac cumi

ciṟitu kūṭa aciyavilli





eṉṟu avaruṭiya tuyarap perumūccu orupāṭalil kēṭkiṟatu. maṟṟoru pāṭalil,

eṉ tuyaramē

aṟivōṭu naṭantukoḷ;

amitiyāka iru!

iraviṉ koṭumikkañci

nī kataṟukiṟāy?

iravu viḻuntuviṭṭatu

itō—

viṭiyal tali tūkkukiṟatu:





eṉṟu ñāṉipōl tamakkuttāmē āṟutal kūṟik koḷkiṟār.

eṉṟālum avar vāḻvil miñciyatu ēmāṟṟamē. avar eḻutiyuḷḷa urip pāṭalkaḷil, vāḻviṉ iṟutiyil avar koṇṭa calippum, ēmāṟṟamum urukkamākap pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

"cila camayaṅkaḷil eṉṉi oru ñāṉiyeṉṟu nāṉ muṭṭāḷtaṉamāka niṉittuk koḷvatuṇṭu. oru maruttuvaṉukku iruppatupōl, aruḷuḷḷam eṉṉiṭattil amiyavēṇṭum eṉṟu nāṉ avāvuvatuṇṭu; appēṟu eṉakkuk kiṭikkappōvatilli. ivvañcaka ulakil eṉṉi nāṉē tolittuviṭṭu, makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉ puṟaṅkiyāl iṭittut taḷḷappaṭṭu alintu tirintu koṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. eṉ kaṭantakāla niṉivukaḷiṉ āḻatti aṭikkaṭi eṭṭip pārttu nāṉ mikavum kaḷittup pōyviṭṭēṉ. ēmāṟṟamum, kacappuṇarcciyum, tuṉpamā-mīṭciyā eṉṟu uṇara muṭiyāta kuḻappamumē eṉakku miñciyavi eṉṟu pulampukiṟār pōtalēr.

mēkattiṉ ucciyil paṟappataṟku utaviya periya ciṟakukaḷiṉ cumi, pūmiyil kaṭaṟpaṟaviyic ceyalaṟṟatākkuvatupōl, kaṟpaṉic corkkattil paṟappataṟku ivarukku utaviyāka irunta ivaratu ciṟakukaḷē (kañcā, aḷavaṟṟa matuppaḻakkam) kaṭici nāṭkaḷil ivari muṭakkip pōṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. pakkavātam, ivari muṭakkippōṭṭup paṭukkiyil taḷḷiviṭṭatu. 'cipilis' ivaric citittu viṭṭatu.

nīṇṭanāḷ kañcāp paḻakkattiṉ kaṭici milkal, tali cuṟṟalum (Vertigo) pittiyamumtāṉ. taṉ kaṭici niliyippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu "iṉpamum atircciyum koṇṭa iḻuppu nōy (Hysteria) eṉakkup paḻakkamākip pōyviṭṭatu. toṭarnatu talicuṟṟalāl nāṉ avatippaṭṭuk koṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. 23-1-1862 ām nāḷākiya iṉṟu, etirpārāta ōr eccarikki eṉakkuk kiṭṭiyatu. paṟantu cellum pittiyac ciṟaku vīcciṉ kāṟṟu, eṉmītu paṭintu ceṉṟati nāṉ uṇarntēṉ" eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

taṉpeyarikkūṭa niṉivukūra muṭiyāta niliyilum, nilik kaṇṇāṭiyil taṉ conta mukattiyē aṭiyāḷam kaṇṭukoḷḷa muṭiyāta niliyilum, puṟakkaṇippukku āḷāki, ātaravaṟṟut tamatu kaṭici nāṭkaḷip pāricil kaḻittār. mikac ciṟanta kaviñarāṉa pōtalērip pukaḻpeṟṟa pireñcu ilakkiyak kaḻakam (French Academy) uṟuppiṉarāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷa maṟantuviṭṭatu. anta vētaṉiyit tāḷāta avar, pārici viṭṭu nīṅki peljiyattil coṟpoḻivup payaṇam oṉṟi mēṟkoṇṭār. aliccal avar uṭali mikavum pātittatu. 1867il pārīs maruttuvamaṉiyoṉṟil uyirtuṟantār.

tām ceyyum entap paṇiyiyum, ceppamākavum, tiruttamākavum ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷkiyuṭiyavar pōtalēr. niṟiya eḻutuvati avar virumpavilli. oṉṟu ceytālum, oppaṟṟatāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu avar viruppam. naccuppūkkaḷi 1850-il toṭaṅkiṉār. ataṉ kuṟikaḷik kaḷintu, pattāṇṭukaḷ tiruttam ceytu, mēṉmēlum aḻakupaṭuttiṉār. 1861-il ataṉ iṟuti vaṭivam uruppeṟṟatu.

pōtalēriṉ paṭippukaḷ paṟṟi ārtar cimaṉs eṉṟa tiṟaṉāyvāḷar, tām eḻutiyuḷḷa "ilakkiyattil kuṟiyīṭṭu iyakkam' (The Symbolist Movement in Literature) eṉṟa nūlil piṉvarumāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār:

"orē oru kaviti nūli eḻutuvatil pōtalēr tamatu vāḻkki muḻuvatiyum celaviṭṭār; ataṉ piṟakutāṉ pireñcu moḻiyil uyarnta kavitikaḷ ellām tōṟṟam eṭuttaṉa.



"ōr uri naṭi nūl eḻutiṉār; anta urinaṭi aḻakuk kaliyāka (Fine Art) matikkappaṭukiṟatu.

oru tiṟaṉāyvu nūl eḻutiṉār. avar vāḻnta kālattil tōṉṟiya tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷil ituvē uyarntatum uṇmiyāṉatum nuṭpamāṉatum ākum.

orumoḻipeyarppuc ceytār; atu mūlatti viṭac ciṟappāṉatu”.

pōtalēr eḻutiyuḷḷa urippākkaḷ (pāriciṉ iḻi nili’[2]) aimpatu irukkum. avi etukiyō, iciyoḻuṅkō illāmal eḻutappaṭṭavi ; uḷḷatatiṉ uṇarccikaḷukkēṟṟa ōciyoḻuṅkum taṉṉi maṟanta cintaṉiyiṉ alivīccum koṇṭavi; yārum eḷitil piṉpaṟṟa muṭiyātapaṭi kalinuṇukkamum, vēlippāṭum mikkavi; urippākkaḷ varalāṟṟil mutaṉmiyākak kuṟippiṭattakkavi; cila paṇpukaḷil oppaṟṟavi; ilakkiyaccōtaṉi muyaṟciyil pārāṭṭattakka ariya veṟṟi.

tilākriks eṉṟa ōviyar paṟṟiyum, tāmiyar, māṉeṭ, ḵpḷāparṭ, vēkṉar eṉṟa eḻuttāḷarkaḷ paṟṟiyum avar eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭṭurikaḷ, tiṟaṉāyvukkali vaḷarccipeṟāta kālattil eḻutappaṭṭavi. akkaṭṭurikaḷipa peruma ilakkiya cātaṉiyākap piṟkāla aṟiñarkaḷ pārāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.

amerikka eḻuttāḷar ālaṉpōviṉ katikaḷip pōtalēr pireñcu moḻiyil moḻipeyarttirukkiṟār. mūlattiṉ ciṟappuk kuṉṟāmalum, mūlattiviṭac ciṟanta kaliyamcattuṭaṉum atu ceyyappaṭṭiruppatāka ellārum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. amerikka makkaḷiṭiyē inta moḻi peyarppukku nalla varavēṟpu uḷḷatu.

1865 ām āṇṭil tamatu paṭippukkaḷ paṟṟit tam tāyiṭam kuṟippiṭum pōtu “eṉ paṭippiṉ aḷavu ciṟiyatāka irukkalām; āṉāl acciṟiya paṭippu eṉ tuyaram mikka kaṭiṉa uḻippiṉ veḷippāṭu” eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṟār

1920 ām āṇṭil āntirēkiṭ eṉṟa aṟiñar pōtalērip paṟṟip pattirikiyil kuṟippiṭumpōtu “avaruṭiya ovvoru muyaṟciyum, avaratu āṉmāviṉ paritavippākavum, oppaṟṟa pōrāṭṭamākavum amintuḷḷatu” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. paṭittavar uḷḷattil eṉṟum nilittu niṟkumpaṭi eṭkār ālaṉ pōvip paṟṟip pōtalēr uṇarcci pūrvamāka oru karutti veḷiyiṭṭār. “iṉṟu nam makiḻccikku ūṟṟāka iruppatu (avar paṭippu) etuvō, atuvē avarik koṉṟuviṭṭatu” eṉṟār. antak karuttu pōtalērukkum poruntum.





↑ citiriyā-kirēkkak kātal tēvati vīṉas vīṟṟirukkum tīvu.

↑ Spleen de Paris.





* * *





eṉṉāl kātalic

cāḷarattukku veḷiyē

tūkki eṟiya muṭiyātu.





ārtar rempō

(1854–1891)



rempō pireñcu nāṭṭu ñāṉacampantaṉ piñcupparuvattil eḻutat toṭaṅkit taṉatu 19 ām vayatil-kavitit toḻili muṭittuk koṇṭavaṉ. aintāṇṭuk kālattil avaṉ eḻutiya coṟpak kavitikaḷ airōppiyak kavitip pōkkiyē māṟṟiyamikkum āṟṟal peṟṟaviyāka viḷaṅkiṉa. avaṉ kaviti vāḻkki kaṇa nērattil miṉṉalip pōṉṟatu; cīṟum puyalip pōṉṟatu. kōṭi maḻiyip pōl tiṭīreṉṟu koṭṭi viṭṭu ōyntu pōṉavaṉ rempō inta vāmaṉa vacantattip pārāṭṭāta mēli nāṭṭu ilakkiyavāti yārumilli.

pireñcu nāṭṭiṉ vaṭa pakutiyil cārlivil eṉṟa ciṟiya nakaril 1954-il piṟantāṉ rempō. ivaṉ tanti ḵpreṭrik rempō pireñcup paṭiyil ‘kēpṭaṉ’ patavi vakittavar. avar irāṇuvap paṇi perumpālum aljīriyāvil kaḻintatu. ḵpreṭrik rempō arapu moḻiyi naṉku kaṟṟavar. kurāṉi pireñcil moḻi peyarttavar.

kaviñaṉ rempōviṉ tāy, kaṇṭippum aṭaṅkāp piṭārit taṉamum mikkavaḷ. avaḷōṭu vāḻat tākkup piṭikka muṭiyāmal, kaviñaṉ rempō āṟuvayatuc ciṟuvaṉāka irukkum pōtē, avaṉ tanti ḵpreṭrik vīṭṭi viṭṭu ōṭi viṭṭār. ataṉ piṟaku rempōvō, avaṉ tāyō avari mīṇṭum cantikkavē illi.

rempō pattu vayatup paḷḷic ciṟuvaṉāka irukkum pōtē ati nuṭpamum, piṭivātamum, putumi viruppamum pōkkirittaṉamum uṭiyavaṉākak kāṇappaṭṭāṉ. paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatil mutaṉ mutalāka avaṉ eḻutiya ilattīṉ kaviti avaṉukkup paḷḷip paricip peṟṟuk koṭuttatu. atu iṭiyar vāḻkkiyik kūṟum nāṭṭup puṟappāṭal[1], patiṉāṉkām vayatil avaṉ eḻutiya ‘ēḻuvayatukkaviñaṉ’ (Seven-year-old poet) eṉṟa kavitiyil taṉ aṟivu nuṭpattit tāṉē pārāṭṭi eḻutiyirukkiṟāṉ. etir kālattil avaṉ nikaḻttavirukkum piramippūṭṭum cātaṉikaḷukku ik kavitikaḷ kaṭṭiyaṅ kūṟuvatōṭu, avaṉ oru navīṉa ārḵpis[2] eṉpatiyum nili nāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.

iyaṟkiyilēyē viṭutali vēṭkiyum piṭivātamum koṇṭa rempōvukkut tāyiṉ kaṇṭippum aṭakku muṟiyum aṭiyōṭu piṭikkavilli, paḻamiyil ūṟip pōṉa naṭuttara varkkattiṉ vāḻkkiyil avaṉukku veṟuppum calippum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. aṭikkaṭi tāyiṭam collāmal ūri viṭṭu ōṭuvatum, tirumpuvatumāka iruntāṉ. intac camayattil tāṉ kaviti veṟi avaṉip paṟṟik koṇṭatu.

1871 ām āṇṭu taṉ patiṉēḻām vayatil pāricukku ōṭiya pōtu, aṅkirunta pukaḻpeṟṟa kaviñarākiya pālverlēṉic cantikkum vāyppu avaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭatu. rempō verlēṉiṉ kuṭumpattil oruvaṉākavē iṭam peṟṟāṉ. āṉāl verlēṉiṉ tāyum maṉiviyum rempōvi veṟuttaṉar. avaṉuṭiya vicittiramāṉa pēccum, koḷkiyum, oḻuṅkīṉamāṉa naṭi muṟikaḷum, paṭukkiyil iruntapaṭiyē pukipiṭikkum paḻakkamum avarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭōṭu piṭikkavilli. itaṉāl verlēṉiṉ tampatiyāriṭiyē maṇamuṟivu ēṟpaṭṭatu. rempōvukkum verlēṉukkum iṭiyil ēṟpaṭṭa takāta pāluṟavup paḻakkam, kaṭiciyil tuppākkic caṇṭiyil muṭintatu. 1974 ām āṇṭil ivarkaḷ iṭiyil ēṟpaṭṭa inta uṟavu muṟivu rempōviṉ kaviti vāḻkkikku aṭiyōṭu muṟṟuppuḷḷi vittuviṭṭatu. verlēṉuṭiya kavitip paṇiyilum carivu ēṟpaṭṭatu.

taṉatu pattoṉpatām vayatil kaviti eḻutuvati viṭṭuviṭṭu, airōppāvilum mattiyak kiḻakku nāṭukaḷilum, āppirikkāvilum cuṟṟiyalintāṉ. appōtu piḻippukkāka avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa paṇikaḷ pala. koñcanāḷ icippayiṟciyum, piṟamoḻip payiṟciyum mēṟkoṇṭāṉ; ṭaccuk kālaṉip paṭiyil cērntu cilakālam paṇi purintāṉ. cipracil kaṭṭiṭa mēstiriyāka vēli pārttāṉ. kaṭiciyil apicīṉiyāvil ‘arārē’ eṉṟa iṭattil niliyākat taṅki kāḵpi, kaḷḷattuppākki, yāṉit tantam mutaliyavaṟṟi viṟpaṉi ceytatōṭu, nīkrō aṭimikaḷiyum pireñcu vaṇikarkaḷukku viṟpaṉi ceytāṉ. iṅku vāḻnta camayattil ivaṉ tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭatākavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

1891-ām āṇṭil muḻaṅkālil puṟṟunōy ēṟpaṭṭu, ataṟku maruttuvam ceytu koḷvataṟkāka pirāṉcu tirumpiṉāṉ. mārcēls maruttuvamaṉiyoṉṟil kāl tuṇṭikkappaṭṭu iṟantāṉ. appōtu-avaṉukku vayatu muppattēḻu. taṅkaḷiṭiyē uṟavu muṟinta niliyilum, verlēṉ rempōviṉuṭiya kavitikaḷit tiraṭṭi oḻuṅku ceytār. rempō iṟappataṟku ōrāṇṭukkumuṉ, verlēṉ arārēvukkuk kaṭitam eḻuti, ētāvatu putiya kavitikaḷ eḻutiyiruntāl aṉuppivikkumpaṭi rempōvik kēṭṭuk koṇṭār. ataṟku rempō, antak kuppiyi nāṉ tīṇṭuvatu kūṭa illi’ eṉṟu patil eḻutiyiruntāṉ. rempō iṟantapiṟakē avaṉ kavitikaḷ muṟiyākat tokuttu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṉ vāḻkiyip paṟṟiyum, kavitip paṇipaṟṟiyum pala kaṟpaṉikaḷum kaṭṭuk katikaḷum paravalāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

oru muṟi pireñcu nāṭṭiṉ peruṅ kaviñarum nāvalāciriyarumāṉa viktar hyūkō rempōvik ‘kuḻanti ṣēkspiyar’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭatāka avaṉiṭam kūṟiya poḻutu ‘eṉakku iṇiyāka yāriyum oppiṭṭuc colla muṭiyātu’ eṉṟu perumitamākac coṉṉāṉām.

rempō taṉatu iṟappukkup piṟaku, taṉatu kavitikaḷōṭu niṟiyak kaṭitaṅkaḷiyum kuṟippukkaḷiyum viṭṭuc ceṉṟirukkiṟāṉ. eṉṟālum mēli nāṭṭu ilakkiyavātikaḷukku ivaṉ periya putirākavē viḷaṅkukiṟāṉ. rempōviṉ cārlivil nakaril, avvūr makkaḷ avaṉukkuc ciliyeṭuttuc ciṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉar.

rempōvukkuc caṟṟu muṉpu vāḻnta kaviñar pōtalēr, ivaṉukku vāḻkkit tuṟiyilum, ilakkiyat tuṟiyilum muṉṉōṭi eṉṟu collalām. vāḻkkiyik kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟi viruppampōl cuvittuc ‘ciṟappuk keṭṭavar’ eṉṟa peyarit tāmē ēṟṟuk koṇṭa pireñcu aruṇakiri pōtalēr. rempō avari appaṭiyē piṉpaṟṟiṉār. tāyiṉ irakkamaṟṟa aṭakkumuṟiyum, kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa iḷamiyum, piṭivātamum pōkkirittaṉamum pāluṟavu vakkiramum rempōviṉ paṭippukkaḷil piratipalikkak kāṇalām.

pōtalēriṉ mānakarak koḷkiyiyum rempō appaṭiyē ēṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ. taṉatu naṇpar verlēṉuṭaṉ ilaṇṭaṉ, pirassals, pāricu nakara vītikaḷil cuṟṟittirintāṉ. cākkaṭi yōrattilum, matuppurikaḷilum vipaccāra viṭutikaḷilum iravik kaḻittāṉ. mānakaraṅkaḷiṉ iruṭṭu vāḻkki avaṉukkup paḻakippōṉa oṉṟu. nīlavāṉilum, kuḷir cōliyilum, aruviyilum, cilirkkum teṉṟalilum, cirikkum pūkkaḷilum potuvākak kaviñarkaḷ uḷḷattip paṟikoṭuttuk kavitikaḷ eḻutuvatu vaḻakkam. āṉāl maṉita vāḻviṉ uyirttuṭippi nakara vāḻkkiyiṉ avalaṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvil rempō kaṇṭāṉ. nakariṉ aḻukal avaṉukkuk kaviti nākarikamākiviṭṭatu. nakaraṅkaḷiṉ iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa naṭimuṟi, amitiyiṉmi, cuṟucuṟuppāṉa iravu vāḻkki, cērikaḷiṉ iḻinta nili, toḻiṟ cālikaḷiṉ ōci, puki paṭinta cuṟṟuc cūḻal yāvum avaṉ kavitiyiṉ karupporuḷkaḷāka iṭam peṟṟaṉa.

piḷḷip paruvattilum viṭalip paruvattilum tāyiṉ aṭakku muṟikku āṭpaṭṭu varuntiya pōtu avaṉiyum aṟiyāmal avaṉ aṭimaṉattil peṇkaḷiṉ mītu oru veṟuppuṇarcci paṭintiruntatu. mutaṉ mutalil vīṭṭi viṭṭu ōṭiyapōtu peṇmiyip paṟṟi oru viyappārvamum, iṉampuriyāta kuṟukuṟuppum avaṉuḷḷattil kuṭi koṇṭiruntaṉa. peṇkaḷ kūṭṭattil nuḻiyum pōtu puṟaccamaya ulakattil tēvatikaḷukku oppāka nuḻiyum vīraṉākat taṉṉik kaṟpaṉi ceytu koṇṭāṉ. āṉāl appōtu avaṉ ārvattōṭu cantitta peṇkaḷ muraṇṭu piṭippavarkaḷākavē avaṉukku vāyttaṉar. avarkaḷ cantippāl ēṟpaṭṭa aṉupavaṅkaḷic cūriyaṉum taciyum (Sun and Flesh) kēli (The Tease) pacci naṭaṉac cāliyil (At the Green Cabaret) aliccal (Wandering) niṉā eṉṉa patiliṟuttāḷ (What Nina Answered) ākiya kavitikaḷil pativu ceykiṟāṉ.

iraṇṭāvatu muṟi vīṭṭi viṭṭup pāricukku ōṭiyapōtu pireñcup puraṭci muṭintu kamyūṉkaḷiṉ āṭci aṅkē naṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭiruntatu. avvāṭciyāl pāricil ēṟpaṭṭa kuḻappamum, koliyum, paṭṭiṉiyum rempōviṉ uḷḷattil oru vaṟaṭciyiyum nampikkiyiṉmiyiyum tōṟṟuvittaṉa. makkaḷāṭciyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tōṉṟum enta amippiyum avaṉ veṟuttāṉ. taṉimiyum avanampikkiyum avaṉuḷḷattil oru vaki arājakattattuvattit tōṟṟuvittaṉa. pāricu nakarameṅkum cāviyum kaṇṇīriyumē cantittāṉ. vaḻiyil iṟantu kiṭanta oru paṭivīraṉiṉ piṇattip pārttu uḷḷam kacintatu. camaveḷiyil tūṅkupavaṉ (The Sleeper and The Valley) eṉṟa pāṭali eḻutiṉāṉ.

1871-il mūṉṟām muṟi vīṭṭi viṭṭu ōṭiyapōtu mikiyāṉa kātaṟ kaḷiyāṭṭattil mūḻkiyatāl peṇmiyiṉ mītu calippum viraktiyum avaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭatu. kāttiruppavarkaḷ (Sitters) eṉ ciṟiya kātalikaḷ (My Little Loveiies) aṉat yō meṉ rati (Wenus Anadyomene) eṉṟa kavitikaḷil peṇkaḷ mītu koṇṭa veṟuppip pativu ceyvatōṭu, avarkaḷ pōlittaṉattiyum, cākacattiyum tōlurittuk kāṭṭukiṟāṉ. kātalukkup putup poruḷ kaṇṭaṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟāṉ. appōtutāṉ verlēṉiṉ putiya uṟavu avaṉukku ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.

mutiriḷamip paruvattil ēṟpaṭak kūṭiya muḻumi peṟāta pāluṇarvu viruppaṅkaḷum accaṅkaḷum mutaṉmutalāka rempōviṉ kavitikaḷil mika nuṇukkamāka ilakkiya vaṭivam peṟṟaṉa. oru pāṭal:

avaṉ

taṉatu ēḻāvatu vayatil

pālikaḷilum

vaṉāntaraṅkaḷilum

iḷamarak kāṭukaḷilum

kaṭaṟkarikaḷilum

cutantaramākac cuṟṟittiriyum

uyirkaḷippaṟṟiya

iṉpakkaṟpaṉikaḷil īṭupaṭṭuk

kavitikaḷ vaṭittāṉ.



vaṇṇappaṭa vāra itaḻkaḷ

avaṉ kaṟpaṉiyit

tuṇṭiviṭṭaṉa.

avaṟṟuḷ veḷiyākiyirunta

speyiṉ ittāliyap

peṇkaḷiṉ aṇivakuppu

avaṉi-

nāṇap paṭuttiyatu.





cīṭṭittuṇi āṭiyaṇintu

aṭuttavīṭṭu

eṭṭu vayatu muraṭṭucciṟumi

ōṭivantu-

avaṉ mītu tāvi viḻuntāḷ

avaṉi-

ōraṅkaṭṭiṉāḷ,

avaṉmītu

cavāri ceytāḷ,

toṅkum kūntali

acittuc cirittāḷ,



avaḷaṭiyil

akappaṭṭuk koṇṭaavaṉ





[ēḻuvayatuk kaviñaṉ]

‘ciṟappuk keṭṭavar’ eṉṟu peyareṭuttālum kiṟittavarāṉa pōtalēr, taṉ camyattiyō, āṉmāviyō, muktip peruvāḻviyō tiyākamceyyat tayārāka illi, āṉāl, ‘rempō taṉimaṉita āḷumi, āṉmā, muktiyāvum kālattiṟ kovvāta taṟperumikaḷ’ eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ. oru piṭivātamāṉa uṇarvōṭum, tuṟavu maṉappāṉmiyōṭum leḷakīka āṉmīkap perumikaḷi avaṉ veṟuttotukkiṉāṉ. kaṭici mūccu irukkum vari maṉita nērmikku matippuk koṭukkāta, kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa vāḻkkiyē vāḻntāṉ.

eṉṟālum, ‘ēmāṟṟappaṭṭa itayam’ (The Cheated Heart) eṉṟa kavitiyil ‘eṉatu mīṭcikku eṉṉa vaḻi?’ eṉṟa pāviyiṉ viṉāvi avaṉē eḻuppik koḷkiṟāṉ. kiṟittava camayatti ēṟṟuk koḷḷāta avaṉukkuc camayattīrvu eppaṭik kiṭikkum? eṉavē avaṉ, tāṉē taṉṉiyoru ñāṉiyākkik koṇṭu, taṉatu pāva vimōcaṉattiṟkāṉa vaḻiyi 15-5-1871-il taṉatu naṇpaṉ pālṭemṉikku eḻutiya kaṭitattil kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ:

“oru kaviñaṉāka māṟa virumpum maṉitaṉ taṉṉi muḻumiyāka āyntu aṟikiṟāṉ; taṉ āṉmāvit tēṭi atik kaṇkāṇikkiṟāṉ; piṉṉar cōtittup pārkkiṟāṉ; kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟāṉ. āṉmāvi uṇarntu koṇṭapiṉ atip paṇpaṭuttukiṟāṉ”

itutāṉ rempō taṉ mīṭcikkum muktikkum vakuttuk koṇṭatiṭṭam, eṉṟālum pirassals maruttuvamaṉiyil cāvukkākap pōrāṭik koṇṭiruntapōtu kattōlikka camayatti ēṟṟuk koṇṭu pāva maṉṉippu peṟukiṟāṉ.

rempōviṉ kavitikaḷ ‘narakattil oru paruvam, [3](A Season In Hell) veḷicaṅkaḷ (Illuminations) eṉṟa iraṇṭu tokutikaḷāka veḷivantirukkiṉṟaṉa. narakattil oru paruvattit ‘taṉ paṭippukkaḷiṉ veṟṟi’ eṉṟu taṉ naṇpaṉ pāl ṭerṉikku eḻutiya kaṭitattil avaṉ kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. 1873 ām āṇṭuk kōṭiyil innūl eḻutappaṭṭu, 1875 ām āṇṭil avaṉuṭiya contac celavil patippikkappaṭṭatu. innūli accil kāṇa avaṉ mikavum virumpiṉāṉ. kavitit toḻilik kiviṭṭuviṭṭup piḻippukkāka mattiyak kiḻakku nāṭukaḷil avaṉ cuṟṟit tirinta pōtu, avaṉuṭiya naṇparkaḷum pāricu nakara ilakkiyavātikaḷum kūṭi 1868-il ‘veḷiccaṅkaḷ’ eṉṟa avaṉatu iraṇṭām kavitit tokutiyi veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. appōtu rempōviṉ naṭamāṭṭattip paṟṟi aṟiyāta kāraṇattāl avaṉ iṟantuviṭṭatākavē ellārum niṉittaṉar.

rempōviṉ iraṇṭu kavitit tokutikaḷil etu mutalil eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉṟa carcci pireñcu ilakkiya vātikaḷiṭiyē nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷāka iruntu koṇṭirukkiṟatu. veḷiccaṅkaḷil cila kavitikaḷi eḻutiya piṟaku, naṭuvil narakattil oru paruvattit toṭaṅki muṭittuviṭṭu, mīti veḷiccaṅkaḷip piṟaku eḻutiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu potuvākak karuta iṭamirukkiṟatu.

‘narakattil oruparuvam’ eṉṟa kavitit tokuti uḷaviyal pūrvamāṉa avaṉuṭiya taṉvaralāṟu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭalām. piḷḷip pirāyattilum, paḷḷipparuvattilum, aṭakku muṟiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa uṇarccik kontaḷippum, taṉimiyilum verlēṉuṭaṉum nakaraṅkaḷil poṟuppiṉṟic cuṟṟittirinta kālattil ēṟpaṭṭa kacappāṉa aṉupavaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ pātippāl aṭimaṉattil ēṟpaṭṭa atirvukaḷum mikanuṭpamāka ippāṭalkaḷil cittarikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. patiṉāṉku vayatuc ciṟuvaṉiṉ mutirntum mutirāta viṭalik kaṉavukaḷum, etirkālattil veṭikkavirukkum avaṉatu pūkampak kavitiyāṟṟalum, kaṭṭukkaḷi aṟuttuk koṇṭu pāyattuṭikkum avaṉ cutantira veṟiyum ikkavitit tokutiyil pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittokutikku rempō cūṭṭiya peyar ‘katikaḷ’ (Stories)

innūliṉ pāyirattil cittāṉiṭattil avaṉ pēcuvatāka oru pakuti irukkiṟatu. atil, tāṉ teyvīkak kātalik kaṇṭaṟiyavum, aḻaki ārātikkavum inta nūli eḻutuvatākak kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. appaṇikaḷic ciṟanta muṟiyil ceytu muṭikka narakattukkup payaṇam mēṟkoṇṭu, taṉ paritāpa muṭivit tāṉē tēṭik koṇṭa ōr āṉmāvic cantittu, ataṉ uḷḷat tiruṭṭiyum, valippup pitaṟṟalkaḷiyum aṟintu koḷḷa virumpuvatākak kūṟukiṟāṉ. antap paritāpa āṉmā vēṟu yārumilli; ivaṉētāṉ, ippayaṇam iṟappiṟkup piṟaku mēṟkoḷvatu pōṉṟa oru kaṟpaṉi.

‘valippup pitaṟṟal’ eṉṟu avaṉ kuṟippiṭuvatu iravum pakalum avaṉ uḷḷatti ōyāmal varuttik koṇṭirukkum iru kuṟṟaṅkaḷ, oṉṟu verlēṉuṭaṉ koṇṭirunta takāta uṟavu; maṟṟoṉṟu 'col racavātam* (Verbal Alchemy) paṟṟiyatu. it tokutiyil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa kavitikaḷil, cīrāṉa cantamō, etuki mōṉiyō illi. etirpārāmal miṉṉum paṭimaṅkaḷi variciyāka aṭukkik kaviti viḷivukaḷi rempō tōṟṟuvittirukkiṟāṉ.

innūliṉ iṟutiyil uḷḷa' ‘kāli’ (Morning), pirivu (Farewell), ākiya pāṭalkaḷil uḷḷattiṟku itamāṉa ōr iṉiya viṭiyali avaṉ varavēṟṟup pāṭukiṟāṉ. “ivvaḷavu nāṭkaḷ eṉṉiṭam ‘nāṉ’ eṉpatu vēṟu ōr poruḷāka iruntatu. anta eṇṇattik kaḷintu viṭṭu, ‘nāṉ’ eṉṟa eṉ āṉmāviyum eṉ uṭalil ataṉ iruppiyum uṇarntu koṇṭēṉ. ituvē nāṉ kaṇṭaṟinta aḻakuk kaṉavu; ituvē eṉ veṟṟi” eṉṟu uṇarcci vacappaṭṭup pāṭukiṟāṉ.

maṟṟoru tokuppāṉa ‘veḷiccaṅkaḷi’ verlēṉ vaṇṇattakaṭukaḷ (Coloured Plates) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār rempōviṉ vāḻkkiyil ēṟpaṭṭa tuṉpaṅkaḷukkum kuḻappaṅkaḷukkum, vakkiraṅkaḷukkum tappi 'veḷiccaṅkaḷ ilakkiyavātikaḷiṉ kikaḷil iṉṟum oḷikuṉṟāmal iruppataṟku avaṟṟiṉ teḷivum, poruḷamitiyum, puriyumpaṭiyāṉa maṟiporuḷ uttiyum kāraṇaṅkaḷākum. ittokuppil uḷḷa kavitikaḷiṉ talippukaḷ orē collāka amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉavi paṉmic coṟkaḷ ittalippukaḷ mikavum kavarcciyāṉavi, kavitiyiṉ ovvoru variyum paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ karuvūlam; avi kuṟippiṭum iṭaṅkaḷum mayaṅkavikkum poyt tōṟṟaṅkaḷ.

Verbal alchemy: I flattered myself that I have invented a poetic language accessible, some day or other to at; the senses I adopted the most absurd and exaggerated more of expression conceivable...That is all past. I now know how to salute beauty-Rimbud.

ittokuppil uḷḷa ellāk kavitikaḷumē ciṟantavi eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. āṉāl kaṭici mūṉṟu veḷiccaṅkaḷum mikavum oḷimikkavi. avaṟṟuḷ mēti (Genie) eṉṟa talippil kūṟappaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ, rempōviṉ paṭippukkaḷil ātikkam celuttum kātal uṇarviṉ tiṟavukōlāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

“eṉṉāl kātalic cāḷarattukku veḷiyē tūkki eṟiya muṭiyātu” eṉṟum “nāṉ kātaliciyiṉ mūlak kuṟippik kaṇṭaṟinta kaliñaṉ” eṉṟum rempō perumitamāka veḷiccaṅkaḷil ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ.

rempō tāṉ kaṇṭaṟintatākak kuṟippiṭum ‘kātaliciyiṉ mūlakkuṟippu’[4] etuvāka irukka muṭiyum eṉṟu tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ mūḷiyik kuḻappik koṇṭaṉar. vāḻkki muḻuvatum nāttikaṉāka vāḻnta rempō, taṉ ceyta kuṟṟaṅkaḷiliruntu mīṭcipeṟa oru vaḻikāṭṭiyit tēṭiṉāṉ. anta vaḻikāṭṭiyit tēvaṉ eṉṟō, tēvamakaṉ eṉṟō, meciyyā eṉṟō, napi eṉṟō kuṟippiṭavilli. aṉpiṉ vaṭivamāka avaṉik kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ:

"avaṉ nammi aṟivāṉ; avaṉ nam ellāriyum virumpukiṟāṉ. intak kuḷirkāla iravil, nilattiṉ oru kōṭiyiliruntu maṟṟoru kōṭivari, pūmiyiṉ kontaḷikkum muṉikaḷiliruntu kōṭṭi kottaḷam vari, nericalāṉa kuṭiyiruppiliruntu amitiyāṉa kaṭaṟkari vari, pārvikku eṭṭiya tūram vari, valimikkum vāṭṭattiṟkumiṭiyil kūṭiyiruntu avaṉit taricikkavum, avaṉi mīṇṭum vaḻiyaṉuppavum vēṇṭum. alikaḷiṉ naṭuvilum, paranta paṉippāliyiṉ ucciyilum avaṉ uṭaliyum, uyir mūcciyum, avaṉ ñāṉap pēroḷiyiyum piṉ paṟṟic cella vēṇṭum!"</poem>

perumpālāṉa kaviñarkaḷ kaṟpaṉiyulakil cañcarittup paḻakkappaṭṭavarkaḷ. naṭimuṟi ulakiṉ veḷippaṭiyāṉa uṇmikaḷ mikavum kacappāṉavi. avaṟṟik kaviñarkaḷ ēṟṟuk koḷḷattayaṅkuvatōṭu, avaṟṟiliruntu tappiyōṭavum virumpukiṉṟaṉar. naṭimuṟi ulakiliruntu tappic cella, mutalil tammi maṟakka vēṇṭum. tammi maṟakkak kaviñarkaḷ matu, apiṉ, kañcā pōṉṟa paḻakkaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. appaḻakkaṅkaḷ uḷakkaṭṭuppāṭṭittaḷartti, ati ataṉ viruppappaṭi celuttukiṉṟaṉa. uḷḷam virumpum kaṟpaṉi ulakamoṉṟit tāmē paṭittuk koṇṭu, ivvulakat tuṉpaṅkaḷi maṟantu tam viruppam pōl kaviñarkaḷ atil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. iti ‘maruḷmayakka nili’ eṉpar. pōtalērip pōlavē rempōvum oru kaṟpaṉic corkkattip paṭittuk koṇṭu, atil payaṇam ceykiṟāṉ.

rempōviṉ paṭippukkaḷil kuṭipōtip paṭaku[5] (The Drunken Boat) eṉṟa kaviti mikavum viḷamparam peṟṟtu. itu oru kuṟiyīṭṭuk kaviti. iḷamiyil tāyiṉ kaṇṭippālum aṭakku muṟiyālum ēṟpaṭṭa kumuṟalkaḷum, verlēṉ uṟavāl ēṟpaṭṭa maṉavikāraṅkaḷum, nakarattiṉ iruṇṭa vāḻkkiyil ēṟpaṭṭa tākkaṅkaḷum, vaṉmuṟiyum āravāramum mikka peruṅkaṭalāka ikkavitiyil uruvakappaṭuttappaṭukiṉaṟaṉa. akkaṭalil mitakkum oru tuṭuppillāta paṭaku rempōvukkuk kuṟiyīṭāka varukiṟatu. kaṭalil takkipōl taḷḷāṭi varum ippaṭaku perumpuyalōṭum, kuṉṟippōl kontaḷikkum aliyōṭum paṉippāṟiyōṭum, timiṅkilam kaṭaṟpāmpu pōṉṟa koṭiya kaṭal uyirikaḷōṭum pōrāṭi muṉṉēṟukiṟatu. iṉamiyil kiṭikkāta cutantiram intakkaṟpaṉip payaṇattil rempōvukkuk kiṭikkiṟatu. vīṭṭiviṭṭu mūṉṟu muṟi ōṭiyapōtu, avaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭa maṉakkōḷāṟāṉa makiḻcciyiyum ippayaṇam paṭippavarkku uṇarttukiṟatu. ‘kuṭipōtippaṭaku’ eḻutappaṭṭapōtu rempō kaṭaliyē pārttatilli eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

pōtalēr kuṟiyīṭṭiyattiṉ (Symbolism) muṉṉōṭi, verlēṉ attuṟiyil cātaṉikaḷ nikaḻttiyavar. kirampō avariyum tāṇṭic ceṉṟavaṉ.

cempō mutalil kavitiyi marapil eḻutiṉāṉ; iṭiyil kaṭṭilāk kavitikku vantāṉ; piṉṉar vacaṉattiyē kavitiyākkiṉāṉ. ‘pireñcuk kavitiyaṉittum etuki mōṉiyaminta urinaṭi’ eṉṟu ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭṭāṉ.

camayam, camutāyam, araciyal ākiyavaṟṟi avaṉ eppaṭi etirttup puraṭciceytāṉō atēpōl kaviti naṭiyiyum etirttup puraṭci ceytāṉ. kaviticcoṟkaḷiṉ iyalpāṉa poruḷiyē māṟṟi, coṟkaḷiṉ taṉṉiyakkam, colmayakkam, colviḷiyāṭṭu, ōci vēṟupāṭu ivaṟṟiṉmūlam moḻiyiṉ toṭakkanilippaṇpukaḷikkiḷappip paṭippavar uḷḷattil palvēṟu uṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷit tōṟṟuvikka muyaṉṟāṉ. tāṉ veḷippaṭuttum ovvōr uṇarccikkum oru putiya kaviti moḻiyi uruvākka muṭiyum eṉṟu kaṉavu kaṇṭāṉ. ataṟkāka muyaṟciyum ceytāṉ. ‘kaṭaṟ pakkam’ eṉṟa kavitiyil taṉ collāṟṟalāl nilapparappi nīrpparappākavum, nīrpparappi nilapparappākavum māṟṟik kāṇpikkiṟāṉ. intac ‘colracavāta’ vittiyit toṭaṅkip piṉṉar kiviṭṭu viṭṭāṉ.

“kaviñaṉukkuk kaṭṭuppāṭu kūṭātu, kaviti kaviñaṉuḷḷattil tāṉākak karuk koṇṭu, kaṟpaṉi vaṭivam peṟṟup pakkuvamaṭintu poṅki varavēṇṭum. kaviñaṉ taṉatu uḷḷattil karuttukkaḷ malarntu varutalik kaṇṭu cuvittu, ati appaṭiyē aṉumatikka vēṇṭum” eṉṟu taṉatu pukaḻ peṟṟa kaṭitattil kuṟippiṭukiṉṟāṉ rempō. ikkoḷki avaṉic carriyalicattukku iṭṭuc celkiṟatu, carriyalicattiṉ ūṟṟuk kaṇ rempō.

‘tīrkka taricaṉam’ vāykkap peṟṟavaṉ kaviñaṉ eṉṟār pōtalēr. tīrkkataricitāṉ (Seer) kaviñaṉ eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ rempō, cātāraṇa makkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷukkup pulappaṭāta akak kāṭcikaḷ tīrkkataricikaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷukkup pulappaṭum. inta akak kāṭci iṉpaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭṭut tammi maṟantiruppataṟkākavē cilar ceyaṟki muṟiyil maruḷ mayakka niliyi nāṭuvatuṇṭu. pōtalēr appaṭi maruḷ mayakka niliyil āḻntiruntu akak kaṭcikaḷi atītamākak kaṇṭavar,

pōtalēriṉ tīrkkataricaṉaṅkaḷi ēṟṟukkoṇṭa rempō avaruṭiya kavitiyiṉ vaṭivatti ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilli. atippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu, “āviyulakik kūviyaḻittuk kāṇa muṭiyātavaṟṟik kaṇṭu, kēṭka muṭiyātavaṟṟik kēṭṭavarpōtalēr. avar oru tīrkkatarici! kaviyaracar! uṇmiyāṉa kaṭavuḷ! āṉāl turatiruṣṭa vacamāka avar kali nuṭpattilēyē karuttāka iruntār; avaruṭiya ilakkiyam ciṟappāṉateṉṟu pukaḻappaṭṭālum, ataṉ vaṭivam mikac cātāraṇamāṉatu. karuttil putumi ceyyumpōtu vaṭivattilum putumi ceyya vēṇṭum” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟāṉ rempō.

rempō kaviti ilakkaṇattiyum, vaṭivattiyum otukkit taḷḷiyatu pōla, coṟkaḷiṉ akarātip poruḷiyum otukkiviṭṭu avaṉuṭiya mutircci peṟṟa vacaṉa kavitikaḷi eḻutiṉāṉ. avaṟṟil avaṉ kaṇṭa akakkāṭcikaḷi maṭṭum varicip paṭuttiṉāṉ. ikkāṭcikaḷi iṇikkum coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kūṭa nīkki viṭṭāṉ. eṉṟālum avvacaṉaṅkaḷ kaviti niliyiliruntu tāḻntu pōkātataṟkuk kāraṇam, atil kūṟappaṭum akakkāṭcikaḷukku ēṟṟa puṟakkāṭcip paṭimaṅkaḷik kalinuṭpattōṭu rempō kiyāṇṭatutāṉ

rempōviṉ akak kāṭcikaḷ palatarappaṭṭa viparīta vaṭivaṅkaḷōṭu kuṭipōtip paṭakil atīta kaṟpaṉiyāka uruvakikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉmāviṉ uḷḷuṇarvuk kāṭcikaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ kūṭap paṭima uttiyāl viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṉuṭiya iḷamikkāla niṉivukaḷ avaṉ uḷḷattit taṭṭum pōtu kavitikaḷ paṭippāṟṟaliṉ uccattiyē toṭukiṉṟaṉa.

nāṉ

ōyāmal aḻutēṉ.

uṇmi!

inta viṭiyalkaḷ eṉṉi

vētaṉip paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.



ovvoru nilavum

eṉakkut tuṉpam;

ovvoru cūriyaṉum

eṉakkuk kacappu.



kaṭu kaṭuppāṉa aṉpu

eṉṉik

koñcaṅ koñcamāka

viḻuṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu.



eṉ muṭṭukkaḷ

muṟiyaṭṭum;

eṉ kappal

kaviḻantu pōkaṭṭum.



nāṉ kariyēṟuvatu

ōr

airōppiyak kaṭaṟkariyāka

irukkaṭṭum,



aṅkē

kariya kuḷattil

māli maṇattil

oru kuḻanti

mēmātap

paṭṭup pūcciyip pōl

melliya paṭakuviṭṭut

taṉ kavalikaḷik

kuttuk kāliṭṭuk

kaḻippatik kāṇavēṇṭum





[kuṭipōtippaṭaku]

mika iḷam vayatil aparimitamāṉa kaviti āṟṟal peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya rempō, taṉ pattoṉpatām vayatil kaviti eḻutuvati ēṉ niṟutti viṭṭāṉ eṉpatu ellārukkum periya putir. avaṉ cantarppa cūḻniliyālō, etir pārāmalō kaviñaṉ āṉavaṉ allaṉ; ilakkiyat tuṟiyil periya cātaṉikaḷic ceytu muṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa kuṟikkōḷōṭu kaviñaṉ āṉavaṉ; ataṟkākak kaṭumiyāka uḻittavaṉ; kali vāḻkkikkum naṭimuṟi vāḻkkikkum vēṟupāṭu illi eṉṟu karutit taṉ kavitip paṇiyit toṭaṅkiyavaṉ. āṉāl uṇmi vēṟu vitamāka iruntatu.

taṉ kaviti nempukōlāl ulakiyē puraṭṭi viṭalām eṉṟu puṟappaṭṭa rempōvukku naṭimuṟi vāḻkkiyil ēṟpaṭṭa cikkalkaḷum, ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷum tāṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyātapaṭi iruntaṉa. ataṉāl tāṉ taṉatu ‘veḷiccaṅkaḷik’ kaṭici viṟpaṉi (Clearance Sale) eṉṟa kavitiyōṭu muṭittuk koḷkiṟāṉ. taṉ kaṭici ilakkiya carakki viṟṟuviṭṭuk kaṭiyiyum mūṭiviṭṭāṉ. kaṭi virittēṉ; koḷvārilli! eṉṟu vaṭalūr vaḷḷal varuntik kūṟiyatu ivaṉukkum poruntum.





↑ Pastoral theme

↑ kirēkkap purāṇattil varum ārḵpis, āṇṭavaṉ appōlōviṉ aruḷ peṟṟa pāṭakaṉ taṉ iciyāl ellā uyiriṉaṅkaḷiyum mayakka valla āṟṟal peṟṟavaṉ. avaṉ maṉivi yūriṭis iṟanta pōtu avaḷi mīḷap peṟac cāvulakam celkiṟāṉ. cāvulakiṉ atitēvati hēṭs. avaṉuṭiya yāḻiciyil mayaṅki, avaṉ maṉiviyi oru nipantaṉiyiṉ pēril aḻittuc cella aṉumatikkiṟāṉ. iruḷ cūḻnta cāvulaki viṭṭu iruvarum veḷiyē ceṉṟa piṟakutāṉ ārḵpis taṉ maṉiviyit tirumpip pārkka vēṇṭum eṉpatē nipantaṉi. ārḵpis cāvulakiviṭṭu veḷiyil vantatum oḷi avaṉ mītu viḻuntatu: uṭaṉē avaṉ tirumpip pārttāṉ. appōtu yūriṭis cāvulakiṉ ellikkuḷ iruntāṉ. hēṭsiṉ nipantaṉiyi mīṟiya kāraṇattāl, ārḵpis taṉ maṉiviyi mīṇṭum iḻantāṉ.

↑ pireñcil 'uyṉ cecōm āāmpēr’ eṉṟu uccarikkavēṇṭum.

↑ the key-signature of love

↑ pirañcup peyar Bateau lvre.

﻿ ici-

ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mūccu;

ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ pēccu.





reyṉar mēriyā rilk

(1875–1926)



pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyilum pālkaṉ nāṭukaḷil piṟanta oruvaṉ taṉṉi enta nāṭṭukkāraṉ eṉṟu collik koḷḷa muṭiyātapaṭi, aṅkē paṭiyeṭuppukkaḷum, virinta āṭci māṟṟaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉa. kaviñar reyṉar mēriyā rilk pirākuvil piṟantavar; pokīmiya iṉattic cārntavar; āstiriyak kuṭimakaṉ; jermaṉkavi.

tanti ōyvu peṟṟa āstiriya irāṇuva atikāri; peṇ kuḻanti vēṇṭum eṉṟu viruppattōṭu kāttirunta tāykku, rilk āṇākap piṟantatu perutta ēmāṟṟam: ēṉavē rilkki oru peṇṇip pōla vaḷarttāḷ ‘reṉi’ eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭi. peṇṇuṭiyum curuḷ muṭiyum aṇivittu, pommic camiyalaṟiyoṉṟu viḷiyāṭak koṭuttu vaḷarttāḷ. poruḷkaḷiyum irukkikaḷiyum tuṭittu tūymiyāka vittiruppatu eppaṭiyeṉṟum, vīṭṭu vēlikaḷil tāykku eppaṭi utavuvatu eṉṟum kaṟṟuk koṭuttāḷ.

aṭikkaṭi nōyvāyppaṭum avaratu iḷami irāṇuvap paḷḷiyilum, vaṇikak kallūriyilum kaḻintatu. pirāku palkalikkaḻakattilum iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ caṭṭak kalvi payiṉṟār. koñcanāḷ vaḻakkuriñarāka irunta taṉ uṟaviṉariṭam paṇipurintār. kaṭiciyil ilakkiyap paṇiyē taṉakku ēṟṟa paṇiyeṉṟu muṭivu ceytār.



peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ maṇavilakkum, irāṇuvappaḷḷi aṉupavaṅkaḷum āvari āḻnta tuyarattil āḻttiṉa. tamatu paṭukkiyil amarntu cāvi vēṇṭi iṟivaṉiṭam avar maṉṟāṭiya nēraṅkaḷ uṇṭu.

1899-1900 ām āṇṭukaḷil rilk lō āṇṭriyal calōmi eṉṟa peṇṇuṭaṉ ruciyappayaṇam mēṟkoṇṭār. aṅkē eḻuttāḷarum tattuvañāṉiyumāṉa liyō ṭālsṭāyic cantittār. piṉṉar perliṉ tirumpiyatum ruciya nāṭṭu varalāṟṟiyum, annāṭṭiṉ kali ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum virumpip payiṉṟār. rilk, pirāṉs, skāṇṭi nēviyā, ittāli, speyiṉ, vaṭāprikkā ākiya nāṭukaḷil cuṟṟup payaṇam mēṟkoṇṭālum, ruciyappayaṇam avar uḷḷattil periya tākkatti ēṟpaṭuttiyatu. avaruṭiya etirkāla vaḷarccikku oru tūṇṭukōlākavum aṭippaṭiyākavum viḷaṅkiyatu; tāṉ taricitta mutal puṉita pūmiyāka atik karutiṉār. ruciyap payaṇattiṉ viḷivākat tōṉṟiya kavitikaḷil ruciya nāṭṭit taṉatu āṉmīkap peruveḷiyāka aticayaṅkaḷ mikka kaṟpaṉi ulakamāka-varuṇikkiṟār. 1900-il avar eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭa ‘kaṭavuḷiṉ katikaḷ’ (Stories of god) eṉṟa nūl avar uḷḷattil āṭci ceyta kaṟpaṉi ruciyāvi mika uyarvāka uruvakappaṭuttukiṟatu.

1902-il pukaḻpeṟṟa pireñcuc ciṟpiyāṉa rōṭiṉiṉ ceyalāḷarākac cērntu iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ paṇipurintār. piṉṉar pāricil pattāṇṭukaḷ taṅkiyiruntu tam ilakkiyap paṇiyit toṭarntār. 1915-ām āṇṭil irāṇuvap paṇikkāka aḻikkappaṭṭu viyaṉṉā ceṉṟār. irāṇuvattiṉ kaṭumiyāṉa naṭimuṟikaḷit tākkuppiṭikka muṭiyāmal nōy vāyppaṭṭār. avaruṭiya mōcamāṉa uṭal nili kāraṇamāka irāṇuvattil avarukku eḻuttar paṇi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. ōriraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷil appaṇiyiliruntum viṭutalipeṟṟu myūṉic ceṉṟu taṅkiṉār. tamatu kaṭici nāṭkaḷi sviṭcarlāntil kaḻittār.

ṣelliyum piraṉum kaviñarkaḷuḷ mikavum aḻakāṉavarkaḷ; ṭapiḷyū ec. āṭaṉ kaviñarkaḷuḷ mikavum aḻakaṟṟavar: aḷavaṟṟa kuṭiyāl cīrkulintu, tōl curuṅki, oṭṭuk kantalāka avar mukam kāṭciyaḷikkum. rilkkiṉ camakālattu eḻuttāḷar oruvar rilkkiṉ tōṟṟattippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu, 'aṭikkaṭi mīciyic cuṇṭum, cuṇṭeli pōl kāṭciyaḷikkiṟār, eṉṟu vēṭikkiyākak kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

mīciyōṭum, iṉiya uṟutiyāṉa mukattōṭum, mevitta tāṭiyōṭum etirppaṭṭatik kirakikkum oḷi poruntiya kūrtta viḻikaḷōṭum, melliya nīḷamāṉa aḻakiya viralkaḷōṭum rilk paṭattil kāṭciyaḷikkiṟār. avar cirippi virumpātavar; jermāṉiyak kaṇṭippōṭu kūṭiya meyviḷakka vāti; paṭitta iḷiñarkaḷōṭum paṇpaṭṭa iḷaṅ kaliñarkaḷōṭum nīṇṭanāḷ kalanturiyāṭik kaviñaṉiṉ kuṟikkōḷkaḷik kaṟṟuṇarntavar.

ṣelli pireñcup puraṭci peṟṟeṭutta kuḻanti; rilk, taṉṉittāṉē aḻittuk koḷḷappuṟappaṭṭa airōppiyap pōr veṟiyiṉ etirppuṇarcci peṟṟeṭutta kuḻanti.

ciṟpiyiliruntu cīmāṭṭivari rilkkiṉ vāḻkkiyil pala peṇkaḷ kuṟikkiṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷiṭam rilk koṇṭa kātaluṟavum cuviyāṉatu; putirāṉatu. rilkkiṉ mutaṟ kātal 1897-il vēlari ṭēviṭ rōṉḵpīlṭ eṉṟa peṇṇiṭam tuvakkaviḻāk koṇṭatu. avaratu mutal kaviti nūlukku avaḷ ūkkac caktiyāka viḷaṅkiyatōṭu anta nūli veḷiyiṭum poruṭcelaviyum avaḷē ēṟṟāḷ. ‘valli’ eṉṟu taṉ kuṟippukaḷil vāñciyuṭaṉ kuṟippiṭukiṟār rilk. āṉāl avaḷ uṟavu kuṟukiya kālattil muṟintatu.

aṭuttu rilkkik kavarntavaḷ ‘lō calōmi’. avaḷ atirccitarum aḻakut tēvati. irupatu vayatil jermāṉiyat tattuvañāṉi nīṭcēyit taṉ aḻakiṉ muṉ maṇṭiyiṭac ceytavaḷ. rilkkic cantitta pōtu avaḷukku vayatu muppattintu; tirumaṇamāṉavaḷ. rilk appōtu irupattu mūṉṟu vayatuk kaṭṭiḷaṅkāḷi. eṉṟālum taṉatu aṉupavakkaraṅkaḷālum amuta moḻikaḷālum rilkki aravaṇittut tālāṭṭiṉāḷ. kaṇṭatum kātal! virivil avarkaḷ pirintālum, kātal toṭarpu aṟṟup pōkavilli; vāḻkkiyiṉ iṟutivari nīṭittatu. lō calōmi rilkkiṉ uḷḷattukku nerukkamāṉavaḷākavum uṇarccip parimāṟṟaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavaḷākavum, ātarca naṅkiyākavum eppōtum viḷaṅkiṉāḷ.

avar neruṅkip paḻakiya peṇkaḷuḷ ṭirisṭi nakarukkarukil irunta ṭiyūṉō kōṭṭi iḷavaraci ‘mērivāṉtāṉ’ kuṟippiṭattakkavaḷ. avaḷuṭiya aṉpukkum aravaṇippukkum kaṭṭuppaṭṭu āṇṭuk kaṇakkāka avaḷuṭiya kōṭṭiyil taṅkiyiruntār rilk. pukaḻpeṟṟa ‘ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpā’ (Duing Elegies) tōṉṟak kāraṇamāka irunta naṅkiyum ivaḷē

ruciyap payaṇam muṭintu jermaṉ tirumpiyatum. cilk ‘vorpsvīṭ’ eṉṟa ciṟṟūrukkuc ceṉṟār. aṅkē ‘kiḷērā vesṭhāḵp’ eṉṟa iḷañ ciṟpiyiccantittār. avaḷ pukaḻ peṟṟa ciṟpi rōṭiṉuṭiya māṇavi. iruvarum kātalittu 1901-il tirumaṇm ceytu koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷukku ‘rūt’ eṉṟu kuḻantiyum piṟantatu. kuṭumpu vāḻkki eṉṉum kaṭṭuttaṟiyil rilk niliyākat taṅkum paḻakkamilli. avar empōtum payaṇam ceytu koṇṭē iruppār. avar mēyccal tari periyatu.

kātalippaṟṟi rilk koṇṭirunta karuttu putumiyāṉatu. muḻumi peṟātatum, kimmāṟu karutātatum tāṉ uṉṉatamāṉa kātal eṉpatu rilkkiṉ karuttu. ‘oru peṇṇiṉ tōḷmītu kivitta aḷavilēyē, eṉṉuṭiya kātaliṉ muḻumiyāṉa uṇarcciyitteḷivāka avaḷukku uṇartta muṭiyum’ eṉṟu avar kuṟippiṭukiṟār. rilkkiṉ uṇmiyāṉa ārvalarkaḷ ellārum peṇkaḷē. avarkaḷ avari naṉku purintiruntārkaḷ; avariṭamiruntu avarkaḷ etiyum etirpārkkavilli. eṉṟālum avariṭam paḻakiyapōtu taṅkaḷ kātal niṟivēṟiya uḷḷa niṟivu avarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭatu.

puṉiviyak kaviñarāka arumpi, aḻakiyak kaviñarāka malarntu iruppiyak kaviñarāka maṇam parappi, patippiyak kaviñarākak kāyttu iṟutiyil tamatu conta uḷḷuṇarvukaḷukkum akak kāṭcikaḷukkum karuttu vaṭivam koṭukkum caṅkētak kaviñarākak kaṉintu viḷaṅkiṉār.

citaṟik kiṭakkum ciṟukavitikaḷ niṟiya eḻutik kuvittiruntālum, rilkkiṟku aḻiyāp pukaḻic cērkkum kavitit tokutikaḷāka mūṉṟik kuṟippiṭalām. avi ‘mālṭē lāriṭs pirikkiṉ kuṟippukaḷ’ (The Note books of Malte Lawrids Brigge) ‘ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpākkaḷ’ Duino Eeegies ‘ārḵpici nōkki eḻutiya īrēḻvarik kavitikaḷ’ (Sonnets To Orpheus) eṉpaṉa. ivi nikaḻ kālattip piṉṉukkut taḷḷiya muṉṉōṭik kavitikaḷ.

mālṭē lāriṭs pirik, rilkkiṉ paṇpukaḷi appaṭiyē uḷ vāṅkik koṇṭa kaṟpaṉip pāttiram. mēlum pōtalēriṉ uruvākkattiyum ippāttirap paṭippil kāṇalām. ikkuṟippukaḷil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa kaṭṭaṟṟa kavitikaḷ rilkkiṉ paṭippāṟṟalukkuc cavālāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantip paruvattip paṟṟiyum, kuṟikkōḷ illāta kātalip paṟṟiyum, cāvip paṟṟiyum, tuṟavipōl naṭikkum peṇkaḷip paṟṟiyum rilk nūtaṉamāka karuttukkaḷi mālṭē kuṟippukkaḷil veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṟār

ikkuṟippukaḷuḷ ‘mukaṅkaḷ’ eṉṟa pakutiyil, ovvoru maṉitaṉum pala mukaṅkaḷip peṟṟiruppatākavum, oru camayattil oṉṟi maṭṭum avaṉ aṇintu koṇṭu maṟṟavaṟṟi iruppil vittiruppatākavum cilar aṭikkaṭiyum avaciyam ēṟpaṭum pōtum tamatu mukaṅkaḷi māṟṟik koḷvatākavum cuviṭak kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāris nakaril nāṭṭarṭām terumuṉiyil, taṉatu mukattik kiyil eṭuttu vittukkoṇṭu amarntirunta peṇṇic cantittatāka oru mikikkaṟpaṉiyi cilk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār:

“teru veṟiccōṭippōy amitiyāka iruntatu; anta veṟumi eṉakkuc calippāka iruntatu; cōrvōṭu atteruveṅkum cuṟṟit tirintēṉ. eṉ kālkaḷ marakkaṭṭikaḷākak kaṉattaṉa, oru peṇ taṉ kikaḷi mukattukkut tāṅkalāka vittuk kuṉintu koṇṭiruntāḷ: eṉṉik kaṇṭatum tiṭukkiṭṭu veṭukkeṉa nimirntāḷ, appōtu avaḷ mukam kiyōṭu vantu viṭṭatu, avaḷ kiyil kiṭanta uṭkavinta veṟṟu mukattip pārttu nāṉ atircciyaṭintēṉ. ativiṭa atu peyarttu eṭukkappaṭṭa iṭam, mukam illāmal kāttiruppatik kāṇa añciṉēṉ.”

mēlē kuṟippiṭuvatu pōṉṟa kāṭcikaḷi aṭimaṉak kōṭpāṭṭu ōviyarkaḷiṉ paṭippiltāṉ kāṇa muṭiyum. ōviyar, rōṭiṉiṭam cilakālam ceyalāḷarāka iruntiruntālum, itupōṉṟa ōviyaṅkaḷip pārttirukka muṭiyātu. kāraṇam, rōṭiṉ cevviya ōviyar (Classicist). kirēkkat teyvamāṉa appōlōviṉ ciliyip pārttu uṇarccivacappaṭṭu rilk pāṭiya kaviti yoṉṟil,

itu-

uṉṉip pārkka virumpāta iṭam.

uṉakku iṅkē iṭamilli;

nī uṉṉi māṟṟik koḷ.





eṉṟa varikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. rilkkiṉ inta varikaḷi ilakkiya vātikaḷ aṭikkaṭi mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttāḷvatuṇṭu. inta varikaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷē mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa mikik kaṟpaṉiyāka viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ṭiyūṉō kōṭṭiyil oru nāḷ taṉimiyil amarntirunta pōtu, tam uḷḷattil nikaḻtta kaviti āvēcattik kīḻk kaṇṭavāṟu paṭarkkiyil pativu ceykiṟār rilk.

“iṉam puriyātoṉṟu avaṉiṭam aṉṟu nikaḻntatu.vaḻakkam pōl oru puttakattik kiyilēntiya vaṇṇam aṅku miṅkumāka naṭanta koṇṭirunta avaṉ, kaviyākap pirintirunta marakkiḷi. yoṉṟil vacatiyāka amarntu koṇṭāṉ. mikavum ōyvāka amarnta niliyil kiyilirunta puttakatti mūṭivittu viṭṭuccuṟṟiyirunta amitiyāṉa, aḻakiya iyaṟkic cūḻniliyil taṉṉi maṟantu mūḻkiṉāṉ. itaṟku muṉ aṉupavittirāta ōr uṇarcci veḷḷam avaṉuḷ aliyākap paravuvati uṇarntāṉ, avaṉ amarntirunta marattiṉ naṭuviliruntu avvuṇarcci melliya atirvukaḷāka avaṉuḷ iṟaṅkiyatu. itupōṉṟa iṉpa atircciyi avaṉ eṉṟum aṉupavittatilli. avaṉ uṭampē āṉmāvāka māṟi, iyalpāṉa taṉ ceyalkaḷi maṟantu, ētō oru pērāṟṟalit taṉṉuḷ ēṟṟuk koṇṭatu pōliruntatu, atuvumaṉṟi eḷitil pulappaṭāta iyaṟkiyiṉ maṟupuṟattik kaṇṭatupōlavum iruntatu.”

rilk ṭiyūṉō kōṭṭiyil iruntapōtu tammi yārō aḻippatippōṉṟu uṇarntār. ‘nāṉ kūviyaḻittāl, tēvatikaḷiṉ kūṭṭattil kēṭpavar yār?’ eṉṟu akkural aṭikkaṭi kūṟikkoṇṭiruntatu. anta aṭiyē ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpāviṉ tuvakkamāka amintatu.

ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpākkaḷi rilk 1912- ām āṇṭil eḻutat toṭaṅkiṉār. āṉāl avaṟṟi 1923 vari muṭikkavilli. ataṟkup palakāraṇaṅkaḷi āyvāḷarkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. 1912-ām āṇṭil avaruṭiya karuttukkaḷiyum, kavitippāṇiyiyum tīviramākap pātikkum aṉupavam avarukku ēṟpaṭṭatu. uvamikaḷ mūlam karuttu viḷakkam ceyvatik koñcam koñcamākak kiviṭṭu, karuttiyum karuttu viḷakkattiyum oṉṟākap purintu koḷḷum uruvaka uttikku māṟikkoṇṭiruntarar; itu mutaṟ kāraṇam. avaruḷḷattil patitta uṇarvukaḷ karutturuvam peṟa nīṇṭa cintaṉi tēvippaṭṭatu: itu iraṇṭāvatu kāraṇam. mēlum, ivviraṅkaṟpākkaḷi eḻutum pōtu uṇarcci pūrvamāṉa ilakkiyat tūṇṭutal tēvippaṭṭatu. attuṇṭutalic ceyaṟkiyākap peṟamuṭiyātu; itu mūṉṟāvatu kāraṇam.

ivviraṅkaṟpākkaḷ rilkkiṉ apūrvap paṭippāṟṟalukkuc cāṉṟāka uḷḷaṉa. ippākkaḷum paṭippōri mayakkavikkum maṟiporuḷkaḷi uḷḷaṭakkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ituvē ippākkaḷiṉ valimiyum, toymiyum ākum. āṅkilak kavitiyulakil, ṭapiḷyū. pi. yēṭcum, ṭi. es. eliyaṭṭum vakikkum iṭatti jermāṉiyak kaviti ulakil rilk vakikkiṟār. ṭi. es. eliyaṭ eḻutiyuḷḷa 'pāḻnilattiṟku' oppāka rilkkiṉ ‘ṭiyūṉō icaṅkaṟpākkaḷi’, ilakkiyavātikaḷ matikkiṉṟaṉar.

ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpākkaḷiṉ veṟṟikku rilk payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkum kaṭṭaṟṟa kavitiyāppē kāraṇam eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu yāppu muṟiyip payaṉpaṭuttiyiruntāl, rilkkiṟkut tēvippaṭṭa nikaḻcci kiṭikkāmal pōyirukkum. rilk ivaṟṟuḷ payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkum mūvacic cīrkaḷ, kaṭṭaṟṟa kaviti yāppil vaḻakkamākak kāṇappaṭum cantat taṭikaḷip pōkkiviṭṭaṉa.

ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpākkaḷiṉ taṉippāviyō, ataṉ pakutiyiyō tammit tavira vēṟu yārum patippikkak kūṭātu eṉṟu rilk aṟivittiruntār. iraṅkaṟpā muḻuvatiyum vēṟu oruvar patippikkavum muṭiyātu. patippikkak kūṭiya kaviti amippilum avi illi.

ārḵpici nōkki eḻutappaṭṭa īrēḻvarip pākkaḷ mottam aimpattintu. ivi aṟputaṅkaḷ nikaḻttiya kirēkkappāṭakaṉāka ārḵpiciṉ āṟṟalōṭu eḻutappaṭṭavi. ārḵpiciṉ vāḻkkiyil kēṭpōri mayaṅka vikkum aṟputaṅkaḷ iruppatu pōla, intap pākkaḷilum paṭippōri mayaṅka vikkum maṟi poruḷkaḷ niṟiya uṇṭu.

"ippākkaḷ iṟukkamum curukkamum mikkavi; ivaṟṟi ‘aṟintu koḷḷutal’ eṉpativiṭa ‘uḷḷuṇarvāl uṇarntu koḷḷutalē’ paṭippōrāl iyalum” eṉṟu rilkkē ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ikkuṟippu rempōviṉ kavitikaḷukkum, avaṟṟiyoṭṭip piṉṉāl tōṉṟiya kavitikaḷukkum poruntum eṉṟālum, ippākkaḷil kiyāḷappaṭṭuḷḷa uttikaḷ rilk kippaṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷap peritum utavukiṉṟaṉa.

kirēkka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mītum kalikaḷiṉ mītum rilkkiṟku niṟiya īṭupāṭu uṇṭu. kirēkkap pāṭakaṉāṉa ārḵpiciṉ iciyāṟṟal, avaṉ maṉivi yūriṭisiṉ iṟappu, avaḷi mīṭka avaṉ iṟappulakam ceṉṟu mīḷutal, kaṭiciyil am muyaṟciyil ēṟpaṭṭa avalam yāvum mikavum uṇarccikaramākak kaṟpaṉinayattōṭu ippākkaḷil pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi kirēkkat teyvatti nōkkip pāṭappaṭṭatāka amintiruntālum, uṇmiyāka makkaḷi nōkkip pāṭappaṭṭavi. vāḻkkiyil ēṟpaṭum iḻappukkaḷi eppaṭit tāṅkik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum, avaṟṟi eppaṭi makiḻcciyāka māṟṟik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum maṟi poruḷākap pāṭukiṟār rilk.

rilk iciyi mikavum virumpiṉār. ārḵpiciṉ yāḻum. avaṉ iciyum rilkkiṉ neñcil nīṅkāta iṭattip peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. ‘iciyē ulakam; iciyē vāḻkki. ulakiṉ ellāk kalikaḷum iciyiṉ māṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷē’ eṉpatu rilkkiṉ karuttu. iciyip paṟṟit tam kavitiyil ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭumpōtu,

ici-

ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mūccu

ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ pēccu

itayattiṉ veḷivaḷarcci:



pēccu muṉittu

avalap pātiyil

cellum pōtu

iṭiyil eḻum

ceṅkuttuc cuvar,



itu-

uṇarvukaḷi

ōci nilamāka ākkuvatu.





[tuṇṭup pāṭal-

ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpā]

mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa mūṉṟu nūlkaḷ allāmal ‘putuk kavitikaḷ’ (The New Poems) eṉṟa nūliyum rilkkiṉ paṭippukaḷuḷ mukkiyamāṉa oṉṟākak kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭum. itil uḷḷa kavitikaḷ iraṇṭu tokuppukaḷāka muṟiyē 1907, 1908 ām āṇṭukaḷil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟutti (The Panther) piyāṉō payiṟci (Piano Practice), līṭā (Leda) veṉicil kālaṅ kaṭanta iliyutirk kālam (Late Autumn In Venice), spāṉiya naṭaṉamaṅki (Spanish Dancer) ūtārip piḷḷiyiṉ puṟappāṭu ( The Departure of The Prodigal Son) eṉṟa pukaḻ peṟṟa kavitikaḷ ittokuppukkaḷilēyē uḷḷaṉa. mutalil kuṟippiṭṭa kavitikaḷukkum, intap putuk kavitikaḷukkum aṭippaṭiyil oru vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. ikkavitikaḷil pārppavariṉ uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷip paṟṟi atikam cintikkāmal, pārkkappaṭum poruḷiṉ tōṟṟam, taṉmip paṟṟiyē rilk atikamākac cintikkiṟār.

rilkkiṉ kavitikaḷik kūrntu kavaṉittāl, avaruṭiya paṭippaṭiyāṉa vaḷarcciyum, avar eppaṭip paṭṭavar eṉpatum pulaṉākum. rilk iḷamiyiliruntē taṉimi viruppamuḷḷavar; etiyum kūrntu pārkkum paḻakkam uṭiyavar; mālṭē lāriṭs pirik kuṟippukaḷil ‘taṉ muṉippumikka maṉitar’ (obstinate man) oruvarip paṟṟiya kuṟippu ōriṭattil varukiṟatu. ammaṉitar nāṭaka āciriyar ipcaṉ eṉṟum, ōviyar rōṭiṉ eṉṟum āyvāḷarkaḷ iruvēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. anta maṉitariṭamiruntē ‘tīviramākak kūrntupār’ eṉṟa pāṭattit tāmkaṟṟuk koṇṭatāka eḻutukiṟār rilk.

ciṟpi rōṭiṉ mikavum kaṇṭippāṉavar: yāriṭamum eḷitil ottuppōkātavar. avariṭattil ceyalāḷarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya rilk‘ avariṭamiruntu tam uḷḷoḷiyāl oru kavitippāṇiyik kaṟṟuṇarntār. akkavitikkuk karup poruḷ kiṭiyātu. kaviñaṉ āḻnta taṉimiyil irukkum pōtu, atu taṉakkuriya karuvit tāṉē paṭittu veṭṭa veḷiyil eṭuttu niṟutta vēṇṭum. atik karukkaviti (Poems of Things) eṉṟu rilk kuṟippiṭukiṟār. ṭapiḷyū. ec. āṭaṉ atip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu.

vāḻka rilkkiṉ karukkaviti

atu taṉimiyiṉ cāntā kḷās![1]





eṉṟu pārāṭṭukiṟār.

rilkkiṉ moḻipeyarppāḷar oruvar avarip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu, “rilk tamatu nēraṅkaḷip poruṭkāṭcic cālikaḷilum, nūlakaṅkaḷilum pūṅkākkaḷilum kaḻippār. iravu nēraṅkaḷil vītikaḷilum, manti veḷikaḷilum, cīṉ natip pālattiṉ mītum taṉimiyil cintaṉiyōṭu cuṟṟit tirivār” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.

taṉimi rilkkiṟkup piriyamāṉa oṉṟu. taṉimiyi avar uṇarntatu maṭṭumallāmal atip pōṟṟic cuvittār; mātākōvil toḻukiyiṉpōtu amitiyāka eḻuppappaṭum iṉiya iciyāka atik karutiṉār. taṉimi taṉṉi vāṭṭuvatāka iḷaṅkaviñar oruvar rilkkiṟku eḻutiya pōtu, taṉimi- uḷḷārnta paranta taṉimitāṉ kaviñaṉukku vēṇṭum. namakkuḷḷēyē nām, nīṇṭanēram oruvariyum etirppaṭāmal taṉimiyil naṭakka vēṇṭum. atutāṉ nām aṭiya vēṇṭiya iṉpattiṉ elli. taṉimi, kuḻantiyiṉ taṉimiyip pōl irukka vēṇṭum. kuḻanti, taṉakkuḷḷiruntu veḷiyulutip pārppatu pōl, kaviñaṉ taṉ uḷḷattiṉ āḻattiliruntu, taṉimiyiṉ utaviyōṭu veḷiyulakip pārkka vēṇṭum. atu tāṉ kaviñaṉiṉ paṇi, taram, toḻil” eṉṟu avaṉukku aṟivuri vaḻaṅkukiṟār.

evaṉ uṟutiyāka naṭuvil nilittirukkiṟāṉō, avaṉē vīraṉ’ eṉṟa emarcaṉiṉ poṉmoḻi rilkkiṟku mikavum piṭittamāṉa oṉṟu. rilk ōyāmal uḻittār. tuṉpattukku ilakkāṉa tam toṭakka niliyiliruntu, paṭippāṟṟali iṭiviṭātu veḷippaṭuttum tēvatiyākat tammi uyarttik koṇṭār.

avar piṟappāl kattōlikka kiṟittuva camayatticcārntavarāka iruntālum, tām eḻutiyuḷḷa ‘kiṟistuviṉ akak kāṭcikaḷ’ (Vision of Christ) eṉṟa nūlil, iyēcuvi oru ñāṉiyāka ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟārē tavira avatāra puruṣarāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilli. 1898-il ṭaskaṉi nakarippārttu viṭṭut tirumpiyavuṭaṉ tamatu kuṟippukkaḷil oru karuttit teriyappaṭuttukiṟār. atil tam uḷḷattil nilipēṟu koṇṭu tammi naṭattic cellum ōru pērāṟṟalit tām uṇarvatākavum, atittavira vēṟu teyvam, etuvum iruppatākattām nampavilli eṉṟum kuṟippiṭukiṟār. veḷiyil kāṇappaṭum maraṅkaḷ, malikaḷ, mēkaṅkaḷ; alikaḷ yāvum tām uḷḷattil kāṇum uṇmikaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷē eṉṟum kuṟippiṭukiṟār. oḻukkak kēṭāṉa navanākarika camutāyattiyum, kōvilukku vārantōṟum cellum carācari paktaṉiṉ āravārattiyum, vacatikku ēṟṟapaṭi vaṭivamikkappaṭṭa camayattiṉ (Ready-made religion) veṟumiyiyum paṟṟik kuṟippiṭa vanta rilk “ñāyiṟṟukkiḻami añcalakaṅkaḷip pōlat tūymiyākavum, mūṭiyum, ēmāṟṟam taruvatākavum uḷḷatu” eṉṟu uḷḷam nintu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.

tamatu ciṟuttip pāṭṭil ‘miyattic cuṟṟi naṭaṉamiṭum pērāṟṟal’ (The dance of forces round a centre) eṉṟa oru karuttik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. appērāṟṟal naṭam puriyum miyam tamatu itayamē eṉpatu avar karuttu anta miyam tamatu itayattil nīṅkāmal iṭam peṟavēṇṭu meṉṟāl, atu tam akak kāṭcikaḷip puṟak kāṭcikaḷāka iṭiyaṟāmal māṟṟi veḷippaṭuttik koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.

tamatu neruṅkiya tōḻiyāṉa lō calōmikku eḻutiya kaṭitattil “nāṉ kaliyi vāḻkkiyiliruntu vēṟupaṭuttip pārkka virumpavilli; eppaṭiyum aviyiraṇṭiṟkum poruḷ oṉṟākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tuvakka kālattil kali, vāḻkki ākiya iraṇṭiṉ ellikaḷi variyaṟuttu, vāḻkki aṉupavaṅkaḷ mūlamākak kaliyi vaḷappaṭuttiṉār rilk. tamatu tōḻiyum iḷam oviyarumāṉa. pavlā mōtar ṣōṉ pekkar’ eṉpavar pēṟu kālattil uyir tuṟantapōtum, tammip pōl kavitiyāṟṟalum cintaṉi vaḷamum mikka ‘keḷaṇṭ vulpkirāḵp varṉklkrīt’ eṉṟa iḷaṅkaviñar taṟkoli ceytu koṇṭa pōtum appēriṭikaḷi rilkkāl tāṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyavilli. avarkaḷ cāvukkāka iṟaṅki eḻutappaṭṭa icip pāṭalkaḷil iraṅkaliyum pukaḻcciyiyum oṉṟu paṭuttiyiruppatōṭu, vāḻviyum cāviyum ārpica nōkkil iṇittu muṭittirukkiṟār. “yār veṟṟiyippaṟṟippēcukiṟārkaḷ? etiyum tāṅkum itayamē vāḻkki” eṉṟu kūṟip peru mūccu viṭukiṟār rilk.

potuvākak kaviñarkaḷ ulakil uḷḷa parup poruḷkaḷip pārttu uṇarcci vacappaṭṭu ataṟkēṟpa uḷḷuṇarvukaḷi veḷippaṭuttuvatu vaḻakkam. āṉāl rilkkiṉ kavitiyāṟṟal ataṟku nērmāṟāṉatu. avaratu āṉmīka uḷḷam eppōtum akk kāṭcikaḷilēyē īṭupaṭṭirukkum. kuṟippāka ulakat tuṉpaṅkaḷāl pātikkappaṭṭu varuntik kaliyiṭam aṭikkalam pukumpōtu avaruḷḷattil akak kāṭcikaḷ niṟiyat tōṉṟum akkāṭcikaḷ veḷippaṭuttum uḷḷuṇarvukaḷukku oppāṉa puṟak kāṭcikaḷi veḷiyulakil tēṭuvār. piṟaku irṇṭiyum pirikka muṭiyātapaṭi oṉṟu paṭuttuvār. puṟavulakil kāṇappaṭum aciyāp poruḷkaḷ kūṭa, avaruḷḷat tuṇarccikaḷōṭu uṟavāṭum uyiriṉaṅkaḷākat teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

vīṭu pāttiram ōṭi katavu

palakaṇi pālam paḻamaram eṉṟu

veṟumiyāyk kuṟippiṭukiṟōm

nāṉ kūṟukiṟēṉa

purintu koḷḷuṅkaḷ.

ip poruḷkaḷ

eṉakku-

evvaḷavu nerukkamāṉavi eṉṟu

avikaḷē niṉittirukka muṭiyātu.





[ṭiyūṉō iraṅkaṟpā]

‘kaviñaṉ’ eṉṟa talippil eḻutiya kavitiyil, tamatu akak kāṭcikaḷ pulappaṭuttiya nuṇṇuṇarvukaḷi ārḵpicōṭu toṭarpupaṭutti kaṉavu pōl cittarikkiṟār rilk.

kaṭavuḷ āṟṟalmikkavar

cātāraṇa maṉitaṉ

teyvīka yāḻiciyit

toṭarntu vara muṭiyumā?



avaṉ aṟivu

kuḻampip pōyirukkiṟatu.

mēlum-

itayap pātiyiṉ kuṟukkē

appōlōviṉ

kōvil kiṭiyātu

pāṭutal

oru viruppamō

allatu

eḷitil kiṭṭum

kātaṟ cantippō aṉṟu.





pāṭutal-

kaṭavuḷukku eḷiya ceyal

āṉāl namakku..?

maṇṇil irukkum nammi

avaṉ eppōtu

viṇṇuk kuyarttuvāṉ?



iḷiñaṉē!

mutaṟ kātal

poṅkik kiḷampum pōtu

itaḻk katavi uṭittuk koṇṭu

uṇarcci

ōciyuruvam peṟukiṟatē!

atuvaṉṟu pāṭṭu,



pāṭuvati

maṟakkak kaṟṟuk koḷ;

atu tēviyumilli.

uṇmiyāṉa pāṭṭukku

oru putu mātiriyāṉa

cuvācam tēvi.

ōr amiti!

oru kaṭavuḷiṉ cilirppu!

oru meṉ kāṟṟu!





[ārḵpici nōkki]

1922 ām āṇṭil mārṣal prōsṭ eṉṟa kaviñaṉ iṟantapōtu avaṉip paṟṟi rilk kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu tam kaṭitattil kuṟippiṭṭeḻutiyuḷḷār:

“ivaṉ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaviti utti mikaccemmiyāṉatu. itu entakkuṟippiṭṭa poruḷilum ūṉṟi niṟpatilli. itu viḷiyāṭṭāka veḷippaṭuttum karuttukkaḷ īṭaṟṟa tulliyattōṭu, putiya putirkaḷi mēṉmēlum tōṟṟuvitta vaṇṇam aṅkumiṅkum

oṭṭikkoṇṭu ūcalāṭum.”

mārṣal prōsṭippaṟṟi rilk kūṟiya karuttukkaḷ avarukkum poruntum. rilkkiṉ kaviti cuvittup paṭippataṟkēṟṟatu. atippiṉpaṟṟi yārum eḻutavum muṭiyātu; ati moḻipeyarkkavum muṭiyātu. tuṇṭu tuṇṭākak kāṇappaṭum avaruṭiya kavitiyiṉ uṟuppukkaḷ, putiya iḷami kuṉṟāta pēccu vitiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavi. mūlamāṉatum, uyirttuṭippāṉatumāṉa ipputir moḻiyip purintu koḷḷak kavitiyiṉ iyaṟ poruḷi ūkkattōṭum īṭupāṭṭōṭum paṭippavarkaḷ aṇuka vēṇṭum.



taṉittaṉmimikka inta jermāṉiyak kaviñaṉiṉ tākkam, āṅkilō-amerikkak kaviñarkaḷāṉa ṭapiḷyū ec. āṭaṉ, ciṭṉikīs, ālaṉ lūyis, eṭitciṭvel, eṭviṉ mūr ākiyavarkaḷip peritum pātittirukkiṟatu. jermaṉiyiṉ ellikku veḷiyilē tamatu kavitiyātikkatti mikutiyākac celuttiyavarum, ketēvukku aṭuttaniliyil vittup pēcattakkavarum, jermāṉiyak kaviñarkaḷuḷ rilk kittavira vēṟuyārumilli.

ruciyak kaviñarkaḷāṉa pāsṭar nākkum sveṭṭēvāvum rilkkiṉ āṟṟali viyantu, avarit tam vaḻikāṭṭiyāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭa ‘aṭiyārkaḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟalām. mallārmēyi viṭak kuṟinta teḷivaṟṟa taṉmiyum, vēlariyiviṭa atika maṉita nēyamum rilkkiṉ paṭippil kāṇappaṭuvatāka āṅkilak kaviti vācakarkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. perumitamāṉa paṭippāṟṟalip poṟutta vari makākavi pōtalērukku oppākac collattakka orē airōppiyak kaviñaṉ rilktāṉ eṉpatil aiyamilli.

rilkkip paṟṟi etir maṟiyāṉa karuttic coṉṉavaṉ, jermaṉ nāṭṭic cērnta mārkciyak kaviñaṉperṭōlṭa prekṭ. ‘rilkkiṉ kavitikaḷ cīraḻintu pōṉa naṭuttara varkkattiṉ pitaṟṟalkaḷ! eṉakku avaṟṟip paṟṟik kavaliyilli’ eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ.

rilk jermaṉiyiṉ tali ciṟanta kaviñarkaḷuḷ oruvar eṉṟālum, avariṭattilum cila kuṟikaḷit tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. avaruṭiya-

(1) ciṟanta paṭippukaḷ purintu koḷḷa muṭiyātavi.

(2)cila paṭippukaḷ - mettap paṭittavarkaḷ maṭṭumē neruṅkattakka āḻnta ilakkiya tuṇukkam poruntiyavi.

(3)cila camayaṅkaḷil avar payaṉpaṭuttum poruttamillāta paṭimaṅkaḷ kavitic cuviyik keṭuttu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

(4) avaruṭiya ciṟanta kavitikaḷil cila (ārḵpis yūriṭis hermis) ceṟivum curukkamumiṉṟip paḻaṅkālap pāṭalkaḷippōl irukkiṉṟaṉa.
