rilkkiṉ cāvum kaṟpaṉic cuvi poruntiyatu. ceṭiyiliruntu oru rōjā malarik kiḷḷiya pōtu, muḷkutti avar iṟantu viṭṭatāka oru ceyti vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ‘ṭeṭṭaṉas’ eṉṟa icivu nōyip paṟṟi aṟiyappaṭāta tēti antak kālattil icceyti viyappiṟkuriya oṉṟāka iruntirukkalām. ēṟkeṉavē irattappuṟṟunōyāl pātikkappaṭṭirunta rilkkiṉ uṭal nili, muḷ kuttiyatum mēlum mōcamāki, atuvē icivu nōykkuk kāraṇamāki, atuvē cāvukkum kāraṇa mākiyirukkalām.





↑ kiṟistumas paricukaḷik kuḻantikaḷukkuk koṇṭuvarum koḻutta civappuk kiḻavaṉ; ivaṉik kiṟistumas tāttā eṉṟum colluvar

avaṉ aḷavaṟṟa paricukaḷik kuḻantikaḷukku vārikkoṭuppatupōl, taṉimi rilikkiṟku aḻakāṉa karukkavitikaḷi vāri vaḻaṅkiyatākap poruḷ koḷḷa vēṇṭum,





* * *





﻿amerikkā-

oru payittiyakkāraviṭuti





esrā pavuṇṭ

(1885-1972)





esrā pavuṇṭ oru putir. muraṇpāṭukaḷiṉ muṭiccu yēṭs, ḵprāsṭ pōṉṟa maṉitāpimāṉam mikka kaviñarkaḷōṭu neruṅkippaḻakiya avar maṉita camutāyattiṉ viṭutali uṇarvi muṟṟilum aḻikka muyaṉṟa ḵpācicavātiyāṉa mucōliṉiyōṭum neruṅkip paḻakiṉār. pavuṇṭ ciṟanta kaviñar; aṟiñar; cīrtiruttavāti.

“kaviñarkaḷuḷ oru cilarē vāḻakkūṭiya tuṇiccalāṉa vāḻkkiyi nam kālattil vāḻntu kāṭṭiyavar” eṉṟu amerikkakkaviñar villiyam kārlōs villiyam ecavim, “intanūṟṟāṇṭil allatu itaṟkumuntiya nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil piṟanta kaviñarkaḷ ellārum pavuṇṭiṉāl pātikkappaṭṭu avar eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu niṟiyakkaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. ati entak kaviñaṉāvatu maṟuttuk kūṟiṉāl, avaṉ kaṇṭaṉattitiviṭap paritāpattiṟkē uriyavaṉ” eṉṟu hemiṅvēyum kūṟiyuḷḷār.

amerikka nāṭṭiṉ pukaḻpeṟṟa kaviñarāṉa ḵpirāsṭ pavuṇṭi oru ‘puyaṟpaṟavi’ eṉṟu karutiṉār. pavuṇṭiṟkāka avar eḻutiya kavitiyoṉṟil, ‘uṇmiyik kūṟukiṟēṉ; uṉṉik kaṇṭu nāṉ mikavum añcukiṟēṉ’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār'

ippaṭi ulakappēraṟiñarkaḷāl orumukamākap pārāṭṭappaṭṭa esrā lūmis pavuṇṭ (Ezra Loomis, Round) 1885 ām āṇṭu akṭōpar 30ām nāḷ aikkiya amerikkāvil itāhō mānilattil heyli eṉṟa ūril piṟantār. ivaruṭiya tāyār amerikka kaviñar heṉṟi vōrṭs vort lāṅḵpellōviṉ uṟaviṉar. tanti aracāṅka aluvalar; heyliyil mutaṉ mutalākak kārik kaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭiyavar.

iḷamiyilēyē ōyāmal paṭikkum kuṇamuṭiyavar pavuṇṭ, niyūyārk hēmilṭaṉ kallūriyil oppilakkiyatti mukkiyap pāṭamāka eṭuttup payiṉṟu iḷaṅkalip paṭṭam peṟṟār. peṉcilvēṉiyāp palkalik kaḻakattil mutukalippaṭṭam peṟṟār. piṉṉar speyiṉ nāṭakāciriyar lōp-ṭi-vēkāviṉ paṭippukkaḷi āyvu ceyvataṟkāka ōrāṇṭu speyiṉ, pirāṉcu, ittāli ākiya nāṭukaḷil cuṟṟup payaṇam mēṟ koṇṭār. mīṇṭum amerikkā tirumpi iṇṭiyāṉāvil oru kallūriyil pērāciriyarākap paṇiyēṟṟār. āṉāl kallūri marapukaḷukku māṟākavum, maṉam pōṉa pōkkilum naṭappatākak kuṟṟamcāṭṭappaṭṭup paṇiyiliruntu vilakkappaṭṭār. innikaḻcciyip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭa pavuṇṭ, “nāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa vāḻki (the Latin Quarter type life) kallūri nirvākattukkup piṭikkavilli” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

pavuṇṭukku amerikkāviyum piṭikkavilli; amerikka makkaḷiyum piṭikkavilli. amerikkā kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭu. aṅku palanāṭṭu makkaḷum kuṭiyēṟi, niliyāṉa oru paṇpāṭṭu vaḷarcciyi aṭiyāta niliyil iruntaṉar. aṅkiruppati viṭat toṉmic ciṟappum, kali ilakkiyap paṇpāṭṭuc ciṟappum mikka airōppiya nāṭukaḷil ceṉṟu vāḻvatu ciṟantatu eṉṟu pavuṇṭ karutiṉār. amerikkāvi ‘arik kāṭṭu mirāṇṭi nāṭu’ (half-savage country) eṉṟu ikaḻntu kūṟiviṭṭu iṅkiruntu veḷiyēṟiṉār. avvāṟu veḷiyēṟiya vēṟiru kaviñarkaḷ heṉṟi jēmsām, ṭi.es. eliyaṭṭum āvar.

tammi ōr eḻucci mikka puraṭcik kāraṉākak karutiya pavuṇṭ paḻamiyiyum parampari parampariyākap paḻamiyil mūḻkik kiṭanta amerikka naṭuttara makkaḷiṉ maṭamiyiyum veṟuttār. ‘pullitaḻkaḷiṉ’ (Leaves of Grass) āciriyarākiya kaviñar vālṭ viṭmaṉik kūṭap pavuṇṭukkup piṭikkātu. pavuṇṭukku avar oru kumaṭṭum māttiri. viṭmaṉiṉ kavitikaḷil toḻil nuṭpam illi eṉpatu avar karuttu.

“viṭmaṉē! nī oru muṭṭāḷ tanti. oru vaḷarnta kuḻantiyāka nāṉ uṉṉic cantikka varukiṟēṉ. uṉṉi nāṉ nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷāka veṟut tiruntālum, uṉṉiṭam naṭpuc ceytu koḷḷum aḷavukku

vayatāṉavaṉ. eppaṭiyiruntālum putiya kaṭṭiyi veṭṭik koṭuttavaṉ nītāṉē! atic cetukkum kālam vantu viṭṭatu. namakku orē mūlam; orē uyir. nam toṭarpu nīṭikkaṭṭum”

eṉṟu pavuṇṭ viṭmaṉiṭam camātāṉam ceytu ceytukoḷkiṟār. itu uṇmitāṉ. viṭmaṉum amerikkak kaviti, tām viṭṭa iṭattilēyē niṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟu collavilli; tām viṭṭa iṭattiliruntu toṭarntu atu muṉṉōkkic cella vēṇṭum eṉṟē virumpiṉār.

airiṣ kaviñarāṉa yēṭcip pavuṇṭ mikavum matittup pōṟṟiṉār. kaṭanta oru nūṟṟāṇṭil yēṭcukku iṇiyāṉa kaviñarkaḷ yārumilli eṉptu avar kaṇippu eṉavē yēṭcic cantittu avarōṭu paḻaka vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvattōṭu pavuṇṭ ilaṇṭaṉ vantu cērntār. pavuṇṭuṭaṉ paḻakiya yēṭs avaruṭiya aṟivunuṭpatti viyantu pārāṭṭiṉār. tamatu naṇpar oruvarukku eḻutiya kaṭitattil ilaṇṭaṉil uḷḷa iḷiya talimuṟik kaviñarkaḷuḷ pavuṇṭ mutaṉmiyāṉavar: tīvira paṭippāṟṟal mikkavar. ivar pāluṇarvaṟṟa ōr amerikkap pērāciriyar; uṇarcciyi viṭa uḻippukku mutaliṭam koṭuppavar; niṉittavuṭaṉ pāṭavalla ciṟanta ācu kavi; ivar kavitiyil uruvatti viṭa naṭi naṉṟāka irukkum; anta naṭiyum iṭiyiṭiyē uṭintum taṭippaṭṭum koṭuṅkaṉavākavum, kuḻappam mikka valippākavum muṭintu viṭum. avaruṭiya cōtaṉi muyaṟcikaḷ tavaṟāṉaviyāka irukkalām. āṉāl eḻucciyillāta marapiviṭa, muṉṉēṟṟamāṉa, tavaṟukaḷ paricukkuriyavi” eṉṟu yēṭs kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.

ilaṇṭaṉil vāḻnta kālattil, eḻuttil tīvirap puraṭcikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttuṭiya iḷiñarkaḷi oṉṟu cērttup pavuṇṭ avarkaḷukkut talimi tāṅkiṉār. iṅku vāḻnta kālattil ivar mēṟkoṇṭa paṇikaḷil kuṟippiṭat takkatu. amerikka aṟiñar erṉesṭ ḵpeṉṉalōcā (Ernest Fennelosa) viṉ kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷi āyntu cīṉa, jappāṉiyak kavitikaḷiyum, nō (Noh)[1] nāṭakaṅkaḷiyum āṅkilattil moḻiyākkam ceytu patippittatutāṉ. jappāṉiya hikkū kavitikaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷiyum, ciṟappukkaḷiyum mēli nāṭukaḷil viḷamparap paṭuttiya perumiyum ivariyē cārum.

pavuṇṭukkup pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil vāḻnta āṅkilak kaviñarkaḷiṉ tākkamum, praveṉkal pāṭakarkaḷiṉ[2] (Provencal Singers) tākkamum mika atikam, āṅkilak kaviñarkaḷuḷ piruṉiṅki ivarukku mikavum piṭikkum. pireḷaṉiṅ kavitikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa kalinuṭpam ivarip peritum kavarntatu. kiṭṭattaṭṭa iruvaruṭiya kaviti uttikaḷum oṉṟē.

ruciya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ivarik kavaravilli. ciṟanta ruciya eḻuttāḷarkaḷāṉa tālstāy, tāstāvski, cekkāv ākiyōriṉ paṭippukkaḷ kūṭa pavuṇṭiṉ nūlakattil iṭampeṟavilli. ilaṇṭaṉil vāḻnta kālattil ārṉāṭṭēṉiyal, kātiyar, kēval kēṇṭi, heṉṟi jēms ākiyōriṉ nūlkaḷi virumpippaṭittār.

pavuṇṭiṉ naṭpāl atikappayaṉ peṟṟavar kaviñar ṭi. es. eliyaṭ. avar pavuṇṭiṉ tiṟaṉāyviṉi ēṟṟup ‘pāḻnilam’ eṉṟa tamatu kavitiyip pātiyākac curukkik koṇṭār. atuvē akkavitiyiṉ veṟṟiyāka amintu avarukku nōpel pariciyum tēṭik koṭuttatu. in naṉṟikkākavē ciṟanta toḻil vallunarukku (the better Craftsman) eṉṟu colli akkavitiyik pavuṇṭukkup paṭittirukkiṟār. eliyaṭṭiṉ veṟṟip paṭippukkaḷāṉa ‘oru peṇṇiṉ varalāṟu’ (Portrit of a Lady), pruḵprāk (Prufrock) ākiya iraṇṭumē pavuṇṭiṉ paṭippukkaḷip paṭitta tākkattāl uruvāṉavi.

cama kālak kavitikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa miku puṉiviyak (Romantic Excess) koḷkiyit tīviramāka etirtta iḷaṅkaviñarkaḷi yellām oṉṟutiraṭṭi ōr amippi niṟuviṉār. maṟṟa kaviñarkaḷiṭamiruntu avarkaḷi vēṟu pirittuk kāṭṭuvataṟkāka avarkaḷukkup paṭimakkaviñarkaḷ (imagists) eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiṉār. piṟaku paṭimakkaviñarkaḷiṉ koḷki aṟikki (the manifesto of imagists) oṉṟiyum veḷiyiṭṭār. avvaṟikki varumāṟu:

(1) pēccu vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum kavitiyil iṭam peṟa vēṇṭum; kavitikku alaṅkārac colliviṭac cariyāṉa collē tēvi.

(2) oru kaviñaṉ taṉatu taṉit taṉmiyi marapiviṭak kaṭṭaṟṟa kavitiyiltāṉ ciṟappāka veḷippaṭutta muṭiyum eṉṟu.nampukiṟōm. eṉavē yāppilakkaṇa aṭippaṭiyil eḻuppappaṭum cantaṅkaḷi viṭa karuttut toṉiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil eḻuppappaṭum cantaṅkaḷē ciṟantavi. aviyē kaviñaṉiṉ maṉaniliyitteḷivāka veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.

(3) kaṭiṉamāka iruntālum cariyāka eḻutappaṭum kavitiyil teḷiviṉmiyō, karuttuṟutiyaṟṟa taṉmiyō irukkātu.

mēlē kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ nalla kavitiyilakkiyap paṭippukkaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyātavi eṉṟālum, ilaṇṭaṉ ilakkiya vātikaḷiṭiyē ikkaruttukkaḷukkuk kaṭum etirppu iruntatu. pōrāṭṭak kuṇam mikka ami lōval (Amy Lowell) eṉṟa peṇmaṇi ivarukku etirākak kiḷampi, iḷaṅkaviñarkaḷit tam pakkam īrttuk koṇṭu paṭima iyakkattukkut talimi ēṟṟār. itaṉāl veṟuppum calippum aṭinta pavuṇṭ avarkaḷi nellik kāy mūṭṭi eṉṟu veṟuttotukkiviṭṭu, ilaṇṭaṉi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟip pāricu nakaram vantu cērntār.

eṅku ceṉṟālum avarip piṉpaṟṟum ōr iḷiñar kūṭṭam avarip cuṟṟik koḷvatuṇṭu. āṉāl pavuṇṭu avarkaḷic caṭṭi ceyvatilli. ivaruṭiya vimarcaṉaṅkaḷum mikak kaṭumiyāṉaviyāka irukkum. eppaṭiyiruntālum ivar oru viyappiṟkuriya maṉitarāka ellārālum karutappaṭṭār. ōviyarum nāvalāciriyarumāṉa viṇṭhām lūyis Wyndham Lews irupatu vayatu iḷiñarāṉa pavuṇṭip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭum pōtu “cakittuk koḷḷa muṭiyāta viṟippum, alaṭṭalum, tuḷḷalum mikka amerikkac centāṭi iḷiñaṉ, taṇṇīril mitakkum eṇṇeyt tuḷiyāka avaṉ yāriṭamum oṭṭāmal iruntāṉ. avaṉ viruppamellām piṟar uḷḷattil taṉṉip paṟṟiya muttiri patikka vēṇṭumeṉpatē” eṉṟukūṟukiṟār.

pavuṇṭiṉ piṭivātamum pulamic cerukkum mēlināṭṭu ilakkiya vaṭṭārattil mikap piracittam. ilakkiya vātikaḷ avari neruṅkavē añcuvar. mēṭiyil pēcum pōtu karakaratta kuralil kalip puraṭcik koḷkiyi urakkap pēcuvār. avaruṭiya muraṭṭut tāṭiyum, yāriyum matikkāta taṉmiyum, kuttaluṭaṉ kūṭiya kiṇṭal pēccum oru carvātikārappuraṭcit talivaṉāka avari ellārukkum aṟimukappaṭuttiṉa. eṉṟālum kalippuraṭciyi muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattavum, kavitip putumiyi nilināṭṭavum, kaviti (Poetry) ūḻikkāṟṟu (Blast) pōṉṟa ciṟiya viṭiveḷḷip pattirikikaḷi naṭattavum avar tēvippaṭṭār; avar puyal uḻippāḷi.

tamatu 27 ām vayatiṟkuḷ pavuṇṭ aintu paṭippukkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭār.avaruṭiya tuvakkakālakkavitikaḷ, paḻamiyāṉa pireñcukkaviti maṟṟum āṅkilap putukkavitikaḷiṉ kalaviyāka amintiruntaṉa; mēlum praveṉkal kaviñarkaḷ, iṭikkālat taṉṉuṇarccik kaviñarkaḷ, pireḷaṉiṅ, villiyam mōris, sviṉparṉ, layaṉal jāṉcaṉ pōṉṟōriṉ paṭippiṉ cāyaliyum avaṟṟil kāṇalām. paḻamiyum ceḻippum mikka nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷum, pireñcu maṭakkup pāṭalkaḷum ivaruṭiya kaṉṉip paṭippukkuk kaṭik kālāka amintaṉa. pavuṇṭ tamatu 29 ām vayatil ‘ṭōrati ṣēkspiyar’ eṉṟa peṇṇi maṇantār. avarkaḷukku ōr āṇ kuḻantiyum piṟantatu.

pāricu nakara naṇparkaḷiyum, iḷiñarkaḷiyum, ilakkiyavātikaḷiyum pavuṇṭ taṉ pēccālum, karuttālum, acāttiyap pulamiyālum, paṭippu vēkattālum, vakkara puttiyiṉālum, muraṭṭuttaṉattālum ericcalūṭṭik koṇṭum tikikka vittuk koṇṭumiruntār. nāḷāka āka avar kaṇṭippumikka muraṭṭu āciriyarāka māṟit tam aṟivurikaḷi ellārukkum vaḻaṅkik koṇṭiruntār. atēcamayattil putuvitamāṉa paṭippāṟṟal oṉṟu avariṭam kālkoḷḷat toṭaṅkiyatu. kuttalum kiṇṭalum kūṭiya uriyāṭal pāṇiyil avar kaviti eḻutat toṭaṅkiṉar moḻipeyarppukaḷ taṉittaṉic ciṟiya kavitit toṭarccikaḷ eṉṟa niliyiliruntumāṟic cikkalāṉa vaṭivamippiyuṭiya nīṇṭa kaviti muṟikku māṟiṉār. 35āvatu vayatil avar eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭa hakkilviṉ māperli (Hug Selwyn Mauberly) eṉṟa kavitit toṭar avar vāḻkkiyil tiruppu muṉiyāka aminatatu.

‘māperli’ paṉṉiraṇṭu kavitikaḷ aṭaṅkiya toṭar. ik kavitit talivaṉ māperli viḷamparamillāta oru kaṟpaṉik kaviñaṉ; taṉakku muṟpaṭṭa kali ilakkiyac cātaṉikaḷi naṉkaṟinta aḻakiyalvāti. āṉāl taṉṉic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kali ilakkiya vātikaḷiṉ pōlittaṉaṅkaḷōṭum, camutāyattiṉ koccit taṉmiyōṭum avaṉāl ottuppōka muṭiyavilli. taṉakkeṉṟu paṭittuk koṇṭa taṟkāppāṉa nuṇṇiya taṉimi ulakil avaṉ taṉṉic curukkik koḷkiṟāṉ. pruḵp rākkiṉ pāttirap paṭippu ṭi. es. eliyaṭṭukku eppaṭi oru muka mūṭiyāka amintatō atēpōla māperli pāttiram pavuṇṭiṉ muka mūṭiyāka amintuḷḷatu. māperli pāttirattiṉ vāyilākat taṉ contak karuttukkaḷiyum, koḷkikaḷiyum, viruppu veṟuppukkaḷiyum kuttalum kēliyum kalantu koṭṭittīrkkiṟār. tamakku anniyamākip pōṉa āṅkilak kalāccārattiṉ kōṇalkaḷiyum, viyāpārat taṉmi malintu pōṉa pōlik kali ilakkiya ulakiyum kaṭumiyākac cāṭukiṟār. māperli vāḻnta iṅkilāntiṉ kaviti ilakkiyac cūḻal paṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭa pavuṇṭ,

inta nērattil

ivarkaḷiṉ avacarattēvi

vacaṉa meṉṉuma kārip pūccu:

calavik kallō

cantac ciṟpamō alla.





eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.

māperli kaviti, caṅkilit toṭarāṉa kali nuṇukkac cātaṉikaḷum, muraṭṭu vēkam kalanta kāṭṭāṟṟuk karuttōṭṭamum, oḻuṅkukkuṭpaṭṭa niṉivalikaḷ iṭiveṭṭum uṇarccimayamāṉa mēṟkōḷkaḷum niṟintatu. pavuṇṭ ikkavitiyip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu itu uṇarcciyiṉ cariyāṉa pativu. “itu kuṟippiṭṭa oru kālattil kuṟippiṭṭa oru maṉitaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭa aṉupavam; itu vāḻkkiyiṉ iṉpiyal tuṉpiyal iraṇṭiyum kuṟippiṭum kāviyam. ārṉālṭiṉ tēyntupōṉa toṭarāṉa vāḻkkiyiṉ āyvu (Criticism of life) eṉpatu itaṟkuc ciṟappākap poruntum” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.

pavuṇṭiṉ paṭippukkaḷil ellārālum atikam pēcappaṭuvatum, tiṟaṉāyvukkum, kaṇṭaṉattukkum, pārāṭṭutalukkum uṭpaṭuttappaṭuvatum avaruṭiya kāṇṭaṅkaḷ[3] (Cantos) ākum. ikkāṇṭaṅkaḷ pavuṇṭiṉ āḻnta cintaṉiyil muḷitta taṉimoḻikaḷ (Monologues). ivaṟṟi eḻutimuṭikkap pavuṇṭukkuk kāl nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ piṭittaṉa. mutal patiṉāṟu kāṇṭaṅkaḷ 1925-ām āṇṭilum, maṟṟa kāṇṭaṅkaḷ aṭutta irupatu āṇṭukaḷilum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. picā nakarukku arukil ciṟi vikkappaṭṭapōtu, avar pattuk kāṇṭaṅkaḷ eḻutiṉār. avi picā kāṇṭaṅkaḷ (Pisan Cantos) eṉṟa talippil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. ellāmākac cērttu eṇpattintu kāṇṭaṅkaḷ innūluḷ aṭaṅkum.

ikkāṇṭaṅkaḷ veḷiyāṉa putitil itippaṭikka muyaṉṟavarkaḷ. ētum puriyatu viḻittaṉar. ivi ētēr maṟi coṟkaḷāl (Code) eḻutappaṭṭavi eṉṟum, ēṟṟatiṟavu kōl illāmal ivaṟṟukkuḷ nuḻiya muṭiyātu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. ikkūṟṟiṉi muṟṟilum tavaṟu eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. ikkāṇṭaṅkaḷ puriya vēṇṭumāṉāl hōmariṉ otīciyatti (Odyssey) uḷḷattil niṉittuk koṇṭu paṭikkat toṭaṅka vēṇṭum. kirēkkak kāppiyattalivaṉ otīciyas tirāy nakarap pōrukkuppiṉ kappalil taṉ tāy nāṭu tirumpukiṟāṉ. āṉāl vaḻitavaṟip pala iṭaṅkaḷilum cuṟṟiyalintu pala vitamāṉa cōtaṉikaḷukkum allalkaḷukkum āḷākip palatarappaṭṭa putiya aṉupavaṅkaḷip peṟṟu iṟutiyil taṉ nāṭāṉa itākāvi aṭikiṟāṉ. iṅkuk kāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ nāyakaṉ esrā pavuṇṭ, otīciyasiṉ kaṭicik kuṟikkōḷ maṉiviyiyum, makaṉiyum ceṉṟaṭivatu. kāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ kuṟikkōḷ ‘tāṉ yār’ eṉpatiyum, ‘ulakam eṉṉa?’ eṉpatiyum kaṇṭaṟivatu tāṉ. ivviṉākkaḷukku viṭiyaṟiyap pārviyaṟṟut tiriyum nāṭōṭiyāka alikiṉṟār pavuṇṭ.

pavuṇṭ intak kāṇṭaṅkaḷit tāntēyiṉ teyvīkak kāppiyattiṟku (Divine Comedy) oppiṭṭup pēcukiṟār. tāntēyiṉ kāppiyam mūṉṟu kāṇṭaṅkaḷāka amintuḷḷatu. mutalkāṇṭam narakam (inferno) iraṇṭāvatu kāṇṭam kaḻuvāy (Purgiaiorio.) mūṉṟāvatu kāṇṭam cuvarkkam (Purgiatouo). tām eḻutiyuḷḷa kirēkkam, maṟumalarcci, mutal ulakappōr paṟṟiya kāṇṭaṅkaḷ narakattiṟkum, poruḷātāram kaṭum vaṭṭi paṟṟiya kāṇṭaṅkaḷ kaḻuvāykkum, iṟutik kāṇṭaṅkaḷic corkkattiṟkum oppiṭukiṟār pavuṇṭ.

āṉāl ilakakiyavātikaḷ ivipaṟṟi vevvēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷik kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. kāṇṭaṅkaḷ pavuṇṭiṉ uṉṉatap paṭippu eṉṟum, uyirōṭṭamuḷḷa vaṟṟāta kāppiyam eṉṟum oru cārār kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. oru cārār ‘kiṟukkup piṭitta, ciṟu piḷḷittaṉamāṉa karuttukkaḷik kuvittuvittirukkum paṭukuḻi’ eṉṟu paḻitturikkiṉṟaṉar.

oru muṟi cilar pavuṇṭic cantittu, “taṉit taṉik kāṇṭaṅkaḷ etik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa? oṭṭu mottamāka ellāk kāṇṭaṅkaḷum kūṟum miyakkarutteṉṉa?” eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉar. ataṟkup pavuṇṭ piṉvarumāṟu viṭiyiṟuttār:

(1) “taṉit taṉik kāṇṭaṅkaḷ aṟivu jīvikaḷiṉ pēccip pōl, oḻuṅkiṟku uṭpaṭāta oḻuṅkuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭum.

(2) mottak kāṇṭaṅkaḷum paṭippōrit tuṉpuṟuttum uruvakakkuppi. paṭippavarkaḷ eṉṉa niṉikkiṟārkaḷō avaṟṟi yellām eṟṟukkoḷḷum”.

intak kāṇṭaṅkaḷip paṭittapiṟaku ālaṉ ṭēṭ (Allen Tate) eṉṟa aṟiñar ‘ivi etip paṟṟiyum illi’ eṉṟa muṭivukku vantār.

‘maṉita vāḻkkiyip pala kuralkaḷilum, pala parimāṇaṅkaḷilum kūṟum kāppiyamē kāṇṭaṅkaḷ’ eṉṟu pavuṇṭ kuṟippiṭṭālum, ivaṟṟil potintuḷḷa mikippaṭṭa varuṇaṉikaḷiyum, maṟimukamāṉa kēlikiṇṭalkaḷiyum, tiṭīr makiḻccip paravacattiyum, viyappurikaḷiyum, vēṟupaṭṭa poruḷ vaḻaṅkum cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷiyum, kuḻappum kuṟumput taṉaṅkaḷiyum purintu koḷḷac cātāraṇa aṟivuḷḷavarkaḷāl muṭiyātu. palmoḻiyaṟivum, paṉmukappaṭṭa ilakkiya varalāṟṟup pulamiyum, kirēkka itikācat teḷivum peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. cīṉa jappāṉiyak kavitip pōkkum, kaṉpūciyat tattuvamum, parcicamum purintirukka vēṇṭum. pērakarāti (Encyclopedia) maṟṟum paṉmoḻic ciṟṟakarātikaḷiṉ tuṇiyum vēṇṭum. pavuṇṭiṉ camakāla naṇparkaḷ, avarkaḷiṭam avar koṇṭirunta naṭpu, antaraṅkam yāvum terintirukka vēṇṭum. ivvaḷavum pōtā. iṉṉum iciyaṟivum, ciṟpa ōviyak kaliyaṟivum vēṇṭum. ivvaḷavu mustīpukaḷuṭaṉ kāṇṭaṅkaḷi neruṅkiṉālum, ‘avaṟṟiṉ poruḷiṉ maṇam kurutiyil paravuvataṟku ovvorumāṉavaṉum āṟumuṟiyāvatu paṭikka vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu pavuṇṭiṉ vi‘ciṟi kaviñar riccarṭu perhārṭ’ eṉpavar kuṟippiṭukiṟār

“kāṇṭaṅkaḷ nākarikattiṉ kariyiliruntu kūrntu nōkki eḻutappaṭṭa ulaka varalāṟu” eṉṟu ḵpōrṭu mēṭāks ḵpōrṭu' eṉpavar kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.

“kāṇṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭumāṉāl, muṭivaṟṟa kuḻappam calippūṭṭum mūṭupaṉiyākat toṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu” eṉṟu ḵpiṭjarālṭu kuṟippikiṟār.

pavuṇṭiṉ paṭippukkaḷ paṟṟi ettaṉiyō karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷ iruntālum, avaruṭiya ārvalarkaḷ avaruṭiya eḻuttip putiya vētamāka eṟṟup pōṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. pavuṇṭ tam kavitikaḷōṭu, pireñcuk kavi villaṉ paṟṟi ici nāṭakam oṉṟum (Opera), pala kaṭṭuri nūlkaḷum eḻutiyuḷḷār. aḷaviṟanta moḻi peyarppukaḷum ceytirukkiṟār. inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ ciṟanta moḻi peyarppāḷar eṉṟu pavuṇṭik kūṟalāma.

pavuṇṭ pārici viṭṭu 1924- ām āṇṭu ittāli nāṭṭu rivirāvil caṟṟu vetuvetuppāṉa rēpallō eṉṟa iṭattukkuk kuṭipeyarntār. 1939-ām āṇṭu amerikkā ceṉṟu koñcanāḷ taṅkiṉār. appōtu ḵpācicattip pukaḻntum, amerikka jaṉātipati jeḵparcaṉi mucōliṉiyōṭu oppiṭṭup pēciyum amerikka makkaḷiṉ etirppukku āḷāṉār. ivari ātaritta naṇparkaḷum ceyvataṟiyātu tikittaṉar. ‘ṭakḷas ṇamutāyakkaṭaṉ tiṭṭatti’ (Douglas Social Credit System)ij paṟṟip pēci yūtarkaḷiṉ etirppiyum campātittuk koṇṭār. amerikkāvi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟi nīṇṭanāḷ nāṭōṭiyākat tirinta taṉimi veṟuppu avari avvāṟu pēca vittuviṭṭatu eṉṟu ellārum eṇṇiṉar. tammik kuṟi kūṟi vimarcippatip pavuṇṭ eppōtum tāṅkik koḷḷamāṭṭār. mīṇṭum ittālikkē tirumpiviṭṭār.

iraṇṭām ulakappōr toṭaṅkiyatu. ḵpācisa vātikaḷukku ātaravākavum, amerikkarkaḷukku etirākavum rōm nakara vāṉoliyil vāram iru muṟi piracārattil īṭupaṭṭār pavuṇṭ. amerikka nāṭṭiyum, ataṉ makkaḷāṭci muṟiyiyum, āṭcittalivar rūsvelṭṭiyum kaṭumiyāka vimarcittu vacimāri poḻintār. avar pēccil yūtaveṟuppu koppaḷittatu; avar kavitikaḷilum ivveṟuppukkaḷ poṅki vaḻintaṉa.

iraṇṭām ulakappōril nēcanāṭukaḷ veṟṟi peṟṟatum, 1945-l tēcatturōkakkuṟṟattukkāka amerikkappaṭiyiṉarāl kitu ceyyappaṭṭu, picā nakarukku arukil irunta irāṇuvac ciṟiccālikkuk (Army Disciplinary Barracks) koṇṭu cellappaṭṭār. aṅkē kaṭum kuṟṟavāḷikaḷukkeṉap pirattiyēkamākat tayārikkappaṭṭa eḵku valik kūṇṭil aṭikkappaṭṭār. appōtu pavuṇṭukku vayatu aṟupatu. āṟu tiṅkaḷ ikkūṇṭil aṭikkappaṭṭa pavuṇṭiṉ uṭalum, uḷḷamum pātikkappaṭṭaṉa. maṟatiyālum, taṉimi nōyālum (Clustro phobia) mika varuntiṉār. eṉṟālum ciṟik kūṇṭilum avar eḻutuvati niṟuttavilli. ciṟiyiliruntu eḻutiya kāṇṭaṅkaḷil ciṟiyaṉupavam, taṉimittuṉpam, āṉmattēṭal ākiyavaṟṟippativu ceykiṟār. tamatu ciṟiyaṉupavattik kuṟippiṭṭa pavuṇṭ,

eṉṉi-

āpattāṉa koṭūra maṉitaṉ

eṉṟeṇṇi

ellārum veṟut totukkiṉar

iravum pakalum

eṉakkuk

kāval iruntatu



cippāykaḷ-

aṭikkaṭi eṉṉi vantu

eṭṭip pārppārkaḷ.

cilar eṉakku

uṇavu koṇṭu varuvar.

kiḻavaṉ es...

paricuk kuriy

oru kāṭcip poruḷ





eṉṟu urukkamāka eḻutukiṟār.

piṟaku pavuṇṭ vicāraṇiyiṉ nimittam vāṣiṅṭaṉukku aḻittuc cellappaṭṭār. aṅku avaric cōtitta maruttuvarkaḷ, avari oru maṉanōyāḷi eṉṟu muṭivu ceytaṉar. ataṉāl vicāraṇiyiliruntum, maraṇa taṇṭaṉiyiliruntum viṭupaṭṭu, ceyiṇṭ elicapet maṉa nōy maruttuvamaṉiyil cērkkappaṭṭār. maruttuvamaṉiyil avar iruntapōtu avaruṭiya ciṟik kavitikaḷukku (Pisan cantos) amerikka nūlakap pērāyattāl (Fellows of library) pōliṅkaṉ paricu (Bollingan prize) vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. appōtu avarukku etirāṉa araciyalvātikaḷuma ilakkiya vātikaḷum kaṭum etirpput terivittaṉar. antap pōrāṭṭam pala mātaṅkaḷ nīṭittatu.

paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu, avaruṭiya taḷḷāmi karuti, maruttuvamaṉiyiliruntu viṭuvikkappaṭṭār. avar mītu cāṭṭappṭṭa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ, avaruṭiya putti pētalippiṉ viḷivu eṉṟu karuti amerikka aracāṅkam avar mītirunta vaḻakkukaḷit tirumpap peṟṟuk koṇṭatu. maruttuva maṉiyi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiyatum pavuṇṭ mīṇṭum ittālikkē tirumpiviṭṭār.

“cutantira pūmi eṉṟu collappaṭum anta nāṭṭiliruntu viṭutali peṟṟataṟkāka nāṉ mikka makiḻcciyaṭikiṟēṉ; amerikkā oru payittiyakkāra viṭuti” eṉṟu aṟivittār pavuṇṭ.





↑ jappāṉiya nāṭakam ḵpeṉṉalōcāviṉ kiyeḻuttup piratikaḷil innāṭkaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. pavuṇṭ ivaṟṟic ceppaṉiṭṭu veḷiyiṭṭār. ‘nō’ nāṭakaṅkaḷiliruntu pavuṇṭ kaṟṟuk koṇṭa pala ceytikaḷ ‘paṭimam’ paṟṟiya avaruṭiya kōṭpāṭukaḷi uruvākkiṉa

↑ pireñcu nāṭṭil provencal eṉṟa pakutiyil vāḻnta pāṭakarkaḷ. ippakutiyil vaḻaṅkiya pireñcu moḻimarapukku ēṟpa eḻutappaṭṭa ivarkaḷuṭiya nāṭṭuppāṭalkaḷip pavuṇṭ mikavum. virumpippaṭittār. tuvakkakālap pavuṇṭiṉ kavitikaḷil ippāṭalkaḷiṉ tākkam iruntatu.

↑ 'Canto' eṉpatu kāppiyap pakuppu. iti camaskirutattil 'kāṇṭam' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟōm.





* * *





eṉ vāḻkkiyik

kāḵpik karaṇṭiyāl aḷantu

calittup pōṉēṉ.





ṭi. es. eliyaṭ

(1888-1965)



tāmas sṭerṉs eliyaṭ eḻupattintu āṇṭukaḷ uyir vāḻntavar; nāṟpattintāṇṭukaḷ tīvira ilakkiyap paṭippil īṭupaṭṭavar; mikacciṟanta āṅkilak kaviñaruḷ oruvarāka matikkappaṭupavar. irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭup putuk kavitiyulakil ivarippōl pātippi ēṟpaṭuttiya kaviñar vēṟuyārumilli. eṉṟālum ivar paṭippukkaḷ cātāraṇa makkaḷic ceṉṟaṭiyavilli. ivar aṟiñarkaḷiṉ kaviñar. ivar paṭippukkaḷil uḷḷa iruṇmiyum (Obscurity) āṉmarakaciyap puṉivum (mystcism) eḷitil evariyum neruṅka viṭuvatilli. eṉavē ivarip potuvāka mēli nāṭṭu ilakkiyavātikaḷ ‘aruvak kaviñar’ (the invisible poet) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu vaḻakkam.

eliyaṭ aikkiya amerikka nāṭṭil missuri mānilattil, ceyiṇṭ. lūyis nakaril 1888-l piṟantavar, tanti heṉṟi vēr eliyar oru toḻilatipar: ceṅkal āli naṭattiyavar. tāy cārleṭsṭerṉs eḻuttāṟṟalum, kaliyārvamum mikka camūkacēvaki. tanti vaḻippāṭṭaṉār ceyiṇṭ lūyil nakaril, iṟiyorumik kōṭpāṭṭut tiruc capiyiyum (unitarian church), vāṣiṅṭaṉ palkalik kaḻakattiyum niṟuviyavar; camayac cārpāṉa pala nūlkaḷi eḻutiyavar. eliyaṭ ōr eḻuttāḷarākavum camayak kaviñarākavum uruppeṟṟataṟku aṭittaḷamāka avaruṭiya pāṭṭaṉārum tāyārum viḷaṅkiṉar. eliyaṭ ciṟanta vaṅki ūḻiyarākavum, patippāḷarākavum viḷaṅkiyataṟkut tantiyiṉ toḻil tiṟami aṭittaḷamāka viḷaṅkiyatu.

ēliyaṭṭiṉ iḷamik kalvi ceyiṇṭ lūyicil iṭam peṟṟatu. piṉṉar hārvārṭu palkalik kaḻakattil cērntu 1906liruntu 1910 vari uyarkalvi payiṉṟār. paḷḷiyil paṭikkum kālattilēyē eliyaṭ ciṟanta māṇavarākak karutappaṭṭār. 1900 ām āṇṭil ilattīṉ pāṭattil ciṟappāṉa tērcci peṟṟataṟkākat taṅkap patakkam peṟṟār. hārvārṭu palkalikkaḻakattil virivāka moḻi, ilakkiyap pāṭaṅkaḷip payiṉṟār. oppilakkiya āyvil iḷamiyiliruntē avarukku viruppam atikam. ataṉāl kirīk, jermaṉ, pireñcu, āṅkila ilakkiyaṅkaḷi viruppap pāṭamākavum payiṉṟār.

eliyaṭṭiṉ etirkāla vaḷarccikkup peritum kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ irviṅpāpiṭ, jiyārj cāntāyaṇā eṉṟa iraṇṭu pērāciriyarkaḷ. paṇṭiya marapukaḷil eliyaṭ teḷinta aṟivu peṟṟataṟku ivarkaḷē kāraṇam. ārtar cimaṉs eṉpār eḻutiya ‘ilakkiyattil kuṟiyīṭṭu iyakkam’ (the symbolist movement in literature) eṉṟa nūli 1908-il eliyaṭ paṭittavuṭaṉ, pireñcuk kuṟiyīṭṭuk kaviñarkaḷiṉ paṭippil- kuṟippāka lāḵpōrkkiṉ kavitikaḷil- avarukku ārvam ēṟpaṭṭatu.

hārvārṭu palkalik kaḻakattil paṭikkum kālattilēyē avarukkuk kaviti eḻutuvatil ārvam iruttatu. hārvārṭu itaḻāṉa 'aṭvakēṭ' (advocate)ṭip patippikkum poṟuppāṉarāka irunta eliyaṭ atil tamatu tuvakkakālak kavitikaṉi veḷiyiṭṭār.

1910-ām āṇṭil hārvārṭil paṭṭam peṟṟa piṟaku pāricu nakaram ceṉṟu cārmōṉ palkalikkaḻakattil cērntu ōrāṇṭukkālam pireñcu kuṟiyīṭṭilakkiyaṅkaḷip payiṉṟār. pāricil appōtu vāḻnta pireñcu ilakkiya vātikaḷōṭu neruṅkip paḻaki, avarkaḷ paṭippukkaḷiyum payiṉṟār. piṉṉar pavēriyā, jermaṉi mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu, aṅkē mukkiyamāṉ cila jermaṉ eḻuttāḷarkaḷic cantittu avarkaḷuṭiya paṭippukkaḷiyum payiṉṟār. piṟaku mīṇṭum hārvārṭu palkalikkaḻakattil cērntu camaskiruta ilakkiyamum intiyat tattuvamum payiṉṟār. ōyvu nēraṅkaḷil kuttuccaṇṭi payiṉṟār. āṉ muṉiyil (Cape Ann) tam tanti kaṭṭiyirutta ōyvukkāla māḷikiyil tamatu viṭumuṟi nāṭkaḷik kaḻittār. appōtu paṭaku ōṭṭum payiṟciyil vallavarāṉār ivaruṭiya kavitikaḷil kaṭaṟpayaṇam paṟṟiya paṭimaṅkaḷ atikamākak kāṇappaṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇam itutāṉ.

mutal ulakappōr tuvaṅkiyatum eliyaṭ jermaṉiyi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟi ilaṇṭaṉil kuṭiyēṟiṉār ākspōrṭu palkalik kaḻakattil cērntu mīṇṭum tamatu kalviyit toṭarntār. iṅkilāntil iruntapaṭiyē pirāṭlēyiṉ tattuvac cintaṉikaḷi āyvu ceytu ‘muṉivar’ paṭṭam peṟa virumpittaṉ āyvuk kaṭṭuriyi hārvārṭu palkalikkaḻakattukku aṉuppi vittār. āṉāl appaṭṭattippeṟa avar hārvārṭu mīṇṭum cellavilli.

poruḷātāra nerukkaṭi kāraṇamāka avar cila āṇṭukaḷ paḷḷi āciriyarākavum, pala āṇṭukaḷ lāyiṭs vaṅki aluvalarākavum paṇipurintār ilaṇṭaṉ vāḻkki avaruṭiya ilakkiya muṉṉēṟṟattukkup peritum utaviyatu. ilaṇṭaṉil appōtu pala ilakkiya vaṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ puḷumspari ilakkiya vaṭṭamum, kaviñar ciṭvelliṉ ilakkiya vaṭṭamum kuṟippiṭattakkavi. yēṭs, esrā pavuṇṭ pōṉṟa ciṟanta kaviñarkaḷiṉ toṭarpu avaratu kavitiyāṟṟalic cāṉipiṭittuk koḷḷap peritum utaviyatu.1915-il ‘viviyaṉ heyk’ eṉṟa naṭaṉamātit tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭu nirantara āṅkilakkuṭimakaṉāka ilaṇṭaṉil taṅkiviṭṭār.

1927-ām āṇṭu tammi āṅkilakkuṭimakaṉākap pativu ceytu koṇṭapōtu, āṅkilikaṉ kiṟittava camayattilum cērntu koṇṭār. ataṉ piṟaku avar eḻutiya ‘mākiyiṉ payaṇam’ (The Journey of the Magi) āṣ veṭaṉesṭē (Ash Wednesday) ākiya iraṇṭu nūlkaḷiṉ kuralum, avar putitākac cērnta camayattiṉ uṇarvukaḷi ōṅki olikkak kāṇalām

eliyaṭṭiṉ kalvi āḻamāṉatu; akaṉṟatu. avar paṉmoḻip payiṟciyum, pal tuṟip payiṟciyum oruṅkē vāykkap peṟṟavar; vāḻnta kālattilēyē mutaltaramāṉa ilakkiya mētiyāka ellārālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭavar. pala muṟi amerikkap palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu ilakkiyac coṟpoḻivukaḷ nikaḻttiviṭṭu vantavar; cākum vari ōyviṉṟi eḻutik koṇṭiruntavar; ulakiṉ mikac ciṟanta paricukaḷāṉa ārṭar āḵp meriṭ, nōpel paricu ākiyavaṟṟōṭu vēṟupala paricukaḷum peṟṟavar.

avaruṭiya mutal maṉivi nīṇṭa nāḷ paṭukkiyil kiṭantu 1947-il iṟanta piṉṉar, tammiṭam ceyalāḷarākap paṇipurinta ‘vēlari ḵpḷeccar’ eṉṟa māti 1957-il tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭār. iṟutik kālattil anta ammiyār eliyaṭṭi aṉpōṭum parivōṭum kavaṉittuk koṇṭār. 1965 ām āṇṭu jaṉavari 4 ām nāḷ pukaḻiṉ ucciyil vāḻntapōtu ilaṇṭaṉil eliyaṭ uyirnīttār. 17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil amerikkāvukku iṭam peyarnta avarkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ vāḻnta ‘īsṭ kōkkar’ eṉṟa kukkirāmattil avar aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭār.

āciriyarākavum, vaṅki aluvalarākavum paṇipurinta pattāṇṭuk kālam eliyaṭ niṟiya eḻutiṉār. aḷavukku mīṟi uḻittatāl aṭikkaṭi nōy vāyppaṭṭār. 1918 il kaṭaṟpaṭiyil cērat tammip pativuceytukoṇṭār; mōcamāṉa uṭalnili kāraṇamāka nirākarikkappaṭṭār. 1917 liruntu 1919 vari ‘ti ekōyisṭ’ (The Egoist) eṉṟa itaḻiṉ tuṇiyāciriyarāka iruntār. 1923-il kiriṭīriyaṉ (Criterion) eṉṟa nāḷitaḻiṉ āciriyarākap poṟuppēṟṟu iraṇṭām ulakappōr tuvaṅkumvari toṭarntu naṭattivantār. 1925-il ḵpēpar aṇṭu ḵpēpar (Faber aud Faber) eṉṟa patippakattiṉ paṅkutārar āṉār. piṉṉar ataṉ iyakkunarākit tam vāḻnāḷ iṟuti variyilum atit tiṟampaṭa naṭatti vantār.

eliyaṭ kaṇṇukku eṭuppāṉa tōṟṟamuṭiyavar; āṭamparamāka āṭi aṇipavar. uyarntu melinta uṭalvāku, cintaṉit tēkkam, aḷavāṉa amitiyāṉa pēccu, āravāramillāta nakiccuvi-ivaṟṟiṉ motta uruvam eliyaṭ.

eliyaṭ kaṭumiyāṉavarākak kāṭciyaḷittālum, piṟariṭam aṉpum irakkamum mikkavar; tārāḷamāṉavar; āṉāl pārppataṟkuk kaṇṭippāṉavarākak kāṭciyaḷippār, kīḻttaramāṉa eṇṇamō, ceyalō illātavar. āḻamāṉa kalviyum, kavitiyāṟṟalum, āṉmīka uṇarvum mikkavar; kuṟṟaṅkaḷik kēli ceypavar; pāvac ceyalkaḷi veṟuppavar; piṭikkāta maṉitarkaḷip pārkkavē virumpātavar; palvēṟu tuṟiyic cārnta kuṟinta naṇpar vaṭṭattiyuṭiyavar.

avar peṇkaḷiṉ kūṭṭattil makiḻcciyākavum ōyvākavum kāṇappaṭuvār. koḷkikaḷip paṟṟiyō, piramukarkaḷip paṟṟiyō pēcat tayaṅkamāṭṭār. appōtu avar veḷiyiṭum karuttukkaḷ āḻnta cintaṉiyiliruntu veḷippaṭum. veṭṭip pēcci avar eppōtum virumpiyatilli. cariyāṉa tūymiyāṉa paḻakkaṅkaḷik kaṭip piṭippār. avariṭattil enta vitamāṉa oḻuṅkīṉattiyum kāṇamuṭiyātu.

eliyaṭ nōykku añcum iyalpuṭiyavar. viḷiyāṭṭil viruppamillātavar. nīṇṭa kippiṭiyuḷḷa kuṭiyi eppōtum eṭuttuc celvār. nalla uṇavu, pīr, oyiṉ ākiyavaṟṟic cuvittuc cāppiṭuvār. eṉṟālum pālāṭikkaṭṭi avarukku mikavum piṭittamāṉa uṇavu.

avar ōr amerikkarāka iruntālum āṅkilakkuṭimakaṉāka māṟiyapiṟaku annāṭṭiṉ camayattiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum muḻumiyāka ēṟṟukkoṇṭār. āṅkila makkaḷippōla varikkōṭu pōṭṭa kāluṟiyum (Striped Trousers) karuppu mēlaṅkiyum aṇintu kikkuṭi toppicakitamākak kāṭciyaḷippār. moḻiyikkūṭa āṅkilēyarippōla olikkakkaṟṟuk koṇṭār. amerikkat taṉittaṉmi uruvākak kāraṇamāka irunta camayakkāḻppu, kuṭiyēṟṟam, cutantirappōr ākiyavaṟṟi aṭiyōṭu maṟantu nūṟṟukku nūṟu āṅkilēyarākavē vāḻntār.

līyar maṉṉaṉ taṉṉippaṟṟit tāṉē kūṟik koḷvatip pōla, eliyaṭṭum tammip paṟṟic cariyāṉa kaviti ōviyattik kīḻk kaṇṭavāṟu varintu kāṭṭukiṟār:

eḻuttariṉ cāyal;

kaṭukaṭuppāṉa puruvam;

pōlip puṉṉaki utaṭukaḷ.



kaṉavāṉ eliyaṭṭik

kāṇac cakikkavilli,

iṉiya pēccu;

‘āṉāl , . eṉṉa . . . kacacitam

iruntālum irukkalām’

eṉṟa iṭi veṭṭukaḷ,





“nāṉ paḻañ ciṟappiyavāti (Classicist); āṅkilō-kattōlikkac camayavāti” eṉṟu kūṟik koḷvār. puṉiviyak kaviñarkaḷ (Romantic poets) vāḻnta kālattukkum eliyaṭ vāḻnta kālattukkum niṟiya vēṟupāṭuṇṭu.

pattoṉpatu, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ēṟpaṭṭa kalvi vaḷarcciyum, aṟiviyal vaḷarcciyum makkaḷic cintikkumpaṭi tūṇṭiṉa. avarkaḷ etiyum appaṭiyē ēṟṟuk koḷḷum niliyil illi. ‘ēṉ? eppaṭi?’ eṉṟa pakuttaṟivuk kēḷvikaḷip pōṭṭu aiyaṅkaḷit teḷivu paṭuttik koḷḷa virumpiṉar.

pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil iṅkilāntil nilappirapukkaḷiṉ āṭciyum, celvākkum carintu, toḻilatiparkaḷ celvākkuppeṟat toṭaṅkiṉar. ataṉāl kirāmaṅkaḷi viṭat toḻil nakaraṅkaḷ peritum vaḷarcci cēṟṟaṉa. nakaraṅkaḷi nōkki makkaḷ kuviyat toṭaṅkiṉar. aḷavaṟṟa makkaṭ perukkattāl nakaraṅkaḷil cukātārakkēṭum, oḻukkak kēṭum, vaṟumiyum, maṇamuṟivum niṟintu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.

ḵprāyṭu, jaṅ pōṉṟa uḷaviyal aṟiñarkaḷiṉ ārāycci muṭivukaḷ ellāt tuṟikaḷilum periya pātippukaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉa. “ellā makkaḷum cintaṉit teḷivōṭu naṭappatilli. niṟivēṟāta viruppaṅkaḷum nacukkappaṭṭa pāluṇarcciyiṉ viḷivukaḷuma oru taṉi maṉitaṉip peritum pātittu, avaṉ: ceyalkaḷukkuk kāraṇamākiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟu avarkaḷ viḷakkiṉar.

pāluṟavip paṟṟip pēcuvatō, eḻutuvatō, ārāyvatō tavaṟilli eṉṟa nili ēṟpaṭṭatu. ḵprāsṭiṉ oṭiyas uḷḷuṇarccit (The Oedipus Complex) tattuvam [1]aṟiñarkaḷ, eḻuttāḷarkaḷ, kaliñarkaḷ ākiyōriṭiyē periya paraparappi ēṟapaṭuttiyatu. iyaṟpiyal, vāṉaviyal, tattuvaviyal tuṟikaḷil ēṟpaṭṭa vaḷarcci, iyaṟki, maṉitaṉ, kaṭavuḷ ākiya karuttukkaḷippaṟṟi āḻamākac cintikka ellāriyum tūṇṭiyatu. poruḷ mutal vātamum (Materialism) mākciyaccintaṉiyum makkaḷiṭattil kaṭavuḷ-camayam paṟṟiya accaṅkaḷi akaṟṟiyatōṭu urimiyuṇarccikaḷiyum vaḷarttaṉa. paḻamiyi matittup pōṟṟum eṇṇam māṟiyatu.

mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu vanta camūkak kaṭṭukkōppu taḷāntatu; oḻukka neṟikaḷ matippiḻantaṉa. kaṭavuḷukku oppāka matittup pōṟṟiya āṇṭikaḷi, ēḻimakkaḷ urimiyuṇarcciyōṭu etirttuk kēḷvi kēṭkat tuṇintaṉar. mutal ulakappōr airōppiya makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattil pītiyiyum accattiyum kiḷappi viṭṭatōṭu, veṟumiyiyum tōṟṟuvittatu. etirkālam iruṇṭu kāṇappaṭṭatu. irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭut tuvakkattil kāl koṇṭirunta ittaṉi eṇṇaṅkaḷum, uṇarccikaḷum, viḷivukaḷum eliyaṭṭiṉ paṭippil iṭam peṟṟaṉa.

putukkaviti paṭippōrukku marapilakkiyap payiṟci tēviyā eṉṟu cilar aiyappaṭuvatuṇṭu. āṉāl, marapilakkiyap payiṟci puttilakkiyam paṭippataṟkum putuk kaviti eḻutuvataṟkum mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttukiṟār eliyaṭ. kirēkka rōmāṉiyap paṇṭi ilakkiyap payiṟciyē eliyaṭṭiṉ veṟṟikku aṭippaṭiyāka viḷaṅkiyatu.

“marapu eṉpatu toṭarcciyāṉa vaḷarcciyuṭiyatu; iṭiyiṭiyē māṟṟam aṭivatu, ovvoru puttilakkiyamum taṉakaku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ toṭarpum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu ciṟappāṉa muṉṉēṟṟamum māṟutalum koṇṭatu. eḻuttāḷaṉ taṉ uḻippāl marapit teḷivākap purintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. marapiyum, putumiyiyum cuvittu makiḻa vēṇṭumāṉāl, ilakkiyattiṉ toṭarnta varāṟṟaṟivip peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum” eṉṟu marapiṉ uyarvi eliyaṭ eṭuttuk kūṟukiṟār.

eliyaṭ marapi matittup pōṟṟiya atēnērattil, marapiṉ kuṟikaḷiyum eṭuttuviḷakkiṉār. irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ tuvakkattil āṅkilak kaviti marapu tēyntu iṟṟup pōṉa niliyil iruntatu. kaviñarkaḷ tēynta paḻampātiyilēyē calippiṉṟi naṭantaṉar. putiya cintaṉiyō taṉit taṉmiyō avarkaḷ paṭippil illāmal pōyiṟṟu. vōrṭsvort pāṭiya vāṉavilliyum, kuyiliyum, ērikkari malark kūṭṭattiyum tirumpattirumpap pāṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar. ippāṭalkaḷ eḷimiyōṭum kaṟpaṉiyaḻakōṭum. iṉiya canta nayattōṭum iyaṟkiyiyē cuṟṟic cuṟṟi vantaṉa. kavitikaḷ niṟiya eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ taram mikattāḻntiruntatu.

eḷitāṉa ippōkki māṟṟi tīviramāṉa oru kavitip pōkki uruvākka niṉittār eliyaṭ. kaviti, cikkaṉam, curukkamum, nuṇukkamum, cikkalum uṭiyatāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu karutiṉār. oru cilarē purintu koṇṭālum, kaviti taramāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu avar karuttu. eṉavē kavitiyiṉ uḷḷaṭakkattilum, uruvattilum māṟṟaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭutta virumpiṉār.

kātalum, vīramum, iyaṟkiyum maṭṭumē kavitiyiṉ pāṭu poruḷ eṉṟa niliyi māṟṟi, avar vāḻntakālattil avaric cuṟṟiyirunta ovvoru poruḷippaṟṟiyum, avarippātitta ovvōr uṇarviyum pāṭṭil vaṭikka virumpiṉār. “kaviñaṉ taṉakkup pāṭu poruḷāka aḻakāṉa ulaki maṭṭumē tēṭic cellak kūṭātu. aḻaku, aḻakaṟṟa taṉmi iraṇṭiyum ārāya vēṇṭum. pukaḻ, calippu, aruvaruppu yāviyum avaṉ pāṭalil iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum” eṉṟu kavitiyiṉ payaṉ (The use of Poetry) eṉṟa tamatu nūlil eliyaṭ kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

pēruntukaḷiṉ pēririccalum, ṭirām vaṇṭikaḷiṉ kaṭakaṭa voliyum, vāṉa ūrtikaḷiṉ perumūccum avar pāṭalkaḷil etirolikkiṉṟaṉa. pōriṉ koṭumikaḷiyum, nakaramakkaḷiṉ avalaṅkaḷiyum, pōli nākarikattiṉ alaṅkōlaṅkaḷiyum, ēmāṟṟattālum ēkkattālum pātikkappaṭṭu maṉanōyāḷikaḷākat tiriyum makkaḷiṉ tuyaraṅkaḷiyum, camayattiṉ pōlittaṉaṅkaḷiyum pāṭu poruḷākkiṉār. cātāraṇa makkaḷiṉ iṉpatuṉpaṅkaḷi, avarkaḷ pēccilēyē kavitiyākkiṉār. tamakku muṉṉōṭiyākac cila kaviñarkaḷit tērnteṭuttu, avariṭamirunta ciṟanta kavitip paṇpukaḷit tām varttuk koṇṭār. pōtalēriṉ karuttuc ceṟiviyum, jāṉṭaṉṉiṉ āḻntakaṉṟa paltuṟi aṟivākkattiyum, lāḵpōrkkiṉ curukkattiyum, tāntēyiṉ ceṭṭāṉa kaviti naṭiyiyum, pavuṇṭiṉ paṭima uttiyiyum tam paṭippil ēṟṟuk koṇṭār.

piṟa kaviñarkaḷiṉ paṭippukkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi ici, ciṟpam, oviyam pōṉṟa kalikaḷiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷum uttikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ putiya vaḷarccikaḷum eliyaṭṭiṉ paṭippip pātittaṉa. avaruṭiya ‘pāḻ nilam’ (Waste Land) eṉṟa kavitiyil kāṇappaṭum canta vēṟupāṭukaḷum, maṭakkukaḷum, cērnticiyāka olikkum. eṉavē ai. ē. riccarṭs eṉṟa aṟiñar eliyaṭṭiṉ kavitikaḷik ‘karuttici’ (the music of ideas) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

oru pāṭakaṉiṉ varicip paṭuttappaṭṭa icit toṭarkaḷ, namakkuk karuttukkaḷi uṇarttuvati viṭa, iṭiyīṭillāta iṉpa uṇarcciyil nammi āḻtti meymaṟakkac ceyvatiyē kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ‘oru nalla kaviti purintu koḷvataṟku muṉpākavē, paṭippavariṉ uḷḷattil nuḻintu viṭa vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu eliyaṭ kuṟippiṭuvatu itittāṉ. miyak karuttip purintu koḷvataṟku muṉpākavē, akkavitiyil uḷḷa paṭimaṅkaḷ, kuṟiyīṭukaḷ, cantanayam ākiyavaṟṟi uḷḷattil naṉku, paṭiyaviṭṭup paṭippaṭiyāka ataṉ muḻumiyi neruṅka vēṇṭum.

eliyaṭṭiṉ kavitiyiluḷḷa iruṇmi vēṇṭumeṉṟē avar virumpi mēṟkoṇṭatu tāṉ. avar tam kavitik karuttukkaḷik kuṟippākavum, maṟi mukamākavum, cikkalākavum, mēṟkōḷkaḷ niṟintatākavum veḷippaṭuttuvār. cilacamayaṅkaḷil tam karuttu veḷippāṭṭiṟku āṅkilam pōtavilli eṉṟu karutiṉāl, vēṟu moḻikaḷiyum iṭiyiṭiyē kiyāḷuvār. cila camayaṅkaḷil coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷiyē kūṭa māṟṟi vaṭuvār. hōmar mutal taṟkālap paṭippāḷar nūlkaḷiliruntum, intumatam, puttamatam piṟa kīḻināṭṭu mataṅkaḷiliruntum, toṉmiyāṉa purāṇaṅkaḷiliruntum, viviliyattiliruntum, viḷamparamillāta kaṭṭukkatikaḷiliruntum, palatuṟit tattuvaṅkaḷiliruntum mēṟkōḷkaḷiyum, maraput toṭarkaḷiyum aḷḷit teḷittuk koṇṭu pōvār. paṭippavar avaṟṟil muṭṭimōtik koṇṭu mūccut tiṇaṟuvar. ‘pāḻ nilam’ eṉṟa kavitiyil maṭṭum irupattintu vēṟupaṭṭa eḻuttāḷarkaḷiṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷum, āṟu vēṟu moḻikaḷil eṭuttāḷappaṭṭa pakutikaḷum iṭam peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. eliyaṭṭiṉ ceṟivāṉa ikkavitip pāṇiyi ai.ē riccarṭs kavitic curukkeḻuttu (Poetic Shorthand) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟār. kavitiyip paṭippavaṉum, kavitiyip paṭippavaṉum kaṭumiyāṉa mūḷiyuḻippi veḷippaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉpatu eliyaṭṭiṉ karuttu.

eliyaṭ tām vāḻnta kālac cūḻnilikku ēṟpap palvēṟupaṭṭa cikkalāṉa poruḷkaḷit tamatu kavitikkuriya pāṭu poruḷkaḷāka mēṟkoṇṭatu pōlak kavitiyiṉ vaṭivattiyum tamatu viruppattiṟku ēṟpa māṟṟik koṇṭār. kaviti ilakkaṇattit taḷarttik kaṭṭaṟṟa kaviti vaṭivattip payaṉpaṭuttiṉār. cetukkiya ilakkiyac coṟkaḷi otukkiviṭṭu, pēccu vaḻakkil uḷḷa naṭimuṟic coṟkaḷiyum, koccic coṟkaḷiyum kūṭap payaṉpaṭuttiṉār. āṅkila yāppil iṉṟiyamiyātatākak karutappaṭṭa iyiput toṭikaḷik kiviṭṭuviṭṭuk karuttuk kēṟpavum uṇarccik kēṟpavum toṭikaḷi amittār. coṟkaḷiṉ ōci vēṟupāṭukaḷi vittuk koṇṭē, uṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷi uruvākkiṉār.

eliyaṭ ciṟanta kaviñar maṭṭumllar, tiṟaṉāyvāḷar tiṟamiyāṉa nāṭakāciriyar. nakara vāḻkakiyiṉ koṭumikaḷum, mutal ulakap pōriṉ piṉ viḷivukaḷum eliyaṭṭiṉ uḷḷattil āḻamāṉa kāyaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉa. akkāyaṅkaḷ āṟāta puṇkaḷāka appaṭiyē nilittuviṭṭaṉa. airōppiyac camutāyattiṉ mītu avarukku avanampikki ēṟpaṭṭatu. makkaḷ nērmiyum, oḻukkamum aṟṟavarkaḷāka avarukkut teṉpaṭṭaṉar. āṉmīka uṇarvu makkaḷ uḷḷattil paṭṭup pōyiruntatu. poy, ēmāṟṟu, kaḷavu, vipacāram, pōlittaṉam eṅkum malintiruntaṉa. inta iḻiniliyiliruntu mīḷavēṇṭumāṉāl, makkaḷ tāṅkaḷ ceyta kuṟṟaṅkaḷukkāka varutta vēṇṭum; kaṭavuḷiṭam muṟiyiṭa vēṇṭum; āṉmīka neṟiyil taṅkaḷik koṇṭu celutta vēṇṭum, eṉṟu eliyaṭ karutiṉār. avaruḷḷattil ēṟpaṭṭa uṇarccik kontaḷippukku vaṭikālākap pāḻnilam amittatu.

“oru kaviñaṉiṉ tiṟamiyi avaṉ maṟṟavarkaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ vāṅkuvati vittē purintu koḷḷalām; vaḷarāta kaviñarkaḷ piṟarip pōla eḻutuvar; vaḷarnta kaviñarkaḷ piṟarkaruttit tiruṭuvar; mōcamāṉa kaviñarkaḷ tiruṭiyati māṟṟuvar: nalla kaviñarkaḷ piṟar karutti mēṉmippaṭuttuvatōṭu, piṟitoṉṟākavē paṭippar” eṉṟu eliyaṭ ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭukiṟār. pāḻnilam piṟar karuttukkaḷi mēṉmip paṭuttuvatōṭu piṟitoṉṟākavum eliyaṭṭāl paṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

pāḻnilattil eliyaṭ tamatu uḷḷattuṇarccikaḷi nēraṭiyāka veḷippaṭuttāmal, avaṟṟiṟkup puṟanili oppīṭukaḷākat (Objective Co-relative) toṉmaṅkaḷilum, kaṭṭuk katikaḷilum iṭampeṟṟa pāḻnilaṅkaḷi, pāttirap paṭippukaḷiyum, nikaḻccikaḷiyum kōvip paṭuttik kāṭṭukiṟār. camutāyattil ellāt tuṟikaḷilum nilaviya malaṭṭut taṉamē pāḻnilamāka uruvakikkappaṭukiṟatu, kavaliyum, ēmāṟṟamum, pāluṇarvu vakkiramum, aṟivup pētalippum ēṟṟa pāttirap paṭippukaḷ mūlam tiṟamiyāka veḷippaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, ikkuṟikaḷ nīṅkip pāḻnilam ceḻikkac cila muyaṟcikaḷum caṭaṅkukaḷum mēṟkoḷḷap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ac caṭaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟutiyil vāṉattil mēkaṅkaḷ tiraḷkiṉṟaṉa. iṭimuḻakkam kēṭkiṟatu. maḻi varum, nāṭu ceḻikkum eṉṟa nampikkiyōṭu kāppiyam muṭikiṟatu. pāḻnilattil eliyaṭ pāluṇarvi aruvaruppōṭum, ataṉ eḻucciyit tuṉpattōṭum cittarippatōṭu, atik kuṟippiṭum iṭaṅkaḷilellām mukañcuḷittut tirumpik koḷkiṟār.

eliyaṭṭiṉ pāḻnilam paṟṟip palvēṟu vitamāṉa muraṇpaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ ilakkiya aṟiñarkaḷāl pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkaviti cilarāl cariyākavum, cilarāl tavaṟākavum purintukoḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. “ulakap pōrukkup piṉ vāḻnta, camutāyattiṉ maṉamayakkattiyum, kuḻappattiyum, āṉmīka vaṟaṭciyiyum ikkaviti kuṟippiṭukiṟatu” eṉṟu aṟiñar eḵp. ār. līvis eḻutukiṟār.

“ikkaviti camutāyattil nilaviya kōḷāṟukaḷiṉ cōtaṉiyē aṉṟittīrvu aṉṟu; itu maṟintu pōṉa pukaḻiyeṇṇi viṭukiṉṟa ilakkiyap perumūccu” eṉṟum. ‘itu pulamiyuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭa ilakkiya maravēli’ eṉṟum, ‘paṭitta muṭṭāḷ taṉattiṉ tokuppu’ eṉṟum palavāṟākap palvēṟu aṟiñarkaḷāl kaṇṭaṉam ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

ik kaṇṭaṉaṅkaḷukku māṟāka ‘ituvoru camutāyac cāṉṟup paṭṭayam (a Social document); naṭappu ulakattiṉar uṇmiyāṉa maṟupatippu’ eṉṟu pārāṭṭik kūṟuvōrum uḷar.

eliyaṭ, kavitikaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, nuṭpamāṉa ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷum, nallanāṭakaṅkaḷum eḻutiyirukkiṟār. eṉṟālum avarukku aḻiyāp pukaḻiyum, nōpel pariciyum tēṭit tantatu pāḻnilamē. tuvakka kālattil eliyaṭ. eḻutiya ‘pruḵp rōkkiṉ kātaṟ pāṭal’ The Love, Song of Alfred Prufrock mikavum viḷamparampeṟṟatu, ellārālum virumpip paṭikkappaṭuvatu. itippaṟṟi eḵp. ār. līvis kuṟippiṭumpōtu, “ikkātaṟ kaviti 19-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu ilakkiya marapiṟku muṟṟilum māṟāṉatu; puraṭcikaramāṉatu. itu eliyaṭṭiṉ putiya pāṇikkum, moḻinaṭikkum, kaviti uttikkum ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭu. kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum aruviyāka uṇarccikaḷiyum koṭṭi eliyaṭ iti veṟṟip paṭippāka ākkiyirukkiṟār” eṉṟu eḻutukiṟār.

ippāṭaliṉ talippē kuṟumputtaṉamāṉatu. ippāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ prūḵprāk ilaṇṭaṉ nākarika vāci; piñcilē vempip pōṉa naṭuttara vayatuk kaṉavāṉ. ippāṭaliṉ talippip pārttatum, ētō kātal nikaḻcci tāṉ itil iṭam peṟṟiruppatāka ellārum potuvākak karutuvar. āṉāl uṇmi ataṟku māṟāṉatu. pruḵprākkiṭam kātalum kiṭiyātu; kātali veḷippaṭuttum vīramum kiṭiyātu; tayakkamum, kuḻappamum, naramput taḷarcciyum mikka avaṉ peṇṇi neruṅkuvataṟkē accappaṭukiṟāṉ; piṉṉar kātali eṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭuttuvāṉ? ippāṭal eliyaṭ kālattil laṇṭaṉil vāḻnta nākarikak kaṉavāṉkaḷiṉ pōlittaṉattiyum kiyālākāt taṉattiyum eḷḷi nakiyāṭukiṟatu.

nēraṭiyākap paṅku koḷḷat tirāṇiyaṟṟu, vāḻkkiyi otuṅkiyiruntu vēṭikki pārttuc calittup pōṉa avaṉ,

‘eṉ vāḻkkiyik

kāḵpik karaṇṭiyāl

aḷantu-

calittup pōṉēṉ’





eṉṟu kaḻivirakkattōṭu pēcukiṟāṉ

‘mēci mītu

mayakkam koṭuttup

paṭukka vittirukkum

nōyāḷiyip pōla

viṇṇiṉ etirē

parappi vikkappaṭṭirukkum

inta māli nērattil

nīyum nāṉum puṟappaṭuvōm’





eṉṟa māli varuṇaṉiyōṭu pāṭal tuvaṅkukiṟatu. uṇarvum mayakkamum kalanta niliyil irukkum nōyāḷiyip pōla, pruḵprākkum aṟintum aṟiyātavaṉāka irukkum avala niliyi inta aḻakiya paṭimam kuṟippāka viḷakkukiṟatu.

‘vāṉampāṭikaḷiṉ naṭuvē sviṉi’ (Sweeny among the Nightingales) eṉṟa kavitiyil āṉmīkattil ēṟpaṭṭa nampikkik kuṟivālum, pāluṇarvu vakkarippālum cīr kulinta nakara vāḻkkiyik kuṟiyīṭukaḷāl viḷakkukiṟār eliyaṭ. ‘tiruc capik kōvil koli’ (Murder in the Cathedral) ‘kuṭumpattiṉ maṟupiṇippu’ (The Family Reunion) eṉpavi avar eḻutiya ciṟanta nāṭakaṅkaḷ.

eliyaṭ irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kaviñarkaḷuḷ atikamāka vimarcaṉam ceyyappaṭṭavar; vāḻum kālattilēyē perum ilakkiya mētiyāka matittu ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭavar; irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ āṅkilakkaviti naṭiyi aṭiyōṭu māṟṟi vittavar. ivaruṭiya vācakar vaṭṭam ciṟitu. āṉāl pukaḻ vaṭṭam periyatu.





↑ oru tāykkum makaṉukkum. tantikkum makaḷukkum. iṭiyē nilavum pāluṇarvuk kavarccikku uḷaviyal nipuṇar ḵprāyṭu, vitta peyar





* * *





eṉatu tīrppu nāḷil,

eṉatu iraṇṭu

ciṟakukaḷē pātukāppākat

tiṟanta mēṉiyōṭu niṟpēṉ.





merīṉā sveṭṭēvā

(1892—1941)



paṭippāṟṟal + aṟivu nuṭpam + kiṟukkuttaṉam = kaviñaṉ eṉṟa kaṇakku, merīṉā sveṭṭēvāvukku mikavum poruttamāṉa oṉṟu. avaḷ pullarikkum putirkkaṉavu ; elli tāṇṭip pūtta ilakkiya vacantam; yārum nirantaramākac contam koṇaṭāṭa muṭiyāta manta mārutam. kātali ārātitta cītamati. paṭṭiṉip pāliyil vāḻnta pōtum, taṉakkeṉṟu paṭittuk koṇṭa oyācisil ōyāmal kūvik koṇṭirunta oṟṟik kuyil.

sveṭṭēvā māskōvil pukaḻpeṟṟa pērāciriyarukkum, nākarikamikka oru maṅki nallāḷukkum makaḷākap piṟantavaḷ sviṭcarlāntilum teṟku jermaṉiyilum kalvi payiṉṟavaḷ; pāricu cārpōṉ palkalikkaḻakattil taṅkiyiruntu ulakap pēraṟiñarkaḷiṉ coṟpoḻivukaḷik kēṭṭuttāṉ ilakkiya aṟivi vaḷarttuk koṇṭavaḷ; ruciyak kaviñar volōviṉiṉ kavitiyāṟṟalāl īrkkappaṭṭu, kirimiyāvil avarukkuc contamāṉa kōkṭepel illattil taṅkiyiruntapōtu, ‘cerji eḵpraṉ’ eṉpavaric cantittu maṇantu iraṇṭu āṇ kuḻantikaḷiyum oru peṇ makaviyum peṟṟeṭuttavaḷ.

ruciyāvil uḷnāṭṭup pōr naṭantapōtu ivaḷ kaṇavaṉ cerji, jāriṉ veṇpaṭikku ātaravāka iruntāṉ. uḷ nāṭṭup pōril veṇpaṭi tōṟṟapōtu, ivaḷum ivaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉum ruciya nāṭṭiviṭṭu veḷiyēṟiṉar. ivaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉ cōviyat atikārikaḷukkup payantu talimaṟivu vāḻkki mēṟkoṇṭāṉ, pala iṭaṅkaḷil cuṟṟiyalintuviṭṭu 1922-il ceknāṭṭiṉ talinakarāṉa pirākuvil iruvarum oṉṟu cērntaṉar. mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ pirākuviṉ puṟanakaril vāḻnta piṟaku ivaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉ taṉ koḷkiyi māṟṟik koṇṭu sṭāliṉiṉ uḷavup paṭiyil cērntu paṇiyāṟṟiṉāṉ. ṭirāskiyiṉ makaṉuṭiya koliyil ivaṉukkum paṅkuṇṭu. araciyaṟpaṇi nimittam ivaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil cuṟṟiyalinta kāraṇattāl, sveṭṭēvāviṉ vāḻkki perumpālum taṉimiyilēyē kaḻintatu.

taṉimi palarōṭu kātal toṭarpu koḷḷum cūḻniliyi ivaḷukku uruvākkiyatu. 1915-16ām āṇṭukaḷil ruciyak kaviñar mēṇṭalsṭāmik kātalittu avaruṭaṉ vāḻntāḷ. 1925-il pirākuvil vāḻntapōtu, oru veṇpaṭi atikāriyik kātalittu avaruṭaṉ ōrāṇṭu vāḻntāḷ. piṟaku 1926-il taṉ kaṇavaṉ cerjiyuṭaṉ mīṇṭum cērntu koṇṭāḷ, pāricil vāḻnta kālattilum ivaḷukkup palarōṭu toṭarpiruntatu. ivaḷ ōyāmal kātalittāḷ: kātalikkappaṭṭāḷ; uṇarcci mikunta kātaṟkavitikaḷ eḻutik kuvittāḷ,

potuvuṭimik koḷkiyum, sṭāliṉiṉ carvātikāramum iṅḷukkuk kaṭṭōṭu piṭikkavilli. kaviñaṉukkum avaṉ paṭippukkum kaṭṭuppāṭu irukkak kūṭātu eṉṟu karutiṉāḷ. aṟiñarkaḷ cutantiramākac cintikka aṉumatikkap paṭavēṇṭum eṉpatu avaḷ koḷki. kaviñaṉiṉ kaṟpaṉikkum pāṭu poruḷukkum elli kaṭṭiya ruciya atikāravarkkatti veṟuttāḷ; avarkaḷukkut tuti pāṭiya ruciyak kaviñarkaḷic collampukaḷāl cāṭiṉāḷ.

puṣkiṉum alekcāṇṭar piḷākkum avaḷukkup piṭittamāṉa kaviñarkaḷ. akmaṭōvā, māyakōvski, pāsṭar nāk ākiyōrip pārāṭṭik kavitikaḷ eḻutiyirukkiṟāḷ. airōppiyak kaviñarkaḷuḷ jermāṉiyak kaviñarāṉa rilkki mika uyarnta kaviñarākap pōṟṟiṉāḷ; avarōṭu kaṭitat toṭarpum koṇṭāḷ.

māyakōvski makkaḷ kaviñaṉ, sveṭṭēvā taṉṉip paṟṟiyum, taṉ conta uṇarccikaḷiyum pāṭiyavaḷ. koḷkiyil iruvarum etiretir turuvaṅkaḷ. eṉṟālum māyakōvskiyiṉ paṭippāṟṟali sveṭṭēlāviyattu pōṟṟukiṟāḷ. 1928-il ‘ruciyāviṟkuppiṟaku’ (After Russia) eṉṟa pāṭali cariyakōvskikku urimiyākkiya pōtu ‘eṉṉippōl vēkak kālkaḷiyuṭiya kaviñaṉukku (To a poet as sleet of foot as I) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟāḷ. māyakōvski taṉakkuc ceyta utavikaḷi niṉivu kūrntu ‘māyakōvskikku’ eṉṟa talippil oru naṉṟik kaviti eḻutiṉāḷ sveṭṭēvā.

ciluvikaḷiyum

pukik kūṇṭukaḷiyum viṭa

uyaramāṉavaṉ;

tīppiḻampilum

pukik karukkalilum

taṉ peyaric

cūṭṭik koṇṭavaṉ,

taṭitta mēṉit tēvatūtaṉ

viḷāṭimir vāḻka!





eṉṟu uḷḷam kacintu appāṭalil māyakōvskiyi vāḻttukiṟāḷ. māyakōvskiyiṉ uyarnta tōṟṟattiyum, pērāṟṟaliyum, añcāmiyiyum niṉivu kūrum vaṇṇam ‘aṉparakkaṉ’ (Kind giant) eṉṟa toṭarāl avaṉi aṭikkaṭi kuṟippiṭukiṟāḷ.

māya kōvski 1927-ām āṇṭu pāricu nakaram ceṉṟirunta pōtu aṅkirunta ruciya māṇavarkaḷ vālṭiyar viṭutiyil (Cafe Voltire) oru kūṭṭam ēṟpāṭu ceytiruntaṉar. akkūṭṭattil māyakōvski tamatu kavitikaḷi vācittatōṭu, ruciya ilakkiyattiṉ vaḷarcci paṟṟiyum pēciṉāṉ. appōtu ‘ruciya veḷiyēṟikaḷ’ (emigres) anta viṭutikku muṉ kūṭi niṉṟu kuḻappam ceytu kūccaliṭṭaṉar. ik kūṭṭam naṭantapōtu sveṭṭēvā aṅkilli. eṉṟālum māya kōvskiyi ātarittu ‘yūrēci’ (Eurasie) eṉṟa ceytittāḷil ōr aṟikki veḷiyiṭṭāḷ. avvaṟikkiyip paṭitta ‘ruciya veḷiyēṟikaḷ’ sveṭṭēvāvit taṅkaḷ kūṭṭattiliruntu vilakki vittaṉar. taṅkaḷ ēṭukaḷilum avaḷuṭiya paṭippukkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvati niṟuttik koṇṭaṉar.

kaviñaṉiṉ ceyaṟpāṭṭiṉ mītum, avaṉ paṭippāṟṟal mītum perumatippum mariyātiyum vittirunta ruciyak kaviñar, sveṭṭēvāvippōl vēṟu yārumilli eṉṟu collalām. kaliyum vāḻkkiyum taṅkaḷukkuḷ parimāṟik koḷḷum koṭi paṟṟi, sveṭṭēvā āṇittaramāṉa koḷkikaḷi veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṟāḷ. ‘kaviti eṉpatu vili matippillāta cottu’ eṉṟum ‘kaviñaṉ camutāyattiṉ oppaṟṟa uṉṉata viḷivu’ eṉṟum taṉtu paṭippukkaḷil pala iṭaṅkaḷil tīrppu vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṟāḷ.

sveṭṭēvāviṉ uḷḷattil nīṅkāta iṭampeṟṟa ruciyap peruṅkaviñar alekcāṇṭar piḷāk, tām iṟappataṟkuc cila nāṭkaḷ muṉpāka māskō kaliyaraṅkam oṉṟil tamatu kavitikaḷi vācittār. atippaṟṟit taṉatu kaviti yoṉṟil kuṟippiṭṭa sveṭṭēvā,

taṉimiyil aṭikkappaṭṭa

ciṟik kitiyiṉ

ēkāntap pēccip pōlavum

allatu-

uṟakkattil

tāṉāka veḷippaṭum

kuḻantiyiṉ pēccippōlavum

alekcāṇṭar piḷākkiṉ

puṉita itayam

antap periya araṅkil

taṉṉi

veḷippaṭuttik koṇṭiruntatu





eṉṟu eḻutukiṟāḷ. ‘kavitiyiṉ muḻumiyāṉa kuṟikkōḷ itayam’ eṉpatu sveṭṭēvāviṉ karuttu ‘itayamum āṉmāvum calaṉamaṟṟu irukkumpōtu, kaviti tāṉākap poṅkip perukkeṭuttu ōṭivaravēṇṭum’ eṉṟa karutti piḷākkippaṟṟiya pāṭalil kuṟippiṭukiṉṟāḷ. taṉatu tattikkaviti (telegram style poetry) paṟṟi oru naṇparukku eḻutiya kaṭitattil ‘vēkamākat tuḷikkappaṭuvatu āṉmā’ (Pierced to the quick = the Soul) eṉṟu eḻutukiṟāḷ.

inta vēkak kaviti muṟiyi mutaṉ mutalil kiyāṇṭavaṉ māyakōvski. iṭattiṟkēṟṟa ōcinayam, curukkam, muraṭṭut toṭarkaḷ, oliyaḻuttam ākiyavi ikkavitip paṇpukaḷ. ippaṇpukaḷik kavitiyil koṇṭuvara māyakōvski varikaḷi uṭittu ēṇippaṭiyāka (the Step-adder effect) aṭukkuvāṉ; sveṭṭēvā iṇippuk kōṭu (—) koṭuttu eḻutuvāḷ. sveṭṭēvāviṉ tantikkaviti:

kuḻappam-parappātīrkaḷ

itayam-taḷartti viṭuṅkaḷ

muḻaṅkikaḷum-taliyum

muḻaṅkikaḷum-uḷḷamum

iḷami-kātalum arppaṇippum

mutumi-tūkkamum koṭṭāviyum

vāḻvataṟku nēramilli:

tappa muṭiyātu.





kēṭṭavuṭaṉ uḷḷattil tittuppāyum uṇarccik kavitikaḷip pāṭavalla peruṅkaviñarkaḷ ivvulakil atika nāḷ vāḻvatilli eṉpatu avaḷ kaṇippu. avvāṟu iḷamiyilē tamatu vāḻvi muṭittuk koṇṭa puṣkiṉ, viḷāk pōṉṟa ruciyak kaviñarkaḷi eṇṇik kaṇṇīr viṭukiṟāḷ sveṭṭēvā. “puṣkiṉ añcāmi, perumitam, nērmi, taṉimi, ūḻ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ oṭṭu motta viḷakkam” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟāḷ. ip paṇpukaḷi mikutiyāka virumpiyatōṭu, taṉ vāḻvilum aḷavukku mīṟik kaṭip piṭittāḷ.

oru kaviñaṉuṭiya paṇiyi, mikappuṉitamāṉa oṉṟāka avaḷ matittāḷ. 1921-ām āṇṭil eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭa kali (Craft) eṉṟa kaṭṭurit tokuppil ōr iḷaṅkaviñaṉiṉ.

kavitiyē!

eṉ turatirṣṭamē!

eṉ celvamē:

eṉ puṉitakkaliyē!





eṉṟa varikaḷi mēṟkōḷāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟāḷ. kavitiyit ‘turatirṣṭam’ eṉṟu anta iḷaṅkaviñaṉ kuṟippiṭṭataṟkuk kāraṇam. uṇṭu. kaviti ellārukkum pukaḻiyum celvattiyum vārik koṭuttu viṭavilli. nalla kaviti eḻutavēṇṭum, eṉpataṟkākac cilar taṅkaḷ vāḻkki vacatikaḷiyum, varuvāyiyum, makiḻcciyiyum tiyākam ceyvati iṉṟum nām kāṇkiṟōm. kavitikku ivarkaḷ koṭukkum vili ivi. itu kaviñaṉukku oru vaki turatirṣṭam tāṉē?

orutaramāṉa kavitiyi eḻuti muṭippataṟku ecu perumāṉ ciluviyil paṭṭa tuṉpatti oru kaviñaṉ aṉupavippatāka sveṭṭēvā karutukiṟāḷ. taṉṉuṇarccik kaviti (Lyric poetry)t tokuppoṉṟi eḻuti muṭittavuṭaṉ, tāṉ paṭṭa aṉupavattik kaviñar pāsṭar nākkiṟkuk kaṭitamāka eḻutiyapōtu, 'nāṉ mikavum kaḷittuviṭṭēṉ; āciris[1] tuṇṭu tuṇṭāka veṭṭi noṟukki eṟiyappaṭṭatu pōl nāṉum uṇarkiṟēṉ. nāṉ eḻutum ovvoru kaviti nūlum eṉṉiliruntu veṭṭiyeṭukkappaṭṭa tuṇṭu; tāṅkik koḷḷamuṭiyāta ēkkap pirivu! camayat tūtar tāmasiṉ viralkaḷ[2] ēcuviṉ puṇkaḷit toṭṭuppārittatu pōl, nāṉ oru kavitikkum maṟṟoru kavitikkum iṭiyil tīṇṭip pārkkiṟēṉ“ eṉṟu uṇarcci poṅkik kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

avaḷ paṭippukkaḷil cik kavitikaḷ (Psyche_poems) māskō kaviti (Verse about Moscow), ceṅkutiri mītu (Astride a Red Horse) eṉpavi kuṟippiṭattakkavi . ciṟumi jār (The Girl Tsar) ekoruks (Egarukså) eṉṟa iraṇṭum kuḻantikaḷukkāṉa nīṇṭa kaṟpaṉik kavitikaḷ (Fairy tale poems), uḷḷaṟiviṉ veḷiccattil kali (Art in the Light of Conscience) eṉṟa kaṭṭuri nūlil, kavitikaḷip paṟṟiya nuṭpamāṉa cintaṉikaḷi sveṭṭēvā potintu vittirukkiṟāḷ. taṉ uḷḷattil kaviti uruvākum paṭippu rakaciyattik kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu atil viḷakkiyirukkiṟāḷ:

"eṉ uḷḷattil oḻuṅkiṟkuṭpaṭṭa oṉṟu ōyāmal olittuk koṇṭiruppatik kēṭkiṟēṉ. atu cila camayaṅkaḷil eṉakku āṇiyiṭuvatu pōlavum, cila camayaṅkaḷil yōcaṉi kūṟuvatu pōlavum irukkum. āṇiyiṭum pōtu appaṭiyē ēṟṟuk koḷḷuvēṉ; yōcaṉi kūṟum pōtu vivātippēṉ.

"āṇiyiṉ ōci taṉittaṉmi mikkatākavum, māṟṟa muṭiyātatākavum, iṭappeyarcci ceyya muṭiyātatākavum irukkum; atuvē kavitiyiṉ cāram. yōcaṉi oliyō, kavitiyiṉ cantappāti. appōtu nāṉ ōciyiṉimiyit tāṉ kēṭkiṟēṉ; coṟkaḷi alla. coṟkaḷi nāṉ tēṭiyāka vēṇṭum; cila coṟkaḷiṉ vēkattikkūṭṭa vēṇṭum; kaṭṭuppaṭutta vēṇṭum. ivitāṉ nāṉ uṇarum yōcaṉiyiṉ vaḻiyākappeṟupavi.

“eṉ eḻuttukkaḷ ellāmē eṉ kēṭkum ceyal, itaṉāl nāṉ eḻutuvataṟku muṉpāka eḻutiyavaṟṟit tiruppit tiruppippaṭikkiṟēṉ. muṉṉāl irukkum irupatu varikaḷippaṭikkāmal nāṉ putiyavari etiyum eḻutuvatilli. ēṟkaṉavē muṟṟākavum, cariyākavum elkō eḻutivikkappaṭṭa kavitiyi niṉivu kūrntu, ataṟkut tuvakkattiliruntu muḻumiyum paravacappaṭuttum iṉimiyum cantanayamum kūṭiya ōviyattip paṭippatu pōṉṟa ōr uṇarvip peṟukiṟēṉ, uṇmiyic coṉṉāl, eṉatu toḻil eṉakkuḷ kiḷampum oliyic cariyākat tulliyamākak kēṭpatutāṉ vēṟu etuvumilli”

◯

ivvulaka vāḻviṉ aṭittaḷattil putintu kiṭakkum taṉatu āṉmāviṉ elliyaṟṟa uṇarvukaḷi veḷippaṭuttat toṭarcciyaṟṟa-aṅkum iṅkumāṉa-coṟ kōviyiyum collāṭciyiyum sveṭṭēvā kiyāṇṭāḷ. tāṉ kiyāṇṭa vaṭivamum, uttiyum, cāramum piṟarāl eḷitākap purintu koḷḷa muṭiyātavi eṉpati avaḷ aṟintiruntālum coṟkaḷiyum ulakattiyum ivvulakattiyum maṟuulakattiyum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭa virumpāmal, cikkalāṉa kuṟiyīṭukaḷākavē veḷippaṭuttiṉāḷ.

uyirōṭṭamuḷḷa coṟkaḷik kavitikaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟi urinaṭiyilum payaṉpaṭuttiṉāḷ sveṭṭēvā. ac coṟkaḷ mikunta uyirt tuṭippōṭu, virivum, paṉmuka māṟṟamum peṟak kūṭiyavi. ataṉāl avaḷ karuttukkaḷip purintu koṇṭu toṭarntu celvatu paṭippavarkku mikavum kaṭiṉamāka iruntatu. taṉ eḻuttil tēviyaṟṟa iṭic coṟkaḷi yellām nīkkiviṭṭu eḻutiṉāḷ. avaḷ kiyāṇṭa uvamikaḷ kūṭa muḻumiyāṉa oṟṟumit taṉmiyi veḷippaṭuttuvaṉavāka illi. avaḷ paṭippukkaḷil oru vēkamāṉa avacaram kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. taṉṉi iḻantu muṟṟilum paṭippōṭu oṉṟum āḻnta tēviyiliruntu tōṉṟiya avacaram atu. ‘uḷḷaṟiviṉ veḷiccattil kali’ eṉṟataṉatu nūlil ‘taṉṉi iḻakkum iṉpam’ (The bliss of annihilation) eṉṟu avaḷ atik kuṟippiṭukiṟāḷ.

1916-il avaḷ eḻutiya kavitiyoṉṟil ‘eṉṉi muṟṟilumāka nāṉ ellārukkum oppaṭippēṉ’ eṉṟu eḻutukiṟāḷ. avvāṇṭu avaḷ eḻutiya kavitikaḷil avaḷuṭiya koṭik kuṇamē mēlōṅkik kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ruciyat talinakarāṉa māskōvi, kaviñar piḷāk, akmaṭōvā māṇṭalsṭām ākiyōrukkuk koṭiyāka vaḻaṅkukiṟāḷ. 1918-il eḻutiya kātaṟ kavitikaḷilum cik, kavitikaḷilum avaḷuṭiya koṭittaṉmiyē malintirukkak kāṇalām:

uṉatu pēṉā eḻutum

pakkam nāṉ

nāṉ oru veḷḷippakkam;

ellāvaṟṟiyum

ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟēṉ.



nī vaḻaṅkum poruḷkaḷukku

nāṉē poṟuppāḷi.

avaṟṟi-

nūṟu maṭaṅkākat

tiruppik koṭuppēṉ



nāṉ oru ciṟṟūr

kaṟuppumaṇ.





eṉakku oḷiyum

īramaḻiyum nītāṉ.



nī-

eṉatu tēvaṉ;

talivaṉ.

nāṉ-

kaṟuppumaṇ :

veḷḷip pakkam.





taṉṉit tārāḷamākat taṉatu 'talivar'kaḷiṭam oppaṭittuk koṇṭa ittaṉmiyāl, tāṉ matittup pōṟṟiya 'kaviñar akma ṭōvā' viṭamiruntu ivaḷ vēṟupaṭukiṟāḷ. ilakkiya vātikaḷ akmaṭōvāvip 'pātip paratti; pātit tuṟavi' eṉpar, sveṭṭēvāvuai 'muḻup paratti' eṉpar

1920 - il avaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa maṟṟoru kavitiyil

eṉṉiṭam-

pēcat tēviyilli.

itō eṉ itaḻkaḷ,

arunta!

itō eṉ kūntal,

varuṭa!

itō eṉ kikaḷ,

muttamiṭa!

illiyeṉṟāl...

nām uṟaṅkuvōm!





eṉṟu tuṇiccalāka, nāṇamiṉṟip pāṭukiṟāḷ.

enta oru putuk kaviñarum kātaṟpiriviṉāl ēṟpaṭum tuṉpaṅkaḷi sveṭṭēvāvippōl uṇarcci poṅkap pāṭavilli eṉṟu niccayamākak kūṟalām (1921-il uḷ nāṭṭup pōril kaṇavaṉip pirinta pōtum, 1925-il pirākuvil taṉ kātalaṉip pirintapōtum, avaḷ pāṭiya kātaṟ kavitikaḷ paṭippavar uḷḷatti uṇarccip piḻampākkum taṉmiyaṉa. anta vakiyil avaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa ‘malippāṭṭu’ (Poem of the Mountin), iṟutippāṭṭu (Poem of the End) iraṇṭum kuṟippiṭattakkavi ‘iṟutip pāṭṭil’ - ‘pirivu’ eṉṉum col māskōvil uḷḷa nāṟpatu muṟikku nāṟpatu mātākōviliṉ' (Forty times forty churches) maṇiyōci pōlac cōkamāka olikkiṉṟaṉa. sveṭṭēvāviṉ kātaṟkavitikaḷ paṭippavar uḷḷattil “ōr uṟavi-nerukkamāṉa uṟavi avaḷōṭu oṉṟākap paṭukkiyil uṟivataṟkum mēlāṉa uṟavip” pulappaṭuttuvatāka ōr āciriyar kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.

oru kavitiyil taṉatu āṉmā veṟipiṭitta niliyilum, oṉṟukkum kaṭṭup paṭāta niliyilum iruppatākak kuṟippiṭukiṟāḷ sveṭṭēvā. taṉṉittāṉē tuṉpuṟuttik koḷḷum oru mata veṟik kūṭṭattit (Flagellants)★ taṉatu āṟṟal mikka urinaṭi nūlāl pārāṭṭip pukaḻntiruppatu avaḷ uḷḷattil paṭarntirunta veṟiyuṇarcciyiṉ viḷivē ākum. taṉatu ellimīṟiya ceyalkaḷāl ‘kiṟukkup piṭitta kaviñar’ eṉṟa peyariyum ivaḷ peṟukiṟāḷ.(ivaḷuṭiya) kavitiyāṟṟalum, aṟivu nuṭpamum viyakkattakka muṟiyil amintiruntālum taṉakkuppiṭikkātavarkaḷi sveṭṭēvā kaṭumiyākat tākki eḻuti virukkiṟāḷ. ruciya ilakkiya vātikaḷ ivaḷuṭiya tākkutali valippuppēccāka (hysterics) varuṇikkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟālum, taṉatu ellimīṟiya taṉmiyi (extremity) ottuk koḷvatōṭu, atittāṉ veṟṟi koḷḷum niliyil iruppatāka, sveṭṭēvā kūṟuvatu uḷḷatti nekiḻviaikkiṟatu. kirēkkakkatikaḷil varum cik[3] (Psyche) eṉṟa tēvatiyē taṉṉuruvil maṟupiṟavi eṭuttu mīṇṭum taṉatu atirccikkuriya vāḻkkip payaṇattit toṭṅkiyiruppatāka orukavitiyil kuṟippiṭukiṟāḷ. kīḻkkaṇṭa varikaḷ avaḷuṭiya maṉakkuḻappattiyum atirccip payaṇattiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa:

coṟkaḷ-

kaṟuppu vāṉil

cetukkappaṭṭaṉa.

aḻakiya viḻikaḷ

kuruṭākkappaṭṭaṉa.



cāvup paṭukki

eṅkaḷukku

accan taravilli,

kātaṟ paṭukkiyum

eṅkaḷukku

iṉpamāka illi





kiṟittava camayattil oru piriviṉar. tamatu kuṟṟaṅkaḷukkut taṇṭaṉi vaḻaṅkat tāṅkaḷē taṅkaḷic cāṭṭiyāl aṭittut tuṉpuṟuttik koḷḷum iyalpiṉar ivarkaḷ ceyali svieṭṭēvar pārāṭṭi eḻutiyirukkiṟāḷ. viyarvi kaciyum eḻuttu

ōyāta-

cintaṉi mēyccal,



nāṅkaḷ-

vēṟoru

neruppi aṟivōm

eṅkaḷ ucciyil

nāṭṭiyamāṭum

melitāṉa tīkkoḻuntu

ōr āvi...

ōr tūṇṭutal.





‘uḷḷaṟiviṉ veḷiccattil kali’ eṉṟa kaṭṭuri nūlil kaliyi iyaṟkikku oppiṭukiṟāḷ. “iyaṟki eppaṭi ellāvaṟṟukkum mūlamākavum, iyaṅku caktiyākavum: paṭippāṟṟalākavum, aḻivuc caktiyākavum irukkiṉṟatō atēpōl kaliyum viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu” eṉṟu kuṟippīṭukiṟāḷ.

potu oḻukkamum, matamum vaṟpuṟuttum maṉaccāṭciyi avaḷ ēṟṟuk koḷvatilli. mōṭca narakaṅkaḷ oruvarukkup paḻaviṉiyiṉpaṭi nirṇayikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭiyum avaḷ ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilli.

iyaṟkiyiṉ aṭippaṭit tattuvaṅkaḷi maṟutalippatu tāṉ kaviñaṉukku narakam ceyaṟkiyil paṭivatu kalāccārattic cīrkulittu, oḻukkak kēṭṭi viḷivikkum' eṉṟu kūṟukiṟāḷ sveṭṭēvā.

sveṭṭēvāvukku cāvup paṭukki eppōtum, accattik koṭuttatilli, 1915-il avaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa ‘nāṉ uṇmiyi aṟivēṉ’ (I Know the truth) eṉṟa pāṭalil ‘cāvu eṉpatu vāḻakki eṉṉum tūkkamiṉmi nōyiliruntu (insomia of tiving) oruvar tēṭum viṭutali eṉṟu kūṟiccāviyum vāḻviyum camaṉappaṭuttukiṟāḷ. eṉṟālum ruciyap puraṭcik kaviñaṉ māyakōvski taṟkoli ceytu koṇṭa pōtum, hiṭlar cekkōslēviyā mītu paṭiyeṭuttu annāṭṭu makkaḷik koṉṟu kuvitta pōtum, cāviṉ koṭumiyi eṇṇip pulampi aḻukiṟāḷ, sveṭṭēvā. ati eḻutu mēci (The Desk) eṉṟa uṇarccik kuṟippukaḷāka eḻutukiṟāḷ. avaṟṟuḷ avaḷuṭiya añcāmiyum, nērmiyum, perumitamum, taṉimiyuṇarvum potintirukkak kāṇalām ak kuṟippiṉ kaṭici varikaḷ:

eṉatu tīrppunāḷil

eṉatu-





iraṇṭu ciṟakukaḷē

pātukāppākat

tiṟanta mēṉiyōṭu niṟpēṉ.





patiṉēḻu āṇṭukaḷ nāṭukaṭantu airōppiya nāṭukaḷil vāḻnta piṟaku, 1939- il taṉ kaṇavaṉit tēṭi sveṭṭēvā mīṇṭum ruciyāviṟku vantāḷ. neṭunāṭkaḷākak kāṇa virumpiya kaviñar akmaṭōvāvi nēril cantittāḷ. appōtu ruciyā mīṇṭum jermaṉiyōṭu iraṇṭām ulakap pōril īṭupaṭṭiruntatu. ruciya atikārikaḷ ellāriyum aiyattōṭu pārttaṉar. aiyattukkuḷḷāṉavarkaḷi irakkamillāmal koṉṟaṉar. sveṭṭēvāviṉ paḻiya varalāṟṟi aṟinta yārum avaḷi ātarikkamuṉ varavilli. vayiṟṟup piḻippukkākak kūlikku moḻipeyarppu vēlikaḷ ceytāḷ. avaḷuṭiya kuṭumpac cottukkaḷ ellām ruciya aracāṅkattāl paṟimutal ceyyappaṭṭu ellārukkum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. jermaṉ paṭikaḷ nāṭṭiṉ ellikkuḷ nuḻintu viṭṭapaṭiyāl, sveṭṭēvāvum māskōvi viṭṭu ‘elāpukā’ eṉṟa uḷnāṭṭu nakarukkuk kuṭipeyarttāḷ. aṅkup piḻikka vaḻiyiṉṟic curukkiṭṭuk koṇṭu iṟantāḷ. ruciya nāṭṭiṉ pukaḻpeṟṟu kaviñarkaḷuḷ oruvarāṉa sveṭṭēvā oru cātāraṇa iṭukāṭṭil entac ciṟappumillāmal putikkappaṭṭāḷ.

kaviñar mēṇṭal sṭāmiṉ maṉivi ‘nā teḻṭā’ sveṭṭēvāvippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭumpōtu, “merīṉā sveṭṭēvāviṉ muṭivippōl tuyarantarak kūṭiyatu ētumilli... avaḷukkut tēvippaṭṭatellām, avaḷuṭiya ovvōr uṇarviyum kaṭici ellivari aṉupavittup pārppatutāṉ” eṉṟu eḻutukiṟāḷ.

ruciyap peruṅ kaviñarāṉa pāsṭar nāk, sveṭṭēvāviṉ iṟappukku varunti eḻutiya kallaṟip pāṭṭil “avaḷ uṇmik kuṭimakaḷāka viḷaṅkiya orē nāṭu- kaviti” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṟār.





↑ ekiptiyap purāṇak kaṭavuḷ āciris. avaṉ maramāka iruttapōtu etirikaḷāl tuṇṭu tuṇṭāka veṭṭic citikkappaṭṭāṉ. ammarat tuṇṭukaḷik kaṭṭiliṉ kālkaḷākac ceytaṉar. ācirisip pirinta avaṉ maṉivi ocis(Usis)oru paṟaviyāka māṟit taṉ kaṇavaṉ iruppiṭattik

↑ tāmas ēcuviṉ cīṭar, apōlastar. ēcu uyirt teḻuntati nampāmal, avaruṭiya puṇkaḷit tamatu viralkaḷāl toṭṭuppār nampiyavar. itaṉāl ivaric cantēkat tāmas (doubting Thomas) caṉṟu viviliyam kūṟum.

↑ kirēkka purāṇattil āṉmā, vaṇṇattuppūcciyiṉ, ciṟakukaḷōṭu peṇṇāka uruvakappaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu. antap peṇ kirēkkak kātaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa īrāsliṉ (Eros) kātaliyāka kūṟappaṭukiṟāḷ. anta ‘cikkōtēvati’ taṉṉuruvil ivvulakil tōṉṟi mīṇṭum taṉ vāḻkkip payaṇattit toṭaṅkiyiruppatāka sveṭṭēvā kūṟukiṟāṉ. ataṉāltāṉ taṉatu taṉatu eḻuttu mēcikkuṟippil iraṇṭu ciṟakukaḷē pātukāppākat tiṟanta mēṉiyōṭu niṟpēṉ eṉṟu eḻutukiṟāḷ.





* * *





nāṉ ippōtu ciṟiyavaṉ.

yārum etirkka muṭiyātapaṭi

eṉṉuḷ oruvaṉ

uruvākik koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ





viḷāṭimir māyakōvski

(1893–1930)



māyakōvski taṉ tantiyiṉ piṟanta nāḷil (jūli 7) piṟantāṉ. eṉavē iruvar piṟanta nāṭkaḷum vīṭṭil oṉṟākavē koṇṭāṭppaṭṭaṉa.

aintu vayatilēyē, taṉ vayatukku atikamāṉa pāṭalkaḷi maṉappāṭam ceytu kūṟum āṟṟalip peṟṟiruntāṉ. avaṉ virumpippaṭitta mutal nūl ṭāṉ kuvik cāṭ.

‘oru pakkam kaviti. maṟṟoru pakkam puraṭci. kavitiyum puraṭciyum eṉ uḷḷattil piṉṉippiṇintu viṭṭāṉ’ eṉṟu taṉatu iḷamik kuṟippil avaṉ, eḻutiyirukkiṟāṉ. taṉatu paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatilēyē kāliyil kaṇ viḻittatum, ‘ceytittāḷ vantu viṭṭatā?’ eṉṟu tāṉ mutalil kēṭpāṉ.

taṉatu 16 ām vayatiṟkuḷ mūṉṟu muṟi ciṟikkuc ceṉṟirukkiṟāṉ. ciṟip paṟaviyāy iruntapōtu kāval tuṟiyiṉr avaṉukkuc cūṭṭiya tirunāmam ‘neṭṭiyaṉ.’ uyarattil avaṉ oru kākacas: 6 aṭi 8 aṅkulam.

vīṭṭi viṭṭuk kiḷampumpōtu vaḻakkamāṉa avaṉ mucumucut toppiyit (Fur cap) taliyil aṇintatum avaṉ vāy muṇu muṇukkum vālipap pāṭal varikaḷ:

payaṉaṟṟa āli mutalāḷi-vīṭṭil

paṭuttiruppāṉ eppōtum

cōmpēṟi! kāli -

āmām paṭuttiruppāṉ eppōtum

cōmpēṟi! kāli!





1909-ām āṇṭil avaṉ patiṉāṟu vayatuk kaṭṭiḷaṅkāḷi. noviṉskayā ciṟic cāliyil aṭipaṭṭu irunta 13 peṇ araciyaṟ kitikaḷ tappic celvataṟku avaṉ tuṇi purintāṉ. akkitikaḷ māṟuvēṭattil tappiccella avaṉ tāyum cakōtarikaḷum paḷḷic cīruṭi tittuk koṭuttaṉar. ataṟkāka avaṉ ciṟippaṭuttappaṭṭāṉ. piṉṉar ‘miṉar’ eṉṟu viṭutali ceyyap paṭṭāṉ.

ciṟiyil iruntapōtutāṉ kāntiyaṭikaḷukku aṟap pōrippaṟṟiya ñāṉōtayam piṟantatu. ciṟiyil irunta pōtu tāṉ etirkālattil tāṉ camikkavirunta camatarumak kaliyip paṟṟiya ñāṉōtayam avaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭatu.

ṣellikallūriyil paṭittuk koṇṭiruntapōtu ‘nāttikattiṉ avaciyam’ (The Necessity of Atheism) kaṭṭuri eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭataṟkākak kallūri nirvākattāl veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭāṉ. kalikkallūriyil payiṉṟapōtu paḻiya marapukkaliyi (Bourgeois Art) ikaḻntu pēciyataṟkākak kallūri nirvākattāl veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭāṉ, māyakōvski. kalikkallūriyi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭapōtu avaṉ tāy nī eppaṭiyāvatu intappaṭippi (Painting) muṭittiruntāl naṉṟāyiruntirukkum. eṉṟu varuttattōṭu coṉṉār. ataṟku avaṉ coṉṉāṉ:

“ōviyat toḻil ceyyat taṉiyāka oru kūṭamum (Studio) tiric cīlikaḷum (canvas) vaṇṇaṅkaḷum tūrikikaḷum iṉṉum palavum tēvippaṭum. āṉāl kaviti eḻuta paḻiya nōṭṭupputtakam oṉṟu iruntālpōtum; enta iṭattilum uṭkārntu koṇṭu eḻutalām. eṉavē nāṉ kaviñaṉākap pōkiṟēṉ.” eṉṟu avaṉ coṉṉapaṭi mākaviñaṉ ākiviṭṭāṉ.

“cilar piṟakkumpōtē pōrāṭṭakkārarkaḷākap piṟkakiṟārkaḷ; avarkaḷuḷ māyakōvskiyum oruvaṉ. ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa putukkaviñar mukāmil māyakōvski oru pōrāṭṭakkāraṉ” eṉṟum, “ruciyā peṟṟeṭutta kaviñarkaḷuḷ māyakōvski pērāṟṟalum tīviramāṉa pōrkkuṇamum, eḷitil piṟarāl veṟṟikoḷḷa muṭiyāta uṟutippāṭum vāykkap peṟṟavaṉ” eṉṟum kaviñar sveṭṭēvā kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṟār. ‘putiya ruciyāvil peruṅkāppiyamum taṉṉuṇarvuk kavitiyum’ (The Epic and the Lyric in Modern Russia) eṉṟa taṉatu kaṭṭuriyil kaviñar pāsṭar nākkit taṉṉuṇarvukkavitiyeṉṟum, māyakōvskiyip peruṅkāppiyam eṉṟum kuṟippiṭṭa sveṭṭēvā avviruvariyum ‘kaviñaruḷ aticayikkattakkavarkaḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

“kālaṅ kaṭanta paṭippukkaḷip pōṟṟip pātukāttu vaḻipaṭum, uyiraṟṟa kalip poruṭ kāṭcic cāli namakkut tēviyilli; terukkaḷilum, vaṇṭippātikaḷilum, toḻiṟ cālikaḷilum, paṇimaṉikaḷilum, pāṭṭāḷikaḷiṉ illaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭum maṉita uṇarvukaḷiṉ uyirt tuṭippāṉa toḻiṟ kūṭaṅkaḷ tēvi” eṉṟu māya kōvski eḻutiṉāṉ. ittakiya cintaṉikaḷ iḷam māya kōvskiyi oru cuṟu cuṟuppāṉa araciyalvātiyāka uruvākkiṉa. kuliyi viṭutalikkuriya oru pōrkkaruviyāka māṟṟi aṭimit taḷiyil cikkit tavitta ēḻikaḷiṉ kiyil koṭukka virumpiṉāṉ. kali mēṭṭuk kuṭiyiṉarukkē uriyatu eṉṟa kaṉavit takartteṟivatutāṉ avaṉ tiṭṭam.

‘paḻamiyiṉ maṟumalarcciyē paṇpāṭu’ eṉṟa koḷkiyi avaṉ ottuk koḷḷavilli. uṟpattip perukkamum, makkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum poruḷkaḷāka mūlap poruḷkaḷi māṟṟiyamikkum tiṭṭamiṭṭa toḻil tiṟamiyumē paṇpāṭu eṉṟu putu viḷakkam koṭuttāṉ māyakōvski.

oḷimikka āṉmīka ulakip paṟṟi ōyāmal pēcik koṇṭiruppatik kiviṭṭu viṭṭuk kaliyāṉatu veṭkappaṭāmal ulakiyalip paṟṟiyum, maṉitaṉiṉ aṭippaṭit tēvikaḷip paṟṟiyum pēcavēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉāṉ. kali, maṉita uṇarvukaḷippaṟṟiyatāka irukka vēṇṭumē tavira, kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭāta ētō oṉṟip paṟṟiyatāka irukkakkūṭātu eṉpatu avṉ karuttu. mēlum, atu taṉippaṭṭa oru maṉitaṉiṉ uṇarcci veḷippāṭāka iruppati viṭa, koḷkikaḷiṉ kūṭṭuk kuralāka olikkumpōtu ataṉ poruttam pulappaṭum eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ.

kaliyippaṟṟit tāṉ veḷippaṭuttiya karuttukkaḷ makkaḷiṭiyil paravi vērūṉṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟāl avaṟṟi aṟikki mūlam viḷamparappaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutiya māyakōvski 1912-il ‘jaṉarañcakattiṉ kaṉṉattil ōr aṟi’ (A slap In The Face of Public Taste) eṉṟa āttiramūṭṭum aṟikkiyoṉṟi veḷiyiṭṭāṉ. ruciyarkaḷ mikavum pōṟṟi matitta ilakkiyavātikaḷāṉa puṣkiṉ, ṭāsṭāvski, ṭālsṭāy ākiyōrit tamatu puttilakkiyap pōrkkappalil iruntu tūkki eṟiyumāṟu iḷantali muṟiyiṉarukku atil āvēcakkaṭṭaḷi iṭṭiruntāṉ. taṉatu puraṭcik koḷkiyi uḷḷaṭakki ataṟku ‘muṉṉōkkiyam’ (Futurism) eṉṟu peyarum koṭuttāṉ. camutāyattiṉ aṭimaṭṭac cūḻniliyiliruntu ikkalivaṭivam uruppeṟṟālum, enta ulaki nōkki itu pēcukiṟatō anta ulaki aṭiyōṭu māṟṟiyamikkum uyirp pērāṟṟalāka viḷaṅkuvatāka avaṉ taṉ aṟikkiyil kuṟippiṭṭāṉ.

1914-il avaṉ pēciya ōr ilakkiyap pēccil “muṉṉōkkiyak kaviti eṉpatu nakarattippaṟṟiya kaviti. taṟkāla ulakiṉ caṅkaṭattiyum kāyccaliyum ...piratipalikkum taṟkāla nakarattiṉ kaviti. nakarattiṉ vaḷivil āṟṟoḻukkō, aḷaviṭappaṭṭa kōṭukaḷō illi. kōṇaṅkaḷum, oṭicalkaḷum, kōṇalmāṇalkaḷumē nakariṉ amippi uruvākkukiṉṟaṉa”, eṉṟu viḷakkam toṭuttāṉ. kāṭcip poruḷkaḷiṉ mītu māyakōvski koṭutta karuttaḻuttam mikavum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. māyakōvski taṉatu māskō nakaravāḻkakiyi, ōr ōviya māṇavaṉākat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

māyakōvskikkut taṉatu āṟṟal mītum, tāṉ toṭaṅkiya puraṭci iyakkattiṉ āṟṟal mītum aḷavukaṭantu nampikkiyum taṉmatippum iruntaṉa māyakōvskiyiṉ kavitiyāṟṟalippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭa sveṭṭivā, "māyakōvski taṉatu mutal kavitiyi eḻutat toṭaṅkiya kālattiliruntē oru muṉṉōkkiyak kaviñaṉ; piṟaviyilēyē pērāṟṟal vāykkap peṟṟavaṉ. iḷamiyilēyē tammuḷ kontaḷittuk koṇṭirunta āṟṟali avaṉ uṇarntiruntāṉ. āṉāl anta āṟṟal ettakiyatu eṉṟu avaṉukkut teriyātu. ‘nāṉ’ eṉṟa taṉmuṉippu avaṉukkatikam. makkaḷ avaṉip pārttu ‘yārnī’? eṉṟu kēṭṭapōtu nāṉ viḷāṭimir māyakōvski eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. ‘viḷāṭimir māyakōvski yār?’ eṉṟu avarkaḷ kēṭṭapōtu nāṉtāṉ! ippōtu itit tavira vēṟu patil illi. āṉāl etirkālam eṉṉip paṟṟic cariyāṉa patili uṅkaḷukkuk kūṟum eṉṟu iṟumāppōṭu kūṟiṉāṉ” eṉṟu māyakōvskiyiṉ iṟappiṟkup piṉ eḻutiya oru kaṭṭuriyil kūṟiyirukkiṟāḷ.

māyakōvskiyiṉ toṭakkakālak kavitikaḷil avaṉuṭiya elliyaṟṟa taṉmuṉippum, taṉṉampikkiyum potintirukkakkāṇalām. ‘uṅkaḷāl muṭiyumā?’ eṉṟa talippil avaṉ eḻutiya kavitiyil muṉṉōkkiyattiṉ muṭiccukaḷiyum, aṟi kūvalkaḷiyum kāṇalām:

oru kuvaḷic cāyatti

eṭuttu vīcit





tiric cīliyil

ulakattiyē uruvākkiṉēṉ



oru kiṇṇappākil

kaṭaliṉ puṭiparappi

uruvākkiṉēṉ



cālmaṉ mīṉiṉ

cetilkaḷil

muṉa utaṭukaḷiṉ

kūk kuralkaḷip

paṭittaṟintēṉ.



oru vaṭikuḻāyip

pullāṅ kuḻalākki

uḷḷatti mayakkum

uyarnta kāṉatti

uṅkaḷāl -

eḻuppa muṭiyumā?





ikkaviti māyakōvskiyiṉ elliyaṟṟa āṟṟaliṉ cuya viḷamparam. yārip pārttu inta aṟi kūvali avaṉ viṭukkiṟāṉō, avarkaḷāl māyakōvskiyi neruṅka muṭiyātu eṉpatum itil aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu.

taṉ uḷḷattil kaviti evvāṟu uruvākiṟatu eṉpati māyakōvski kīḻk kaṇṭavāṟu viḷkkukiṟāṉ:

“nāṉ eṉ kikaḷi vīci vārttikaḷ ētumiṉṟi muṇu muṇutta vaṇṇam naṭakkiṟēṉ: eṉ muṇu muṇuppukku iṭiyūṟu varāta vaṇṇam naṭakkiṟēṉ; eṉ naṭiyil vēkam kūṭumpōtu, nāṉ vēkamāka muṇumuṇukkiṟēṉ.”

“ippaṭiyē taṉṉittāṉē cetukkikkoṇṭu kavitikku aṭippaṭiyāṉa cantam pīṟiṭṭuk kiḷampukiṟatu. avvāṟu kiḷampum anta ōciyiliruntu coṟkaḷ vaṭivam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.”

“pīṟiṭṭuk kiḷampum antac canta ōci, eṅkiruntu varukiṟatu eṉṟu yārukkut teriyum? atu eṉakkuḷ tirumpat tirumpat tōṉṟum oli; oru tālāṭṭu; eṉṉuḷ mīṇṭum mīṇṭumtōṉṟum ētō oṉṟukku nāṉ koṭukkum oli vaṭivam.”

“anta olikaḷi muyaṉṟu iyakkutalum, oṉṟic cuṟṟi anta olikaḷi oḻuṅku paṭuttuvatum, avaṟṟiṉ iyalpiyum paṇpiyum kaṇṭaṟitalum tāṉ kavitiyiṉ toṭarnta uḻippākum, cantak kuvippum ākum. cantam eṉakku veḷiyiliruntu tōṉṟukiṟatā, uḷḷiruntu tōṉṟukiṟatā eṉṟu eṉakkut teriyātu. icippeṭṭiyil acivu ēṟpaṭumpōtu, ataṉ narampukaḷil iṉiya ōci kiḷampuvatu pōl, eṉ uḷḷam kuluṅkum pōtu cantam urup peṟukiṟatu.”

“cantam kavitiyiṉ aṭippaṭi āṟṟalākavum uyirppākavum viḷaṅkukiṟatu. cantattippaṟṟip pēcalām. āṉāl kāntattip paṟṟiyum miṉcārattip paṟṟiyum eppaṭi viḷakka muṭiyātō, atip pōl cantattiyum viḷakka muṭiyātu. cantam paṟṟiya uṇarvi ovvoru kaviñaṉum taṉṉuḷ vaḷarttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.”

māskō maṉṟaṅkaḷil avaṉ taṉatu kavitikaḷip paṭittapōtu, iḷiñar paṭṭāḷam uṇarcci vacappaṭṭu, mey maṟantu āravāram ceyvatu vaḻakkam. āṉāl atē camayattil muṉvariciyil amarntirukkum kāval tuṟi atikārikaḷukku avaṉ kavitik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ etik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu puriyātu; avaṉ karuttukkaḷ tīviramāṉaviyā, nakiccuviyāṉaviyā eṉṟu puriyāmal viḻippar. puriyāta ivaṉ kuṟiyīṭṭuk kavitikaḷ paṟṟi avaṉ tāy oru muṟi kēṭṭapōtu. māyakōvski kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu viḷakkukiṟāṉ

“ellārukkum puriyumpaṭi teḷivāka eḻutiviṭṭāl nāṉ māskōvil irukka māṭṭēṉ. cipīriyāvil kaṇkāṇāta iṭattukku nāṭu kaṭattappaṭuvēṉ, kāval tuṟiyiṉ kaḻukuk kaṇkaḷ eppōtum eṉmītu vaṭṭamiṭṭuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. nāṉ eppaṭi veḷippaṭiyākap pāṭa muṭiyum? aṭakkumuṟi oḻika! ...”

māyakōvski ruciyāvil mukkiya nakaraṅkaḷukkellām ceṉṟu mālik kūṭṭaṅkaḷil taṉatu kavitikaḷip paṭippatu vaḻakkam. ittakiya mālik kūṭṭam māskōvil aṭikkaṭi naṭipeṟuvatuṇṭu. akkūṭṭiṅkaḷil māyakōvskiyiṉ cakōtari aṭikkaṭi kalantu koḷvāḷ. oru muṟi avaṉ tāy, tāṉum akkūṭṭattil kalantu koḷḷa viruppam terivitta pōtu avaṉ coṉṉāṉ:

“nāṉ eṉ kavitiyik kūṭṭaṅkaḷil paṭikkumpōtu eṉ etirikaḷ eṉṉi ikaḻntu pēcuvārkaḷ; etirt tākkutal naṭattuvārkaḷ. uṉṉāl atit tāṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyātu. uḷḷam uṭintu varuntuvāy eṉavē nī vara vēṇṭām!”</poem>

uṇarcci vacappaṭum kaviñarkaḷ. kātalip poṟuttavariyil eppōtum kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟavarkaḷ, canta vēṟupāṭukaḷum, cāyal mayil vēṟupāṭukaḷum avarkaḷāl tavirkka muṭiyātavi. piraṉ, ṣelli, ketē, pōtalēr, pāplō neruṭā ellārum kātal maṉṉarkaḷē! māyakōvski maṭṭum ataṟku vitivilakkā?māyakōvskiyiṉ vāḻkkip pātiyil ettaṉiyō pūntōṭṭaṅkaḷ! kuḷir aruvikaḷ! iḷamarakkāṭukaḷ!

māyakōvskiyiṉ ‘mutaṟ kātal’ avaṉatu 22ām vayatil oṭicā nakaril malarntatu. mēriyā ṭeṉicōvā eṉṟa patiṉeṭṭu vayatu aḻakiyi avaṉ cantittuk kātal koṇṭāṉ. melinta uṭalvākum, neṭiyatōṟṟamum, oḷi vīcum aḻakiya kaṇkaḷum vāykkap peṟṟavaḷ mēriyā. avaḷic cantittu, avaḷ paṭatti varintatōṭu kuṟippeḻuttil (Cryptogram) taṉ kātaliyum veḷip paṭuttiṉāṉ.

.. V . . O .





I love you





. . n . . o. . r... ic.





.. n. . J. you are nice





d. . rl.. b. e





dear lovable





a.. r. .l k. . s





adorabile kiss





me please





me please





do you love me?





do you love me?





āṉāl ikkātal oru talikkātalāka muṭintatu. ikkātal tōlviyi avaṉāl tāṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyavilli. ittōlvi, 'kāl cerāy aṇinta mēkam (The cloudin ants) eṉṟa talippil uḷḷam urukkum oru kavitiyāka urup peṟṟatu.

mēriyā!

oru kaviñaṉ

taṉ uḷḷattil

tiṭīreṉṟu kutitteḻunta

kavitic col oṉṟi

maṟappataṟku

eppaṭi añcuvāṉō

atupōl-

uṉ peyari maṟakka

nāṉ añcukiṟēṉ.



mēriyā!

pōril miñciya

taṉatu oṟṟik kāli

oru pōr vīraṉ

eppaṭi nēcippāṉō

atupōla,

uṉ paḷiṅku mēṉiyi

nāṉ nēcikkiṟēṉ,

āṉāl ...

nī eṉṉi virumpavilli.





nī yeṉṉi

otukkiyatu uṇmiyeṉṟāl

oru nāy

rayil cakkarattil cikki

aṟipaṭṭut toṅkum

taṉatu kālip

paritāpamāka iḻuttuk koṇṭu

nakarvatu pōl

nāṉum

taṉiyāka

kaṇṇīril naṉinta

eṉ itayattic

cumantu koṇṭu

nakarntu celvatum uṇmi!





[kāl cerāy aṇinta mēkam]

ivaṉ oru peṇṇi muḻu mūccōṭu kātalikkat toṭaṅkiṉāl uṭaṉē avaḷukkut tirumaṇam vēṟoru celvac cīmāṉōṭu muṭintuviṭum. itu ivaṉ rāci, mēriyāvukku aṭuttāṟpōl elcā eṉṉum peṇṇik kātalittāṉ, māyakōvski, avaṉ lūyi arakāṉ eṉṟa celvaccīmāṉi maṇantu koṇṭāḷ. piṉṉar avaḷuṭiya tamakki lilli eṉpavaḷik kātalittāṉ. avaḷum ācippirik eṉṟa cīmāṉi maṇantu koṇṭāḷ. eṉavē tirumaṇamāṉa lillipirikkiyē avaṉ toṭarntu kātalittāṉ. avarkaḷ kātal vivakārattil cikkal ēṟpaṭṭa pōtellām taṟkoli ceytu koḷvatākap pala muṟi miraṭṭiyirukkiṟāṉ māyakōvski. antaccamayaṅkaḷil accikkalkaḷit tīrttuc camaracam ceyya avarkaḷ iraṇṭu pērukkum tūtuvaṉāka iruntu utaviyirukkiṟāṉ lilliyiṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa cīmāṉ pirik. ulakak kātalvaralāṟṟil kuṟippiṭattakka poṟumicāli intappirik cīmāṉ. ivarkaḷ kātal vivakāram ippaṭi iḻupaṟiyākap pattāṇṭuk kālam nīṭittatu. mikka ārvattōṭu kātalitta lilli, pirik cīmāṉit tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷap pōvatākak kūṟiyatum ivaṉ atirntu pōṉāṉ. ēmāṟṟam ivaṉ kuralvaḷiyi nerittu mūccut tiṇaṟumpaṭi ceytatu. appōtu avaṉ uḷḷattil pīṟiṭṭeḻunta kaviti, kurutiyil kuḷittu vantatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.

nī eṅku

ōṭi oḷintālum

muṭamāṉa eṉa kātaṟcumi

uṉṉik kaṭṭāyam

aḻuttik koṇṭirukkum.





ēmāṟṟattiṉ koṭumiyik

kaṭici muṟiyāka

nāṉ aḻutu tīrkkiṟēṉ



uḻittuc calitta erutu

taṇṇīril paṭintu

taṉ taḷarcciyi

nīkkik koḷḷum,

eṉakkō-

uṉ kātal kaṭalit tavira

muḻakuvataṟku

vēṟu iṭamilli.



āṉāl -

vaḻiyum kaṇṇīr

eṉ pātiyi maṟikkiṟatu.

kaḷitta kaḷiṟu

kaḷipputtīra virumpiṉāl

cūriyac cūṭēṟiya maṇalil

cukaṅkāṇap puraḷum.



eṉakku

itamāṉa

cukaccūṭutarum cūriyaṉ

uṉṉiyaṉṟi vēṟilli.



āṉāl-

ippōtu

uṉkiyil viḷiyāṭum

kātalaṉa yāreṉṟu

eṉṉāl

ūkikka muṭiyavilli.





(aṉpu lillikkup patil kaṭitam)

namakku māyakōvaskiyiṉ kātal vivakāram avvaḷavu mukkiyamilli, ikkātal tōlviyiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa tākkam avaṉ ilakkiyattip peritum pātittatu. māyakōvaskiyiṉ kātal tōlvi peṟṟeṭutta kāṟcerāy aṇinta mēkam(Cloud in Pants) itu (it) , aṉpu lillikkup patil kaṭitam eṉṟa kavitikaḷ mūṉṟum uṇarcci mikka kātaṟ kavitikaḷ. ivaṟṟukku oppāka oru cilavaṟṟiyē ulaka ilakkiyattil tēṭippiṭikka muṭiyum. kātal tōlvi māya kōvskiyi āyiram yāṉi palattōṭu kiḷarnteḻac ceytatu. ēḻikaḷiṉ kātalik kūṭa īvu irakkamillāmal taṭṭippaṟikkum mutalāḷittuva camutāyap piṉṉaṇiyit taṉ tīkkaṇkaḷāl uṟuttup pārttāṉ. atinōkki.-





nāṉ-

eṉṉip poṟuttavari





mikamikac ciṟiyavaṉ

eṉpati uṇarkiṟēṉ.



āṉāl-

yārum etirkka muṭiyātapaṭi

eṉṉuḷ oruvaṉ

uruvākik koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ.

eṉṉi viṭa

oru piccikkāraṉ

vacatiyāṉavaṉ

eṉpatic cuṭṭikkāṭṭik

kēli ceykiṟāy;

āṉāl-oṉṟi maṭṭum uṇarttu koḷ!

vecuviyas erimali

kumuṟi eḻunta pōtu

celvam koḻutta

pāmpi[1] nakaram

urut teriyāmal

aḻintatu pōl

nīyum aḻiyap pōkiṟāy!





[kāl cerāy aṇinta mēkam]

eṉṟu cāpam koṭuttāṉ. kātaṟ pōṭṭiyil mutalāḷittuvattukkuppaṭik kalaṉkaḷāka iruntu tuṇipurinta matam kali, ilakkiyam, camutāya amippu yāvaṟṟiyum taṉ eḻuttuc cūṟāvaḷiyāl cuḻaṉṟaṭittāṉ. kātaṟ pōṭṭiyil maṉitaṉukku maṉitaṉ camaniliyiliruntu pōṭṭiyiṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu avaṉuṭiya kātaṟ koḷki.

māyakōvskiyiṉ kaṭici kātali ‘verōṉikā pōlaṉs kayā’ eṉṟa māskō naṭiki. avaḷum tirumaṇamāṉavaḷ. avaḷuṭiya kaṇavaṉ mikeylyāṉ ṣiṉ oru naṭikar; nāṭaka iyakkunar, māyakōvski verōṉikā kaḷḷak kātal ōrāṇṭukku mēl toṭarntatu. tiṭīreṉṟu orunāḷ māya kōvski verōṉikāvi avaḷ kaṇavaṉiṭamiruntu maṇa vilakkup peṟṟuk koṇṭu taṉṉōṭu niliyākat taṅki viṭumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttiṉāṉ. appōtu eḻunta karuttu vēṟupāṭṭil uḷḷam uṭinta māyakōvski tuppākkiyit taṉ itayattukku nērāka vittuc cuṭṭuk koṇṭu iṟantāṉ. aṉṟu 14- 4- 1930.



māyakōvski iṟantu eṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu, avaṉōṭu koṇṭirunta toṭarpi oru kaṭṭuriyāka eḻutittarumpaṭi verōṉikā pōlaṉskayāvi 'māyakōvski aracu aruṅkāṭciyakam (Mayakovsky State Museum) kēṭṭukkoṇṭatu. akkaṭṭuri nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭāmal iruntu 1988-il cōviyat ilakkiyam (Soviet Literature) itaḻil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. atil māyakōvskiyiṭam tāṉ koṇṭirunta toṭarpi virivāka eḻutiyiruppatōṭu, avaṉ cāvukkuk kāraṇamāṉa aṭippaṭik kāraṇaṅkaḷiyum avaḷ viḷakkiyirukkiṟāḷ:

“viḷāṭimir viḷāṭimirōvic[2] ruciyap potuvuṭimik kaṭciyiṉpāl aḷavukaṭanta paṟṟum mariyātiyum vittiruntār; cōviyat aracāṅkattiṉ ciṟiya tavaṟukaḷi naṇparkaḷiṭam cuṭṭikkāṭṭi varuntuvārē tavira, cōviyat aracāṅkattiyō potuvuṭimik kaṭciyiyō avar veṟuttup pēciyatilli; cōviyattukku etirāka yārum kēli ceytu pēcuvati avar poṟuttuk koḷḷamāṭṭār.”

“nāṉ orumuṟi veḷināṭṭu aḻakuccātaṉam oṉṟi vilikoṭuttu vāṅkiyataṟkāka eṉṉi mikavum kaṭintu koṇṭār. ruciyāvil uṟpattiyākum poruḷkaḷiyē vāṅkip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉpatil avar mikavum kaṇṭippāṉavar.”

“avar mikavum veḷippaṭiyāṉavar. pācāṅku avarukkup piṭikkātu. ruciyak kuṭimakaṉ ovvoruvaṉum taṉakku otukkappaṭṭa paṇiyil uṇmiyākavum nāṇayamākavum naṭantu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉpatil atika akkaṟi kāṭṭiṉār. ataṟku māṟāka naṭantu koḷpavar yārāka iruntālum, atē iṭattil kaṇṭikkat tayaṅkamāṭṭār.”

“tamatu taṉippaṭṭa vāḻkkiyip paṟṟi avar atikam kavalippaṭṭatilli. araciyal mukkiyattuvam vāynta akila ulaka nikaḻccikaḷiliruntu, cōviyat aracāṅkattil iṭam peṟṟa ciṟiya nikaḻccikaḷ vari avari atikam pātittaṉa.”

“viḷāṭimir viḷāṭimirōvicciṉ cōka muṭivukku eṅkaḷ kātal vivakāramum karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷum aṭippaṭik kāraṇam alla; vēṟupala kāraṇaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. avaṟṟi eṉṉāl ūkikka muṭiyum.”

“1930-ām āṇṭu ilakkiyattuṟiyip poṟutta variyil avarukku oru tōlviyāka muṭintatu. ‘kuraliṉ ucci’yil (At the Top of One’s voice) eṉṟa avaruṭiya kaviti veṟṟip paṭippu eṉṟālum, yāriṭamiruntu ataṟkup pārāṭṭukkaḷ varavēṇṭumeṉṟu etirpārttārō avarkaḷiṭamiruntu entap pārāṭṭum varavilli. niṟinta etirpārppōṭu eḻutappaṭṭa ‘kuḷiyal aṟi’ (The Bath-House) eṉṟa nāṭakamum tōlviyaṭintatu. māskō ilakkiyavātikaḷum vimarcakarkaḷum anta nāṭakatti alaṭciyap paṭuttiyatōṭu, atip pārāṭṭiyō, etirttō etuvum eḻutavilli. itu viḷāṭimirōvicciṉ uḷḷattip peritum pātittatu.

“akṭōpar puraṭciyiṉ pattāvatu āṇṭuviḻā koṇṭāṭappaṭṭapōtu, puraṭciyiṉ piraccāra iyakkat talivar eṉṟa muṟiyil leṉiṉip paṟṟiyum, cōviyat nāṭṭip paṟṟiyum pārāṭṭik kavitikaḷ eḻutiyiruntālum, cōviyat araciṉ ceyal tiṭṭaṅkaḷum, cātaṉikaḷum viḷāṭimirōvicciṟkup perutta ēmāṟṟattit tantaṉa. avaruṭiya kaṟpaṉi varaṇṭu viṭṭatu eṉṟu colli avaratu cōviyat piraccāra eḻuttukkaḷi māskō ilakkiya vātikaḷ kēliceytaṉar.”

“avaratu ilakkiya vāḻkkiyil irupatāvatu āṇṭu viḻāvi naṇparkaḷ koṇṭāṭiyapōtu, cōviyat aracāṅkam entavitamāṉa ātaravō, pārāṭṭō vaḻaṅkavili.”

“ōyāta uḻippāl avar uṭal nili cīrkeṭṭiruntatu. aṭikkaṭi mūṭṭu valiyālum, ḵpḷu kāyccalālum avatippaṭṭār. ivvāṟu palavita iṉṉalkaḷukkum tuyaraṅkaḷukkum āṭpaṭṭirunta viḷāṭimirōvicciṟku eṉ uṟavu āṟutalāka iruntatu. ali kaṭalil avatippaṭṭa avarukku nāṉ oru turumpākak kiṭittēṉ. nāṉum avarōṭu karuttu vēṟupāṭu koṇṭapōtu, cāvi nōkki avar virintār”

☐

māyakōvski uyirōṭu vāḻnta kālattil leṉiṉō sṭāliṉō avaṉi matittup pārāṭṭavilli. cōviyat aracāṅkamum avaṉip puraṭcik kaviñaṉāka aṅkikarikkavilli. airōppiya ilakkiya vātikaḷum akkālattiya ‘puṣkiṉāka’ avaṉi ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilli. akmaṭōvā, pāsṭarnāk, mēṇṭelsṭām, sveṭṭēvā ākiyōrukku aṭutta niliyilēyē avaṉukku iṭam otukkiṉar. 1985-il potuvuṭimik kaṭciyiṉ vēṇṭukōḷiṉpaṭi sṭāliṉ maṉantiṟantu māya kōlskiyip pārāṭṭi aṟikki viṭṭār. ataṉ piṟakē māskō ilakkiya vātikaḷ māyakōvskiyi oppaṟṟa puraṭcik kaviñaṉāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar; avaṉatu kavitikaḷum enta vita vimarcaṉamum illāmal makkaḷāl virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. cōviyat aracāṅkam puraṭcik kaviñaṉāka avaṉi aṅkīkarittatum, ivvaḷavu nāḷākap pōṟṟippārāṭṭiya airōppiya ilakkiya vātikaḷ-avaṉ cārntirunta kaṭciyiṉ kāraṇamāka- otukkat talippaṭṭaṉar.

māyakōvski taṉṉic cuṭṭuk koṇṭu iṟantāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyik kēḷvippaṭṭatum, kaviñar pāsṭar nākkiṟku avaṉ eḻutiya kīḻkkaṇṭa varikaḷē niṉivil vantu niṉṟaṉa.

eṉṉik kuṟikkum

‘nāṉ’

eṉakku

mikac ciṟiyatāka

nāṉ karutukiṟēṉ.

poṟuttiruntu pāruṅkaḷ

eṉṉilirunatu

evaṉō oruvaṉ

veṭittuk kiḷampak

kāttirukkiṟāṉ





“māya kōvski palavitap pērāṟṟalkaḷit taṉṉuḷ aṭakkiya oru miṉkānta ākkappoṟi (Dynamo); ataṉ miṉṉiṇippil ēṟpaṭṭa kōḷāṟu (Short circuit) atip pocukkiviṭṭatu” eṉṟu pāsṭar nāk kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.

‘viḷāṭimir māyakōvski-oru cōkanāṭakam’ (Mayakovsky a tragedy) eṉpatu māyakōvski eḻutiya oru nāṭakam . atil taṉṉic ciluviyil maritta ēcunātarukku oppiṭṭuk koḷkiṟāṉ.

“oruvaṉ piṟarukkākat tuṉpaṅkaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷḷalām. piṟari iraṭcikka muṭiyāta niliyil taṉatu uyirittiyākam ceytu koḷvatu atikappaṭca ceyal; mēlum poruḷaṟṟatu; tēviyumillātatu;”

eṉṟu ōriṭattil māyakōvski kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. ik kūṟṟu avaṉukkum poruntum.”





↑ rōmap pēraraciṉ pukaḻ peṟṟa nakaram. ki. mu. 79- il vecuviyas erimali kumuṟi eḻuntu innakari aḻittatu, ki. pi. 1755-il itaṉ aḻivukaḷ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.

↑ verōṉikā māyakōvskiyi viḷāṭimir viṉāṭimirōvic eṉṟu tāṉ kuṟippiṭuvatu vaḻakkam.





* * *





itayattil īṭṭiyip pōl

pāyak kūṭiya kavitiyi

iṉṉum nāṉ eḻutavilli.





lārkā

(1898–1936)





nāṉ-

koli ceyyappaṭṭati

uṇarntēṉ



avarkaḷ. . .

uṇavu viṭutikaḷilum

iṭukāṭukaḷilum

tēvālayaṅkaḷilum

eṉṉit tēṭiṉar.



eṉ-

taṅkap paṟkaḷik

kavarntu celvataṟkāka

mūṉṟu elumpuk kūṭukaḷip

puti kuḻikaḷiliruntu

tōṇṭip pārttaṉar.



avarkaḷāl-

eṉṉik kaṇṭupiṭikka

mūṭiyavilli.





ḵpeṭerikō kārciyā lārkā, tāṉ iṟappataṟku muṉpākavē, taṉ iṟappip paṟṟip pāṭi vitta pāṭal itu. avaṉ pāṭivittapaṭi, avaṉ iṟappum iyalpāṉatāka illi; koli ceyyappaṭṭāṉ. koli ceyyappaṭṭa piṟaku avaṉ uṭampip paṟṟiya taṭayam etuvum kiṭikkavilli.

speyiṉil uḷnāṭṭuppōr naṭantukoṇṭirunta nērattil, araciyal kāraṇaṅkaḷukkāka ivaṉ kollappaṭṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvar. pāratiyip pōl, pāratitācaṉip pōl, jermāṉiyak kaviñaṉ pireṭ (Bertolt Brecht) ṭippōl ivaṉ tīvira araciyalvāti allaṉ. entak kaṭci muttiriyum ivaṉ mītu viḻavilli. entak koṭik kampattiṉ kīḻum ivaṉ koḷki muḻakkam ceytatu kiṭiyātu. nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷāka speyiṉ tīpakarppattil vērviṭṭuc ceḻitta kali, ilakkiyam, paṇpāṭu ākiyavaṟṟip piratipalikkum kālak kaṇṇāṭiyāka viḷaṅkiya mākaviñaṉ ivaṉ

lārkāviṉ paritāpaccāvu ulaka makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattil periya atircciyi uṇṭākkiyatōṭu, lārkāvukkum aḷavu kaṭanta viḷamparattiyum pukaḻiyum īṭṭik koṭuttatu. amerikka nāṭukaḷilum, iṅkilāntilum ivaṉ kavitikaḷ moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭu, ellā makkaḷālum virumpip paṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.
